<p> The Vancouver Sun - RSS Feedhttps : **27;8844;TOOLONG , 23 Jan 2020 17:52:07 +0000Douglas Todd : Vancouver 's luxury builders should be nervoushttp : **33;8873;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;8908;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver 's high-end builders are watching the way thousands of New York City condos in glistening skinny towers are going unsold . " alt= " Vancouver 's high-end builders are watching the way thousands of New York City condos in glistening skinny towers are going unsold . " data-portal-copyright= " Gerry Kahrmann " **28;8944;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1196372 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;8974;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Manhattan , half the luxury condos built in the past five years remain unsold . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver builders are watching as thousands of New York City condos in glistening &amp;ldquo ; super-skinny&amp;rdquo ; towers , rapidly erected to serve a flood of demand from global millionaires , are &lt;a href= " https : **33;9010;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;largely empty&lt;/a&gt; as that city core&amp;rsquo ; s real-estate bubble is bursting . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The future is looking wobbly for high-end developers in potentially more risky Vancouver , where prices in the past decade have risen three times faster than in the Big Apple . In both places , middle-class young people are being squeezed out of home ownership . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even though there are differences between the affluent housing markets in New York and Vancouver , which are routinely ranked among the &lt;a href= " http : **34;9045;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;world 's sought-after destinations&lt;/a&gt; , the downward trend is similar in the East and West Coast cities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As with New York&amp;rsquo ; s flashy highrises , many luxury condos with spectacular views in Vancouver are going un-bought . Dramatic price drops are also occurring among the posh single-family residences of Metro Vancouver , where prices have plunged by more than one-quarter since the peak in 2017. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Global media outlets have noted that popular Canadian singer Michael Bubl&amp;eacute ; last year had to sell his sprawling West Vancouver mansion for 28 per cent less than its assessed value . It was bought by the only one who showed interest : An increasingly hard-to-find buyer from China . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Prices have plummeted by more than 25 per cent in the past two years across hilly West Vancouver , which is often ranked as the municipality with the highest per capita income in Canada . A similar dip has occurred in the city of Vancouver and many of its suburbs , where assessed &lt;a href= " http : **34;9081;TOOLONG ... went down by more than 11 per cent in 2019 alone . &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;9117;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;9159;TOOLONG ... Todd : A tale of two desirable cities : Barcelona and **30;9195;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;9227;TOOLONG ... Todd : Contrasting two costly housing markets : Toronto and **30;9263;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;9295;TOOLONG ... Todd : Royal couple 's move to B.C. mimics migration trends of **28;9331;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; The city of Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s luxury condo market might not be quite as hard hit as Manhattan&amp;rsquo ; s . But it&amp;rsquo ; s struggling considerably , with roughly one quarter of the city&amp;rsquo ; s most opulent inventory going unsold . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hundreds of condo units in towers rising in Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Oakridge neighbourhood , with price tags in the multi-millions of dollars , don&amp;rsquo ; t have buyers , for instance . Most of these swank tower homes are priced at more than $2,400 a square foot , completely out of reach of local wage earners . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yet that&amp;rsquo ; s a common square-foot cost for thousands of other exorbitant condo units being built in this West Coast city , which is often ranked among the world&amp;rsquo ; s most unaffordable . The gap between median wages and median housing costs in Vancouver is far greater than in New York City , which has the advantage of being a global financial centre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Knight Frank Global Real Estate Consultants , which tracks prices in 15 of the world&amp;rsquo ; s most sought-after cities , projects that New York City&amp;rsquo ; s overall housing prices will fall again in 2020 , by 4.4 per cent . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s housing values are &lt;a href= " https : **33;9361;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;forecast to tumble even more this year , by 10.2 per cent&lt;/a&gt; overall , which Knight Frank says will be the steepest drop in all 15 of the world&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;ldquo ; prime&amp;rdquo ; cities ( which include London , Hong Kong , Los Angeles and Dubai ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Developers globally thought the way to make money was to build luxury , &amp;rdquo ; says a Vancouver-based real estate analyst , &lt;a href= " http : **34;9396;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Steve Saretsky. &lt;/a&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &amp;ldquo ; But the general theme is the luxury market in global cities has really died off . It&amp;rsquo ; s going to be a sluggish market in Vancouver . The luxury bid , particularly out of China , has really slowed down . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tighter local government regulations are a factor . The B.C. government&amp;rsquo ; s 20 per cent foreign-buyers tax and speculation and vacancy tax , which are designed to discourage offshore and domestic investment , have been working . But they&amp;rsquo ; re similar to rising restrictions in most of the world&amp;rsquo ; s top housing markets . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The most important thing curbing elite housing is the global economic slowdown , plus restrictions on the amount of money allowed to flow out of China , whose buyers account for one out of four purchases in Sydney and London . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The power of buyers from China has been even greater in Vancouver , which has a much higher proportion of ethnic Chinese residents . China is home to one-sixth of the world&amp;rsquo ; s new millionaires , a cohort that&amp;rsquo ; s been expanding at the rate of 250 an hour for the past eight years , according to The Economist . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And the &lt;a href= " http : **34;9432;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Hurun Global Housing Index&lt;/a&gt; says relatively small Vancouver remains the seventh most sought-after city in the world for investors from China , many of whom are looking for a haven for their capital . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even though the valuations on Metro&amp;rsquo ; s most pricey single-family homes are clearly declining sharply , one could argue that Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s trendy needle towers are not hit quite as hard as those in New York City . Still , figures indicate a slowdown . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s top-of-the-line developers used to boast they could entirely &amp;ldquo ; pre-sell&amp;rdquo ; towers of condo units ranging from $2 million to $20 million each , before they&amp;rsquo ; re built . Now figures from the Altus Group show a significant portion of the thousands of upmarket condo units being constructed have not found buyers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The market in Metro Vancouver today has kind of switched back to being a local market , &amp;rdquo ; says Saretsky. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The high-end market is coming down . What is moving is the affordable stuff , including the one-bedroom condos in the City of Vancouver . They&amp;rsquo ; re getting multiple offers , and in some cases their prices are actually pushing up , because it&amp;rsquo ; s all anyone can afford , &amp;rdquo ; Saretsky said . Developers , especially in Surrey , Richmond and Langley , are responding again to the domestic market . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; High-end developers might end up stuck with more of their units than they would have liked , he said . &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t know if they&amp;rsquo ; re worried , because they&amp;rsquo ; re well-capitalized . But they could end up in a situation where they finish their building and wonder : Do we cut the prices , or do we rent them out and wait for the market to turn around ? If they start discounting prices quite a bit , that kind of screws everybody who bought into the building initially . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Saretsky can&amp;rsquo ; t say how it&amp;rsquo ; s all going to play out for domestic buyers . &amp;ldquo ; Do the reductions at the high end trickle down ? Or does it all trickle up ? &amp;rdquo ; The key thing he knows is the Metro market is now extremely segmented . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s the case in most of the world&amp;rsquo ; s desirable cities , where there is a crisis of homelessness , as well as unaffordability for the middle class , at the same time the world&amp;rsquo ; s multimillionaires have been , at least until two years ago , driving up values . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Whether the downturns in New York , Vancouver and &lt;a href= " http : **34;9468;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;other prime cities&lt;/a&gt; will stretch on for years is difficult to predict . But the immediate drop in the luxury market offers a glimmer of light to the expanding number of ordinary people whose housing needs are , to say the least , going unmet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;MORE RELATED : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;9504;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Forces slowly dropping home values in Metro Vancouver &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;9540;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Douglas Todd : Five things we learned about migration in 2019&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;9576;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver 's high-end builders are watching the way thousands of New York City condos in glistening skinny towers are going unsold . " alt= " Vancouver 's high-end builders are watching the way thousands of New York City condos in glistening skinny towers are going unsold . " data-portal-copyright= " Gerry Kahrmann " **28;9612;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1196372 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; dtodd@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;9642;TOOLONG &lt;a href= " https : **25;9669;TOOLONG " class= " twitter-follow-button " data-show-count= " false " &gt;Follow @DouglasTodd&lt;/a&gt; **25;9696;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Douglas ToddThu , 23 Jan 2020 17:52:07 **45;9723;TOOLONG ... NewsNationalReal EstateStaff BlogsWorldSense of space maximized at The Hollandhttp : **33;9770;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;9805;TOOLONG ... title= " The Holland is a project from Townline in Surrey . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " The Holland is a project from Townline in Surrey . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " John Sinal " **28;9841;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1170698 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; As condos shrink in size , Metro Vancouver developers are challenged to find ways to give homebuyers the biggest bang for their buck in new projects . That means making sure that no square foot of space is wasted , from medicine cabinets to showers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; An analysis of Statistics Canada data by real estate blogger Better Dwelling found that the median size of a Metro Vancouver condo built between 2016 and 2017 &amp;mdash ; the latest figures available &amp;mdash ; was 769 square feet . That is down 3.5 per cent from those built between 2011 and 2015 , and 16 per cent smaller than the median of condo sizes built between 1971 and 1990. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At its 25-storey concrete highrise project in Surrey , The Holland , Townline is offering 250 one- and two-bedroom homes ranging from 453 to 828 square feet . It takes pride in being able to offer well-thought-through spaces that are much more functional than some larger spaces built in the past . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The nice thing about our homes is we don&amp;rsquo ; t have wasted square footage , &amp;rdquo ; says Chris Colbeck , Townline&amp;rsquo ; s vice-president of sales and marketing . &amp;ldquo ; You just can&amp;rsquo ; t afford to waste space anymore . People are paying a lot for these homes . They don&amp;rsquo ; t want to be paying a lot for things like wasted corridor space . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Years ago , a developer might build a 750-square-foot one-bedroom suite , but it would have a lot of inefficient space that one would never use , Colbeck said . &amp;ldquo ; Nowdays , you get the same utility from a 550 square-foot home because we build a lot more functionality into a smaller space . We pride ourselves on attention to detail . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We focus first on our floor plans and their efficiency and liveability . We don&amp;rsquo ; t let the architecture dictate the floor plan . What is the best floor plan for the market ? It is about the homes that work for the demographic we are after . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He said maximizing space means reviewing seemingly mundane enhancements in spaces &amp;mdash ; for instance , making efficient use of medicine cabinets in bathrooms or building a niche in the shower wall , rather than using precious space for storing toiletries in the bottom drawer of a vanity . Colbeck says Townline thrives in today&amp;rsquo ; s less-harried market because it encourages buyers to use their time to ask questions about how Townline maximizes suite space , even including considerations about how the way a door swings could reduce usable space . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the same time , all the suites in The Holland can accommodate a queen-sized bed , with some of the larger suites allowing for placement of a king , he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The same thoughtfulness is extended to planning of amenities available to all residents , he said . The Holland has more than 13,000 square feet of amenities inside and outside the tower . The design strives to curate social spaces where residents are encouraged to interact with other residents . Inside , there is a social lounge with TVs , foosball and a billiards table , a full chef&amp;rsquo ; s kitchen and a harvest-style table . Outdoors , there is a social gathering bar with barbecue , a fireside lounge , another harvest dining table , a children&amp;rsquo ; s play structure and green lawn space . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even co-work space is considered : there are two meeting rooms with whiteboards , individual work stations , a coffee bar area and printer and copier spaces . Also included are a fitness studio with cardio and strength-training equipment and a designated yoga and stretch area . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Inside the homes , Vancouver-based BYU Design has created a choice of two colours : a warm grey and a sandy tone . Wide-plank laminate flooring is offered throughout each suite and there is air conditioning in every home . All closets have solid wood shelves and chrome rods while each home has contemporary roller blinds for control of natural light and UV protection . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;9871;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;9898;TOOLONG ... title= " The Holland is a project from Townline in Surrey . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " The Holland is a project from Townline in Surrey . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " John Sinal " **28;9934;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1170903 " /&gt; On offer in the kitchen is a Samsung stainless steel appliance package , including a 24-inch refrigerator with bottom freezer , a 24-inch four burner gas cooktop , a slide-out AEG hood fan , a 24-inch wall oven , an integrated dishwasher and a Panasonic built-in microwave with trim kit . The cabinetry is contemporary two-toned upper and lower cabinets with under-cabinet LED lighting , soft close doors and drawers and a solid 1&amp;frac14 ; inch stone countertop and full-height backsplash. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The bathroom offers the same level of detail with mirrored medicine cabinets for additional storage in the ensuite and main bathrooms , a contemporary deep soak tub and shower with a niche for toiletries . There are large-format porcelain tiles on the vanity wall , feature wall and floors . All homes come with a Samsung front-load washer and dryer in a laundry closet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even though The Holland could be considered a &amp;ldquo ; boutique-style highrise , says Colbeck , it comes with services common to larger towers : a state-of-the-art concierge service with electronic cold and hot storage for packages opened with combination codes , a dog-wash area and a fenced dog run . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Holland , which is expected to be ready for occupancy in the spring 2022 , is less than a five-minute walk from Surrey City Centre&amp;rsquo ; s 150 shops and services and SkyTrain and close to the SFU campus and Kwantlen Polytechnic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;The Holland&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project location&lt;/strong&gt; : &amp;nbsp ; 133 Street &amp;amp ; Old Yale Road , Surrey &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project Scope&lt;/strong&gt; : A total of 250 homes , including one- and two-bedroom condominiums and townhomes , in a 25-storey concrete highrise overlooking the green space at Holland Park . More than 13,000 square feet of indoor and outdoor amenities in the development . Close to SkyTrain , Simon Fraser University and Kwantlen Polytechnic , plus shops and services at Central City shopping centre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; Starting at $375,900 for homes ranging from 453 to 828 square feet &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Developer : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; Townline &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt; Architect : &lt;/strong&gt; GBL Architects Inc. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Interior designer : &lt;/strong&gt; BYU Design &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt; Sales centre : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; 13260 Old Yale Road , Surrey &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales phone : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; 604-951-8111 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Centre hours : &lt;/strong&gt; noon to 6 p.m. or by appointment ( closed Fridays ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;9964;TOOLONG : &amp;nbsp ; theholland@townline.ca &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Website : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //Townline.ca " &gt;Townline.ca&lt;/ ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Occupancy : &lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; Spring&amp;nbsp ; 2022 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Michael BernardSat , 04 Jan 2020 13:37:04 **45;10000;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosMusings on Metro housinghttp : **33;10047;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;10082;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " Francis Georgian " **28;10118;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1164721 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before putting pen to paper for this 2020 Metro Vancouver real estate outlook , I reviewed my previous Vancouver Sun year-end columns to see when I was right and when I was wrong . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While many predictions turned out to be correct , my timing was usually off . It generally took more than 12 months for most forecasts to be realized , and others have yet to be realized . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For example , I often predicted car-sharing would become increasingly popular with a corresponding reduction in municipal parking requirements . But while car-sharing has become popular in many municipalities , it is not sufficiently widespread throughout the region or province . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although lower parking standards could improve affordability and reduce traffic congestion and pollution , municipalities still demand too much parking in new developments . If municipalities would convert their minimum parking requirements into maximum parking requirements , we would all be better off . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Over the years , I have repeatedly predicted increased popularity for fee-simple individually owned row houses , since although strata-titled townhouses can offer a carefree lifestyle , condominium living , with its many rules and sometimes authoritative strata councils , is not for everyone . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I was wrong . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Only a few fee-simple row houses have been built and the concept has not become commonplace as it is in Toronto and most international cities , due in part to outdated zoning bylaws and building regulations . Hopefully , changes in 2020 will increase the supply of this much-needed type of housing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For many years , I predicted relocatable modular housing , set up on vacant lots awaiting redevelopment , would become a viable housing solution for the homeless . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 2017 , Vancouver opened its first temporary&amp;nbsp ; modular housing on city-owned land , and thanks to provincial financial support , other municipalities are now creating similar projects . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the coming years , I predict we will see more temporary and permanent modular housing constructed , not just for the homeless , but for a broader range of households seeking affordable homes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In last year&amp;rsquo ; s year-end column , I predicted two factors would dominate our housing discussions in 2019 : increased concern about climate change and increased income disparity , resulting in many more low-income households concerned about rent increases and &amp;lsquo ; renovictions&amp;rsquo ; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As we now know , the provincial government addressed the latter by reducing allowable rent increases from inflation plus two per cent to just 2.5 per cent in 2019 and 2.6 per cent in 2020. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I also wrote that given the hundreds of thousands of empty bedrooms in the region , we could expect innovative home-sharing concepts to match seniors with other seniors , or seniors with students who might help around the house . While home-sharing programs have been set up elsewhere , few are operating in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I also forecast that co-living would become more popular . Co-living should not to be confused with co-housing , which combines the benefits of individual homeownership and cooperative living . Co-living offers a small furnished room for rent within a shared suite in a building that includes an communal amenities , services and activities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Co-living can appeal to individuals , especially young adults , seeking a more affordable housing choice in an urban setting . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But it is not just for singles . &amp;nbsp ; &amp;lsquo ; Kin&amp;rsquo ; offers co-living for urban families with children . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Major American companies , including WeLive , Common and Ollie , are developing this type of housing across the U.S. and in Europe . While some are looking at Vancouver , they have yet to provide this type of housing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last year , I suggested that European-styled retractable balcony glass systems that make balconies more usable and weatherproof would become more popular . While some municipalities now allow them , most do not . However , given their other benefits , including noise reduction and improved energy performance , expect to see retractable glass systems appear on more buildings in the coming years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So , what else can we expect in 2020 ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Just as we have recently been learning about the dangers of vaping , expect an increased focus on the negative impacts of traffic pollution and noise on our health . This will lead to the design of &amp;lsquo ; healthier&amp;rsquo ; buildings and communities . Some municipalities will begin to require health impact assessments for major developments , like the environmental impact assessments that became mandatory for major projects in the past . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When it comes to housing prices , I will offer the same prediction as last year . New home prices will not collapse next year , nor will they surge again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Housing markets are greatly influenced by supply and demand . Last year , I observed that some Metro Vancouver municipal councils could be expected to respond to residents&amp;rsquo ; traffic congestion and neighbourhood character concerns by rejecting projects and implementing a go-slow approach to new development . Sadly , I was right . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If this trend continues , and supply does not keep up with demand , expect increased housing prices for both homebuyers and renters , since another 30,000 people will arrive in our region next year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Three additional factors will increase the cost of housing in 2020. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the end of 2018 , the B.C. government introduced something called the Energy Step Code to help address climate change . It sets out stricter energy design requirements that will ultimately benefit the environment and reduce operating costs . However , these requirements increase construction costs and , in turn , will raise prices for new homes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; New home prices and rents can also be expected to increase due to the plethora of new tax programs that were implemented to deter foreign buyers , second-home buyers and investors . While these taxes , including the so-called school tax , will boost government revenues , do not expect any of them to significantly increase the supply of affordable rental or ownership housing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finally , in the coming year , expect your property insurance costs to rise . Climate change is affecting premiums worldwide , and insurance costs will increase next year , sometimes dramatically . This will be especially true for poorly maintained condominium developments and other properties exposed to high risks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On this sobering thought , I wish you a safe and healthy 2020 , and look forward to joining you again in 12 months to examine which of this year&amp;rsquo ; s forecasts come true . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Happy Holidays . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Michael Geller is a Vancouver-based architect , planner , real estate consultant and developer . He also serves as an adjunct professor at Simon Fraser University . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;10948;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1143842 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bruno Jury isn&amp;rsquo ; t your garden-variety developer . His mission encompasses much more than building homes to put up for sale . He says he wants create happy places where the residents not only spend more time getting to know each other , but also look outwards to making their environment a better place . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And to do that , Jury , a Mexican attracted to Vancouver eight years ago by the city&amp;rsquo ; s goal of becoming the greenest place on the planet , is using his first development &amp;mdash ; the 73-unit Natura , which overlooks Abbotsford&amp;rsquo ; s Horn Creek Park&amp;mdash ; to put his mission in motion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re not selling condos , we are selling a lifestyle that everyone can identify with . I am not really selling you a particular unit , I am selling you a community that you will be happy to live in . Our motto is we build happy homes , and we mean it . I can not promise that you will be happy , but we can provide all the elements that you are looking for to be happy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of Jury&amp;rsquo ; s prime goals is to get Natura homeowners&amp;nbsp ; &amp;ndash ; many of them downsizers &amp;mdash ; to take ownership of the 3.3-acre Horn Creek Park that surrounds the development on three sides . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;10978;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;11005;TOOLONG ... title= " An artist 's rendering of Natura , a project from Naturbana Properties in Abbotsford . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " An artist 's rendering of Natura , a project from Naturbana Properties in Abbotsford . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;11041;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1147624 " /&gt; &amp;ldquo ; These are people who are retired and have free time . Why not organize them and make them feel proud of the park ? I&amp;rsquo ; ve already been in contact with the Fraser Valley Conservancy , an organization that takes care of green spaces in the valley . They want to provide tours for kids to learn about the kinds of plants that grow in the forests and to keep the park clean . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bringing together older and younger people creates collaboration he said . &amp;ldquo ; It is proven that older people really benefit from talking to younger generations and vice versa , so everybody wins . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jury has been consulting with a number of people , including the Fraser Valley Conservancy and local elementary schools , to see how they can help turn Natura into a place where people are happy to live . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Greg Lowe , whose firm rareEarth Project Marketing Ltd. is selling the homes at Natura , said the development has been designed to take full advantage of the park view , but also to encourage interaction among homeowners , more and more of whom are now working alone in their homes . &amp;ldquo ; As you come in the front lobby , you look through an interactive lounge area to see the park , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; The lobby opens on to a lounge with comfortable seating where people can share workspace , as well as meet at a coffee bar . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Outside the indoor lobby/lounge will be a 5,000-square-foot terrace with firepits and sun loungers and a gazebo with eating tables . Similar interactive space has been created upstairs on the fifth floor , where the &amp;ldquo ; sky lounge&amp;rdquo ; will be built . It will have an indoor space with a lounge area and bar and outdoor space with an outdoor kitchen , both of which can be booked for private events . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;11071;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;11098;TOOLONG ... title= " An artist 's rendering of Natura , a project from Naturbana Properties in Abbotsford . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " An artist 's rendering of Natura , a project from Naturbana Properties in Abbotsford . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;11134;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1147622 " /&gt; Jury said the &amp;ldquo ; activated lobby&amp;rdquo ; came from consulting with Charles Montgomery , award-winning author of the best-seller book Happy City : Transforming Our Lives Through Urban Design &lt;em&gt;. &lt;/em&gt; From him , he learned about &amp;ldquo ; what one can do to make people interact and use more of the common space on an everyday basis , not only when they have a party . &amp;rdquo ; The building architects also designed extra-large individual balconies so residents can connect with the forest outside . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jury , who is also a strong advocate of green technology , is proud that the development being built by his partner , Kerkhoff Construction , is also adopting Step Code Level 3 , an accelerated program for upgrading building standards designed to achieve a 50-per-cent increase in efficiency . The building also boasts Heat Recovery Ventilation ( HRV ) systems that reduce energy loss , while providing healthy environments . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Inside , all homes will have nine-foot-high ceilings with the level-five penthouses having 14-foot ceilings in the living and dining rooms . Portico Design Group gives buyers a choice of light and dark interior colour schemes , two-inch faux blinds and laminate flooring with an option to upgrade to carpeting in the bedrooms . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kitchens feature a dining peninsula or island topped with durable quartz stone and porcelain tile backsplashes . Cabinetry is Canadian made with soft-close doors and drawers and Grohe engineered plumbing . Buyers can choose a KitchenAid package with a 30-inch or 36-inch refrigerator with ice dispenser , a stainless steel range with induction cooktop , a Faber stainless steel slide-out fan and a stainless steel dishwasher or an optional package is by Fisher &amp;amp ; Paykel. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;11164;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;11191;TOOLONG ... title= " Natura is a project from Naturbana Properties in Abbotsford . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Natura is a project from Naturbana Properties in Abbotsford . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;11227;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1147630 " /&gt; The ensuite bathroom has quartz countertops and vanities with dual sinks in select homes . It is also equipped with a frameless 10-mm glass shower , while the main bathroom has a shower and tub combination with porcelain tile surround . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Other thoughtful features include digital parcel delivery lockers , a convenient heated storage locker on the same level as the home , community and private electric-vehicle charging stations , a bike room with automatic doors and repair space and a dog- and bike-wash station . Also planned is a community garden with raised planter boxes and a tool shed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kim Schuurman , 54 , and her 58-year-old husband Doug bought a two-bedroom penthouse unit . She said she felt Natura was a possibility as soon as they visited the presentation suite and looked over the floor plans . They are downsizing from a detached home about 10 minutes away in Abbotsford , now that their daughter has left home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My husband has been wanting to downsize for a few years and we have been looking around . It is located in the middle of a beautiful forested area , yet it is in the middle of town . It made me think I would miss my garden less if I was surrounded by all this greenery and forest . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But the other thing they liked about Natura was how the building&amp;rsquo ; s design promoted mingling with other residents . &amp;ldquo ; I am quite a social person and I want to get out and meet people , &amp;rdquo ; said Schuurman , who has recently changed jobs from being a law office administrator to an event planner with a kitchen shop . &amp;ldquo ; With the firepits and the lounge areas , it looked homey and didn&amp;rsquo ; t look like you would be in your little apartment not meeting anybody . There is an opportunity to get out and meet people . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Natura &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project location&lt;/strong&gt; : 3182 Gladwin , Abbotsford &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project scope&lt;/strong&gt; : A total of 73 one- , two- and three-bedroom homes in a five-storey wood-frame building overlooking Horn Creek Park . More than 8,700 square feet of interior and exterior amenity space , including lounge , shared work space as well as park-side and roof-top patio areas . " data-portal-copyright= " Gerry Kahrmann " **28;11375;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 969485 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;11405;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; The City of Vancouver has collected almost $40 million in net revenue since 2016 from property owners who have chosen not to rent out their empty homes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The money , collected through the empty homes tax , has been used to fund several affordable housing initiatives , Vancouver Mayor Kennedy Stewart said Wednesday as the city released its &lt;a href= " https : **33;11441;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;2018 empty homes tax annual report&lt;/a&gt; . It has also led to a decrease in vacant properties . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The main objective of Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s empty homes tax is to influence property owners to put their empty properties on the rental market , and the data shows that is happening , &amp;rdquo ; said Stewart . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For those who choose to keep their properties unoccupied , we appreciate their contributions to the funds that are supporting various , much-needed affordable housing initiatives across the city . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The report shows 22-per-cent fewer vacant properties in Vancouver in 2018 compared to 2017 ( 1,989 compared to 2,538 ) , while the number of properties declared &amp;ldquo ; tenanted&amp;rdquo ; rose by seven per cent . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The majority of the vacant properties for both years were condos . The largest number of vacant properties was recorded in downtown Vancouver , while the West End recorded the highest percentage of unoccupied homes relative to the number of residential properties in the neighbourhood . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The report listed several affordable housing initiatives funded by the empty homes tax revenue , including $17 million for the recently approved Community Housing Incentive Program , which will provide grants to housing providers to make social and co-op housing more affordable . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;11476;TOOLONG : &lt;/strong&gt; ... " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of Alabaster Homes " **28;12294;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1102852 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Buying a &lt;a href= " https : **33;12324;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;first home&lt;/a&gt; &amp;ndash ; in Greater Vancouver , it is likely a condo &amp;ndash ; is one of life&amp;rsquo ; s sweet spots , an event that is often later recalled at family get-togethers and celebrations . But don&amp;rsquo ; t let misunderstood terminology be one of the story lines . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While condo developers and marketers do their best to avoid industry jargon , some words and phrases inevitably become part of the conversation . Here are some terms worth explaining . &lt;/p&gt; **27;12359;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; This term is often used when discussing the energy efficiency of the building , says &lt;a href= " https : //elevatecorp.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Elevate Development Corporation&lt;/a&gt; &amp;rsquo ; s vice-president Tim Clark-Hollis . Although it was originally an acronym for American Society of Heating , Refrigerating and Air-Conditioning Engineers , according to the organization it officially rebranded it as a stand-alone term to reflect the industries&amp;rsquo ; standards , research , education and certification to promote a healthy and sustainable built environment . Clark-Hollis suggests prospective buyers ask if the building meets or exceeds &lt;a href= " https : //www.ashrae.org/ " &gt;ASHRAE&lt;/ ... . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The higher the ASHRAE , the better the energy efficiency for the building and the lower the strata fees in the long-term , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; **26;12388;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; While most highrise developments include the self-explanatory hollow core or solid core swing doors , now and then there are some variations . A pocket door literally slides ( and disappears ) into a pocket hidden inside the wall , says Pam Chilton , owner and principal designer at &lt;a href= " https : //www.zimba.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Zimba Design&lt;/a&gt; who explains it is often used when there is no space for a swing door . A french door features glass inside a solid frame and is often used as an exterior door to a patio but can be used inside , enabling light to flow through into an interior space , says Chilton . A Dutch door &amp;ndash ; a door that comprises two horizontal halves &amp;ndash ; has glass in the top section , she says , pointing out that when both halves are solid it is typically referred to as a stable door . &lt;/p&gt; **29;12416;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; While the base of many bathroom vanities stands on the floor , those that are anchored to the wall are often referred to as &amp;lsquo ; floating&amp;rsquo ; . Chilton says it indicates there are no visible legs or brackets . &amp;ldquo ; This gives the illusion of openness and more space and helps to make a room look larger and less cluttered , &amp;rdquo ; she says . The same term is applied to shelves that have no brackets or visual support . &lt;/p&gt; **29;12447;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Often used interchangeably with &amp;lsquo ; cabinetry , &amp;rsquo ; millwork includes built-in cabinets , drawers and shelves . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Porcelain and ceramic tiles&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kitchens and bathrooms in residential highrises often include porcelain and/or ceramic tiles &amp;ndash ; what&amp;rsquo ; s the difference ? Violeta Ivanescu , operations manager for the &lt;a href= " http : //www.ttmac.com " &gt;Terrazzo Tile &amp; Marble Association of Canada&lt;/a&gt; , explains ceramic tiles are milled clays ( and other inorganic materials ) , formed into a tablet and fired at a very high temperature , a process that changes the raw materials into a ceramic tile . Ceramic tiles can be divided into several subsections depending on the method of forming , water absorption or the type of raw materials used in their production . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Porcelain tiles are impervious ceramic tiles with a water absorption of less than 0.5 per cent , which means they are a type of ceramic tile , says Ivanescu , explaining these tiles require a very specific group of raw materials . Porcelain tiles are the most-dense group of ceramic tiles . They are generally frost proof , making them suitable for exterior use , and can be used in the most difficult floor applications ( with some qualifications ) , says Ivanescu. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Quartz countertop&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although it&amp;rsquo ; s referred to as &amp;lsquo ; quartz&amp;rsquo ; , these engineered stone countertops are not necessarily comprised only of quartz . Depending on the manufacturer , they are made using crushed stone &amp;ndash ; quartz and other stone &amp;ndash ; and bonded with resin . While a natural stone such as marble is porous and stains quite easily , good quality engineered stone is stain- and scratch-resistant and more durable in typical kitchen applications . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;12478;TOOLONG ... title= " At Alabaster Homes ' Shaughnessy Residences in Vancouver a chandelier placed above the table signals the dining room zone . " alt= " At Alabaster Homes ' Shaughnessy Residences in Vancouver a chandelier placed above the table signals the dining room zone . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of Alabaster Homes " **28;12514;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1102866 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Shaker cabinetry&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; This style of cabinetry design traces back to the Shaker community&amp;rsquo ; s focus on simplicity and functionality and comprises a straightforward five-piece construction including the panel and the &amp;lsquo ; rails&amp;rsquo ; that define the four sides . This simple design has stood the test of time and is found in many kitchens and bathrooms . While the five-piece construction remains the same , designers often vary the depth of the four rails , often specifying them to be finer ( or shallower ) , to make them easier to clean . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Waterfall edge&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; This term is most often used to describe the countertop material also being used on the sides of a kitchen island . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Like a waterfall that is cascading and flowing over , a waterfall edge is the continuation and run-over of the countertop material down the sides of the island , &amp;rdquo ; Clark-Hollis says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The waterfall edge on a kitchen island requires purchasing extra countertop material such as quartz and is considered a desirable design feature . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Wood and wood-look flooring&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Flooring is an important part of every home . Excluding carpet ( often seen in bedrooms ) and ceramic or porcelain tiles ( in bathrooms ) the &lt;a href= " https : **33;12544;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;four wood or wood-look flooring options&lt;/a&gt; typically included in design schemes are : hardwood &amp;ndash ; made from solid hardwood ; engineered hardwood &amp;ndash ; made using a hardwood veneer adhered to a decking ; laminate &amp;ndash ; has the appearance of wood and is made by bonding an image of wood to medium density fibreboard ( MDF ) ; and vinyl plank &amp;ndash ; another manufactured wood look-alike with the advantage of being water resistant . &lt;/p&gt; **25;12579;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; While many condo floor plans are open concept with no walls between the kitchen , dining and living areas , homeowners need to designate spaces for different uses &amp;ndash ; these are often referred to as zones . A living room for example , is usually &lt;a href= " https : **33;12606;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;defined with an area rug&lt;/a&gt; in staged show suites , while a chandelier placed above a table signals the dining room zone . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;12641;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;12683;TOOLONG ... 101 for first-time **27;12718;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;12747;TOOLONG ... condo furnishings ? PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;13024;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1096911 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Big changes are coming to the Austin Heights neighbourhood of Coquitlam . In recent weeks , one of Western Canada&amp;rsquo ; s oldest Safeway stores was reopened on Austin Avenue after a rebuild by Beedie Living . On either side of the new 65,000-square-foot grocery store , the same developer has just broken ground on the first of two 25-storey residential towers that will be part of a major revitalization of the area . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The new development is appropriately named The Heights on Austin . But buyers of the homes won&amp;rsquo ; t necessarily have to choose a plan at the top of one of these buildings to enjoy spectacular views , according to Beedie&amp;rsquo ; s director of marketing and strategy , Sunny Hahm. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Our views are one of our biggest selling points and they compare favourably to any development that&amp;rsquo ; s been launched recently in Burnaby or Coquitlam , &amp;rdquo ; Hahm said . &amp;ldquo ; Even when you&amp;rsquo ; re only on the third level , you&amp;rsquo ; ll already have incredible southward views of Surrey , the Port Mann Bridge and the Fraser River . There&amp;rsquo ; s Burquitlam , Lougheed Town Centre and Braid stations , serving three different SkyTrain lines that connect you to all of Metro Vancouver . You&amp;rsquo ; re away from the craziness and the hustle and bustle , but still well connected to transit options if you&amp;rsquo ; re a commuter . In addition , there are 750 acres of green space within four kilometres of the site , including the prestigious Vancouver Golf Club . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Just behind our site , Ridgeway Avenue has been designated by the City of Coquitlam to be a new pedestrian area with an incredible new streetscape , &amp;rdquo ; Hahm said . &amp;ldquo ; It will be a beautiful promenade with cafes , restaurants and public art installations &amp;ndash ; a fully walkable neighbourhood right on your doorstep . Austin Heights is not just another highrise development . We&amp;rsquo ; re building a new town centre for the City of Coquitlam and the local business community . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When it comes to the issue of affordability , we&amp;rsquo ; re seeing purchasers shift from west to east and there&amp;rsquo ; s a level of expectation that comes with that , &amp;rdquo ; Hahm added . &amp;ldquo ; Somebody who&amp;rsquo ; s been living for a number of years in somewhere like Yaletown or Coal Harbour will have certain expectations when it comes to the appliances in their homes and the quality of living they&amp;rsquo ; re looking for . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;13147;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;13174;TOOLONG ... title= " The Heights on Austin is a project from Beedie in Coquitlam . There are white upper Shaker cabinets with wood-grain lower cabinets , soft-close cabinet hardware with polished chrome pulls , LED under-cabinet lighting and quartz countertops and backsplashes. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bathrooms will have custom mirrors and medicine cabinets , matte porcelain floor tiles , quartz countertops and undermount sinks . There are porcelain beveled subway tiles with niches , as well as polished chrome Grohe shower systems and adjustable shower wands in all ensuites . Main bathrooms feature luxurious soaker tubs. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;13240;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;13267;TOOLONG ... title= " The Heights on Austin is a project from Beedie in Coquitlam . Shop for an area rug firsthttp : **35;13408;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;13445;TOOLONG ... title= " The area rug defines the dining space in this open concept layout by Lori Steeves of Simply Home Decorating . " alt= " The area rug defines the dining space in this open concept layout by Lori Steeves of Simply Home Decorating . " data-portal-copyright= " Tracey Ayton Photography " **28;13481;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1099802 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Time to decorate your new condo ! If you&amp;rsquo ; re starting with a blank slate and working with the existing hard-surface &lt;a href= " https : **33;13511;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;flooring&lt;/a&gt; like engineered hardwood or laminate , put the area rugs at the top of the design list , says Lori Steeves , founder and creative director of &lt;a href= " https : **27;13546;TOOLONG " &gt;S ... Home Decorating&lt;/a&gt; , a boutique design studio that specializes in interior decorating . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Steeves has a from-the-ground-up approach and advises selecting the area rug first and basing other design choices on that decision . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There are more colour choices in &lt;a href= " https : **33;13575;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;sofas&lt;/a&gt; and drapes than there are in area rugs&amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; Start with the limiting piece first because if you do a whole scheme and then you&amp;rsquo ; re looking for a carpet to &amp;lsquo ; match&amp;rsquo ; you&amp;rsquo ; re going to have trouble because there just aren&amp;rsquo ; t as many options of carpets in the dimensions you may need . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She advises using area rugs to anchor a zone in an open-concept space . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;13610;TOOLONG ... title= " Placing at least the front legs of furniture on an area rug advises Lori Steeves of Simply Home Decorating . " alt= " Placing at least the front legs of furniture on an area rug advises Lori Steeves of Simply Home Decorating . " data-portal-copyright= " Tracey Ayton Photography " **28;13646;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1099840 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Having an area rug under the living room furniture helps to visually separate the space . Often in a living room you want a cosy feel and some sound absorption so layer a big area carpet underneath the furniture , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To determine the size area rug you need , Steeves has a &amp;ldquo ; levels of luxury&amp;rdquo ; process . The level most people have in their homes is to plan for all the front legs of furniture &amp;ndash ; such as the sofa and occasional chairs &amp;ndash ; to be on the carpet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The ultimate luxury layer is when all the furniture is fully sitting on top of carpet , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While deciding on an area rug&amp;rsquo ; s pattern and colour is challenging enough , budget is also often a factor , says Steeves . For a living room , it usually needs to be a nine-by-12 feet and often larger . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The nine-by-12 is typically the largest standard size available &amp;mdash ; sometimes one can find a 10-by-14 feet but that&amp;rsquo ; s more rare and the price skyrockets above a nine-by-12 , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In bedrooms with hard surface floors , area rugs add texture and colour and the all important cosy factor , especially during the cooler months . Referring back to her &amp;ldquo ; levels of luxury&amp;rdquo ; model , Steeves says while one could add an area rug at the foot of the bed , the ideal is to have a large enough carpet under the bed and extending three or four feet past the end of the bed and then up the sides to within about 10 inches of the nightstand. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She suggests a nine-by-12 feet area rug can &amp;ldquo ; just&amp;rdquo ; work for a king size bed and an eight-by-10 feet for a queen , although it&amp;rsquo ; s a little scant . Although the result is a lot of the area rug is hidden by the bed it makes up by being a major contributor to the overall ambience of the space . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Runners &amp;ndash ; area rugs that are long and narrow &amp;ndash ; are often used in places like a home&amp;rsquo ; s front entrance and in a galley kitchen or between an island and perimeter cabinets in a larger &lt;a href= " https : **33;13676;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;kitchen&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; These runners are subject to a lot of foot traffic , &amp;rdquo ; notes Steeves , adding it is essential to have a good non-slip gripper to ensure they don&amp;rsquo ; t move or present a tripping hazard . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before you start shopping for an area rug Steeves has these tips : &lt;/p&gt; **28;13711;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; You need to measure the space and establish the size area rug you need . Take into account traffic patterns so you don&amp;rsquo ; t have a corner of a carpet in a traffic area &amp;ndash ; often a corner can curl up a little and become a tripping hazard . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Colour and pattern&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Decide on a colour or pattern that fits your design style . There are options for those who prefer something very plain or an organic look with florals or vines or a geometric pattern . They all lend themselves to different styles and should be considered when you&amp;rsquo ; re deciding what will work best in your space . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Try before you buy&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you are buying an area rug for a room where there is existing furnishing and d&amp;eacute ; cor , purchase a few and try them in the space before making the final decision . The direction of the pile ( the density and height of the fibres ) affects the way the colour looks , and it may look different in the store . At home , turn it 180 degrees so you can see if the pile creates a different colour &amp;ndash ; usually there is a light and dark side &amp;ndash ; and decide if it suits , and which way you prefer it be placed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Daring design&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Area rugs are a great way to bring colour into a home and a saturated or bright colour is not as &amp;lsquo ; shocking&amp;rsquo ; as for example , a red sofa or red accent wall . It&amp;rsquo ; s an opportunity to be a bit more daring because a lot of the surface area of the rug will be covered with furniture so it breaks up the colour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;13741;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;13783;TOOLONG ... diligence is crucial when buying a **26;13818;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;13846;TOOLONG ... home inspection may be the best investment you ever **25;13881;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;13908;TOOLONG ... the right sofa : 8 trends worth **32;13943;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;13977;TOOLONG ... 101 for first-time **27;14012;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Kathleen FreimondMon , 23 Sep 2019 13:30:43 **45;14041;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; **31;14088;TOOLONG 101 for first-time buyershttp : **34;14121;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;14157;TOOLONG ... title= " The wide-plank hardwood floors laid in a classic herringbone pattern are an attention-grabber in Chelsea , Cressey Development Group 's low-rise residential project at 31st Avenue and Cambie Street in Vancouver . " alt= " The wide-plank hardwood floors laid in a classic herringbone pattern are an attention-grabber in Chelsea , Cressey Development Group 's low-rise residential project at 31st Avenue and Cambie Street in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of Cressey Development Group " **28;14193;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1098460 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; As one of the largest surface areas in any home , flooring must be durable , look good and be at a price point that works for your budget &amp;ndash ; no easy ask . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As one of the first decisions for every selection of materials and &lt;a href= " https : **33;14223;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;colour palettes&lt;/a&gt; compiled by interior designers for pre-sale condos , flooring choices often determine the look and feel of many &lt;a href= " https : **33;14258;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;first-time buyers&lt;/a&gt; &amp;rsquo ; homes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While floors in bedrooms are often carpeted , and porcelain or ceramic tiles rule in the bathrooms , the main living areas are typically hardwood , engineered hardwood , laminate or luxury vinyl plank ( LVP ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although the type of flooring is often part of the standard package offered by the developer , sometimes there is the option to upgrade , so it&amp;rsquo ; s worth knowing the pros and cons of each of the four major flooring options . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Describing it as &amp;ldquo ; a floor for a lifetime , &amp;rdquo ; solid hardwood floors that have been in place for more than 100 years are still being refinished , says Kjell Nymark , vice president of the &lt;a href= " http : //www.bcfca.com/ " &gt;BC Floor Covering Association&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A solid hardwood floor probably provides the best value of any of the flooring options , says Nymark. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It can be refinished multiple times , then , when styles change , the floor can change along with it rather than having to remove it and replace it , &amp;rdquo ; he adds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Engineered hardwood floors are made using a hardwood veneer adhered to a decking , and depending on the wear surface &amp;ndash ; the thickness of the layer of wood veneer &amp;ndash ; can also be refinished a number of times , says Nymark , the BCFCA&amp;rsquo ; s board member tasked with the education portfolio . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Companies have different processes but broadly , the wood veneer is bonded to the manufacturer&amp;rsquo ; s choice of decking which could be plywood , individual boards , slats that run in opposing directions , or medium density fibreboard ( MDF ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The thickness of the wear surface will dictate the price point for the product . The thinner the veneer or wear surface the less expensive the product . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But when you get to thin veneers then sanding options are not available , so the longevity of that floor is not going to be as good as an option with thick veneers , &amp;rdquo ; says Nymark. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While laminate floors have the appearance of wood , they are not a wood product , says Nymark. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Laminate is a wood look-alike and even though the core is typically made of MDF , laminate is not considered wood flooring , &amp;rdquo ; he says , explaining that the surface of laminate is actually a photograph bonded to the MDF . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Laminate is often a choice dictated by budget . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;14293;TOOLONG ... title= " While laminate floors have the appearance of wood , they are not a wood product , says Kjell Nymark , vice president of the BC Floor Covering Association . " alt= " While laminate floors have the appearance of wood , they are not a wood product , says Kjell Nymark , vice president of the BC Floor Covering Association . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;14329;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1098466 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; If someone wants wood but then looks at their budget and what it allows , they make a decision to have something that looks like wood as opposed to the cost of actually having real wood , &amp;rdquo ; says Nymark. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But laminate does not have the advantage of the longevity of hardwood or engineered hardwood flooring . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There are some finish manufacturers who claim you can refinish laminate floors , but typically for the price &amp;ndash ; coming in and doing a re-coat on a laminate floor &amp;ndash ; it&amp;rsquo ; s not usually cost-effective , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vinyl is making a &lt;a href= " https : **33;14359;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;comeback&lt;/a&gt; &amp;ndash ; back in the &amp;rsquo ; 70s and &amp;rsquo ; 80s typically there was carpet and vinyl in most homes , recalls Nymark . At that time the vinyl was supplied as a sheet of flooring &amp;ndash ; often with a pattern . Now , luxury vinyl planks ( LVP ) come in a range of lengths and widths and look like wood &amp;ndash ; they&amp;rsquo ; re another wood look-alike with the advantage of being water resistant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Right now , the trend in flooring is very low lustre , low sheen and a lot of LVP products are good at creating that look and they&amp;rsquo ; re quite durable and easy to maintain , &amp;rdquo ; says Nymark. &amp;ldquo ; What it often comes down to is : people are drawn to wood but have to consider budget and convenience . They say , &amp;lsquo ; I love the look of wood , but I don&amp;rsquo ; t know if I can afford it right now&amp;rsquo ; and then they consider other options . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;14394;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;14436;TOOLONG ... home decor **27;14471;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;14500;TOOLONG ... to choose the perfect white **26;14535;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;14563;TOOLONG ... essential tool kit for first-time **31;14598;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Kathleen FreimondFri , 20 Sep 2019 13:00:27 **45;14631;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; **28;14678;TOOLONG Van project offers great location , good varietyhttp : **33;14708;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;14743;TOOLONG ... title= " Ebb and Flow is a project from Woodbridge homes in North Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; Simon BriaultSat , 14 Sep 2019 12:55:57 **45;14995;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosMillennials dominate first-time buyers markethttp : **34;15042;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;15078;TOOLONG ... title= " Millennials prefer to live closer to work and have access to green spaces and parks . " alt= " Millennials prefer to live closer to work and have access to green spaces and parks . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;15114;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1089045 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; With millennials now making up the single largest group of Canadian home buyers , according to Re/Max Canada , sellers are increasingly looking to customize their marketing strategies to cater for the specific needs of this group , particularly in the hot Vancouver market . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A Re/Max trends &lt;a href= " https : **33;15144;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;report&lt;/a&gt; points out that millennials dominate the first-time buyers&amp;rsquo ; market in Vancouver , favouring condos in particular . This generation is tech-savvy and environmentally conscious , both of which play a big role in their decision-making process . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; According to Re/Max , millennial buyers are looking for : &lt;/p&gt; **25;15179;TOOLONG , &lt;a href= " https : **33;15206;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;15241;TOOLONG homes with good insulation , efficient lighting and updated plumbing to save savings and a reduced the home 's footprint. &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Smart , &lt;a href= " https : **33;15273;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;connected&lt;/a&gt; homes with the ability to control the lights , home security upgrades and &lt;a href= " https : **33;15308;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;smart&lt;/a&gt; thermostats. &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Space for entertaining such as open , usable spaces to host friends and family . Open floor plans and functional outdoor spaces -- a view of Vancouver 's waterfront is a bonus . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;The &lt;a href= " https : **33;15343;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;15378;TOOLONG for some buyers is as important as the property itself . Millennials prefer to live closer to work and have access to green spaces and parks . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ashley Smith , president of the &lt;a href= " https : //www.rebgv.org/ " &gt;Real Estate Board of Greater Vancouver&lt;/a&gt; , says affordability is also a critical factor for millennials when it comes to buying a home in the region , adding that a condo may be their only option , rather than the preferred option . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; While many people still dream of owning a detached house , others prioritize an urban setting over the traditional white picket fence . I do think there is a shift for many millennials more towards lifestyle rather than square footage , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For many condo developers in Greater Vancouver millennials are a key demographic and they need to include what those buyers are looking for in their new home , adds Smith . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;15407;TOOLONG ... title= " For many condo developers in Greater Vancouver millennials are a key demographic and they need to include what those buyers are looking for in their new home . " alt= " For many condo developers in Greater Vancouver millennials are a key demographic and they need to include what those buyers are looking for in their new home . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;15443;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1089047 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; At the end of the day price point is still going to be the most important factor , but besides that , features like electric car charging stations give newer strata developments an advantage over older buildings , which may be more affordable , but not include modern features that millennials are looking for , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And with an increasing number of millennials giving serious thought to their environmental footprint and the cost of owning and operating a gas-powered car , electric vehicle charging facilities may become a must-have for their new home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another trend that may emerge as an added attraction for millennials is for strata developments to include a car sharing scheme as an amenity for the owners . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We are hearing a bit more about it and I&amp;rsquo ; m curious to know where it goes as a trend , &amp;rdquo ; says Smith . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s certainly an interesting concept as more and more people become comfortable with the shared economy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Along with the usual gyms and bicycle storage facilities , an increasing number of new condo developments are hoping to attract millennials by adding amenities such as dog wash stations , doggy parks within the grounds &amp;ndash ; a high-value , low cost feature , says Smith &amp;ndash ; and home management technology in the units themselves . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Smith says another trend among millennials &amp;ndash ; albeit a controversial one &amp;ndash ; is the issue of rentals , including short-term rentals such as Airbnb , which are often restricted or banned by the strata itself or the municipality . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think millennials are not necessarily looking at rental as an investor , but they want flexibility . For example , they may need to move for work , or they move in with a new partner , but want to hold on to their condo and rent it out , which in many cases their strata does not allow , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For older buildings in particular , restrictions on rentals may deter millennial &lt;a href= " https : **33;15473;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt; buyers&lt;/a&gt; , adds Smith . Rental flexibility &amp;ndash ; including &lt;a href= " https : **33;15508;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;short-term rental&lt;/a&gt; &amp;ndash ; is key to a buying decision for many millennials and she is watching carefully to see how it plays out in the Greater Vancouver market . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;15543;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;15585;TOOLONG ... key for new-home **27;15620;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;15649;TOOLONG ... the best up-and-coming neighbourhoods in Greater **30;15684;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;15716;TOOLONG ... hidden costs of buying a **25;15751;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Kathleen FreimondFri , 13 Sep 2019 13:00:32 **45;15778;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; **26;15825;TOOLONG EstateInsurance 101 for condo ownershttp : **34;15853;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;15889;TOOLONG ... title= " Condo owners should buy personal liability insurance to cover claims that may arise from unintentional injury to other people or damage to their property either on the strata property or elsewhere . " alt= " Condo owners should buy personal liability insurance to cover claims that may arise from unintentional injury to other people or damage to their property either on the strata property or elsewhere . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;15925;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1086797 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Knowing what insurance to buy can be a challenge for condo owners , particularly first-timers who may be confused by what they need to insure and what the &lt;a href= " http : **34;15955;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt; strata corporation&lt;/a&gt; &amp;rsquo ; s policy covers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A rule of thumb is that the strata corporation is responsible for insuring all &lt;a href= " http : **34;15991;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;common property&lt;/a&gt; while individual unit owners must buy their own insurance for personal property they bring into the unit or keep in a storage locker , such as furniture , appliances , clothing and jewelry . Owners should also buy personal liability insurance to cover claims that may arise from unintentional injury to other people or damage to their property either on the strata property or elsewhere . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s important to know that while the strata corporation&amp;rsquo ; s policy will typically cover original fixtures and fittings such as floor coverings , cabinets and countertops , insuring upgrades such as new countertops , hardwood floors to replace carpet or any internal structural changes is the responsibility of the unit owner . This applies to not only improvements you might make , but also those made by previous owners . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sarah Thompson , vice-president , marketing and real estate , &lt;a href= " http : **34;16027;TOOLONG ... International Insurance Brokers&lt;/a&gt; , says condo owners should review the strata&amp;rsquo ; s insurance policy , which is typically provided in the annual general meeting package or on request from the strata council or management company , to see what the strata is responsible for . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s important to note the strata&amp;rsquo ; s deductibles , as the unit owner may be charged back the deductible if they are found responsible for a loss that originates from their unit , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thompson also advises owners to consult the &lt;a href= " http : **34;16063;TOOLONG ... Property Act&lt;/a&gt; and their &lt;a href= " http : **34;16099;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;strata bylaws&lt;/a&gt; to determine what they are responsible for and when they could be charged back a strata insurance deductible . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She says strata bylaws may include provisions to charge back deductibles to individual owners whose action resulted in a claim under the strata corporation&amp;rsquo ; s policy . A condo owner&amp;rsquo ; s policy will usually cover this type of claim either in full or in part . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thompson recommends all condo owners buy earthquake coverage and points out that less than 50 per cent have earthquake coverage . A simple calculation can show how much an owner could be liable for as their share of a strata&amp;rsquo ; s earthquake deductible &amp;ndash ; and it&amp;rsquo ; s an eye-opener. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For example , if a building&amp;rsquo ; s replacement value is $50-million and the earthquake deductible is 10 per cent ( i.e. $5-million ) and there are 125 units , each owner could be liable for approximately $40,000. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While stratas can purchase an earthquake deductible buy-down policy to reduce the collective deductible to as low as three per cent , if it has not done so owners should purchase their own buy-down policies , says Thompson . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Consulting an &lt;a href= " http : **34;16135;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;insurance broker&lt;/a&gt; is probably the best way to ensure you have adequate insurance to avoid a financial shock if you need to make a claim , or if you are deemed liable for damage or injury originating in your unit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The &lt;a href= " https : //www.choa.bc.ca/ " &gt;Condominium Home Owners Association of BC&lt;/a&gt; offers a checklist of key questions to ask your broker when buying condo insurance : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;ul&gt;&lt;li&gt;What is the limit of coverage provided to me for the improvements in my unit ? &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;If my unit is uninhabitable due to an insured loss , what amount of coverage is provided for the additional living expenses I incur ? &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;If the strata suffers a loss that originates from my unit , what amount of coverage does my policy provide if I am charged back the strata 's deductible ? ( Take a copy of the strata 's insurance coverage with you so your broker can see the strata 's policy deductibles. ) &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;If there is damage in my unit , to original strata property ( floors , ceilings , walls , but not improvements and betterments or personal property ) , and the damage falls below the strata corporation 's deductible , what amount of coverage does my policy provide to repair this damage ? &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;What is my limit of coverage provided for assessments made necessary by the strata 's earthquake deductible ? &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;16171;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;16213;TOOLONG ... your mortgage **29;16248;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;16279;TOOLONG ... survival **26;16314;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;16342;TOOLONG ... is the key to selling your home without a **28;16377;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Kathleen FreimondFri , 06 Sep 2019 13:00:57 **45;16407;TOOLONG ... EstatePilothouse designed with downsizers in mindhttp : **36;16454;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;16492;TOOLONG ... title= " An artist 's rendering of Pilothouse , a project from Polygon Pilothouse Ltd. in Ladner . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " An artist 's rendering of Pilothouse , a project from Polygon Pilothouse Ltd. in Ladner . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;16528;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1078182 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; When the Polygon development group set about designing the homes at its Pilothouse apartment project in the master-planned Hampton Cove community , it had one main buyer group in mind : the downsizer. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And those individuals are reflected in a number of ways in the boutique 87-unit , three-level , riverfront building , now under construction in Ladner. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The project , the only apartment building in what is primarily a townhome community , reflects a keen awareness of the individuals who will likely be relocating from single-family homes &amp;mdash ; and in possession of a good number of belongings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To that end , the homes , most of which have two bedrooms , have ample storage . Many will have laundry rooms with sinks , and some bedrooms will have not only a walk-in closet , but a secondary closet as well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then there are the kitchens , with their enormous islands and ceiling-height cabinets . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We spent a lot of time designing these kitchens , &amp;rdquo ; says Goldie Alam , Polygon&amp;rsquo ; s senior vice-president of marketing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Most of the kitchens have huge , generous islands . We did a bit of research and found that was very important to people , especially when they&amp;rsquo ; re downsizing. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They have so much kitchen stuff , and they want the space they&amp;rsquo ; re used to having . They&amp;rsquo ; re used to entertaining , cooking and hosting friends . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On display at the Pilothouse sales centre is such a kitchen , which includes shelving in the island &amp;mdash ; perfect for storing cookbooks or displaying knick-knacks &amp;mdash ; and a wine fridge , an optional upgrade . The decorative hood fan , which fronts a full-height tile backsplash , is also an upgrade . &amp;ldquo ; If you don&amp;rsquo ; t want that , you have cabinets and a built-in hood fan , &amp;rdquo ; Alam notes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;16558;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;16585;TOOLONG ... title= " A kitchen vignette is on display in the Pilothouse sales centre .
