PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " Ami Sanyal " **28;16621;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1078191 " /&gt; Bruce Kaduhr no doubt typifies the type of buyer who will call Pilothouse home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 2000 , Kaduhr built a 4,850-square-foot home on an acre of property in Surrey he&amp;rsquo ; d lived on for 51 years , and is now poised to move into a ground-level unit at Pilothouse. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He says he never thought he would move &amp;mdash ; that is , until wife Patti showed him the Pilothouse display . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was sold , &amp;rdquo ; says Kaduhr , who is semi retired from his work in the movie industry . &amp;ldquo ; We are world travellers , so love the idea of moving into a luxurious two-bedroom-and-den apartment facing the Fraser River ( where we ) can enjoy the sunset each night from our wrap-around balcony . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some 21 of the Pilothouse homes have been sold , with occupancy expected in mid to late 2020. &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s a good time for people if they&amp;rsquo ; re planning to downsize , &amp;rdquo ; Alam says . &amp;ldquo ; They have time to sell their house and get their ducks in a row to move here . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pilothouse , the second-to-last phase at Hampton Cove &amp;mdash ; a final townhome component is still to come , bringing the total number of residences to about 600 &amp;mdash ; will have plenty of green space at its doorstep , and be fronted by the 16-kilometre Millennium Trail and a marina . Adjacent to the building will be a small commercial space , which may be home to a pub . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Like the other homes at Hampton Cove , Pilothouse will reflect what Polygon calls &amp;ldquo ; iconic seaside architecture&amp;rdquo ; , with touches that include board and batten siding , stone detailing and cedar shingles . Exteriors colours will be soft blues , beiges and greys &amp;mdash ; hues that provide what Alam calls &amp;ldquo ; a seaside-y feel&amp;rdquo ; &amp;mdash ; while white accents and decorative cross bracing on the balconies enhance the charm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The spacious decks will have gas outlets for barbecues or heaters , making them ideal for entertaining . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Inside , buyers have a choice of two colour palettes : Moonlight and Sunlight . Four different sets of plans are on offer for the residences , which will have overheight ceilings and engineered hardwood flooring throughout the main living areas . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;16651;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;16678;TOOLONG ... title= " An artist 's rendering of a living area at Pilothouse showcases an image of the actual outlooks that will be on offer . All bathrooms will have engineered stone counters , flat-panel cabinetry and porcelain tile flooring . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Residents of Pilothouse will find themselves within strolling distance to a golf course , a jaunt from the character shops , services and eateries in Ladner and a short drive from the myriad amenities at Tsawwassen Mills and Tsawwassen Commons . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While at home , however , they&amp;rsquo ; ll no doubt want to make use of the 12,000-square-foot Hampton Club , an amenity centre with an outdoor pool and hot tub , enormous great room , children&amp;rsquo ; s play area , fitness centre , gymnasium and guest suites . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;16744;TOOLONG &lt;img src= " https : **34;16771;TOOLONG ... title= " Residences of Pilothouse will be able to make use of The Hampton Club , a 12,000-square-foot amenity building that has , among other things , an outdoor pool , hot tub and sizable great room . You walk in there ( to the Hampton Club ) and you feel like you are walking into a resort &amp;mdash ; except everyone knows each other . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She says the project is attracting attention from people in South Delta and beyond &amp;mdash ; &amp;ldquo ; there are a lot of people looking to downside in the area&amp;rdquo ; &amp;mdash ; and says there&amp;rsquo ; s become something of an inter-generational aspect to some of the residency at Hampton Cove . &lt;/p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Barbara GunnSat , 31 Aug 2019 12:20:40 **45;16910;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosIncrease living space with an enclosed balconyhttp : **33;16957;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;16992;TOOLONG ... title= " Enclosed balconies create additional space to enjoy year-round without altering an apartment 's floor area . " alt= " Enclosed balconies create additional space to enjoy year-round without altering an apartment 's floor area . " data-portal-copyright= " Handout " **28;17028;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1072334 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; When David Duchovny , star of the locally filmed TV series The X-Files , complained about the amount of rain in Vancouver , he weathered a storm of criticism . &lt;br&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Vancouver is a very nice place if you like 400 inches of rainfall a day , &amp;rdquo ; Duchovny famously said back in 1997 on a popular late-night talk show . &amp;ldquo ; It is kind of like a tropical rainforest without the tropics . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Duchovny eventually moved back to warmer , drier Los Angeles , B.C. residents continue to enjoy our &amp;ldquo ; liquid sunshine , &amp;rdquo ; making the weather just about the only topic besides real estate to dominate discussions . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But amid the talk , and complaints , a Finnish company is using B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s precipitation to foster innovation . Lumon , a 40-year-old company with a strong international and Canadian presence , including an office in Langley , sees rain and snow as an opportunity for a new building advancement in retractable glass panel systems that enclose balconies . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The systems allow weak winter light in while keeping much of the cold wind and rain out . This offers apartment dwellers in some municipalities additional space to furnish and enjoy year-round without altering their total floor space ratio or livable space . That means their taxes and strata fees don&amp;rsquo ; t increase for that space . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While enclosed balconies can&amp;rsquo ; t be used as an extra bedroom , they function like a solarium with added energy savings thanks to thick glazed glass and they also offer a 50-per-cent noise reduction from outdoor sounds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The only problem is that this enclosed balcony design isn&amp;rsquo ; t yet allowed in every municipality . We&amp;rsquo ; re hoping that changes . Today , you&amp;rsquo ; ll find some of these buildings in Langley and Abbotsford. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Making better use of our space leads me to another design-related issue that drastically reduces the number of three-bedroom , family-friendly apartments we all want to encourage in our communities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the municipal policies preventing more three-bedroom units is a requirement that each bedroom have a window to the outside , usually along the exterior walls . Sounds reasonable , for a low-rise apartment that might need an emergency fire escape route , but doesn&amp;rsquo ; t make a lot of sense if you&amp;rsquo ; re on the 25th floor . A fire truck ladder can&amp;rsquo ; t reach that high , and besides , those high-rise windows often won&amp;rsquo ; t open widely for child-safety reasons . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But that simple window policy fundamentally changes the way a building can be constructed , adding about 200 square feet onto the space required and increasing costs . Out of necessity , those larger three-bedroom units are often corner units , or even penthouses , which are usually not affordable for the average family . &lt;br&gt; UDI has long called for the allowance of &amp;ldquo ; borrowed light&amp;rdquo ; or &amp;ldquo ; indirect light&amp;rdquo ; windows . That means a bedroom window could be on an interior wall , capturing light from an adjoining living room , but with frosted glass for privacy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As a region , we must act collectively if we are to improve the availability of three-bedroom apartments for growing families . The regional government&amp;rsquo ; s recently updated Metro Vancouver Housing Book confirms that just five per cent of all new units constructed between 2001 and 2016 had three bedrooms . More than half of all the units built during the same time were bachelor or one-bedroom units&amp;mdash ; at 32,305 units , or 46 per cent of the total . Living in a one-bedroom apartment with children is a challenge . The need is clear . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Anne McMullin is president and CEO of the Urban Development Institute , a non-profit and non-partisan industry association of the residential , commercial and industrial builders . This sector supports more than 220,000 good-paying , family-supporting B.C. jobs through nearly $23 billion in annual economic activity . Through municipal fees and contributions , the industry builds homes , offices , daycares , social housing , parks , public art , museums , schools and community centres throughout B.C.&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;17058;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;17100;TOOLONG ... desperate housing situation calls for desperate **29;17135;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Anne McMullinSat , 24 Aug 2019 13:00:25 **45;17166;TOOLONG ... Homes and DesignWith The Westerly , Fairwinds continues to growhttp : **36;17213;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;17251;TOOLONG ... title= " The Westerly is a project from Seacliff Properties at Fairwinds on Vancouver Island . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " The Westerly is a project from Seacliff Properties at Fairwinds on Vancouver Island . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;17287;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1055417 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In terms of established master-planned communities , Vancouver Island&amp;rsquo ; s Fairwinds is more than a little noteworthy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Created more than 30 years ago , the 750-acre community in Nanoose Bay &amp;mdash ; about a 20-minute drive up island from Nanaimo and its ferry crossing to the mainland &amp;mdash ; is now home to some 1,000 people . In the years ahead , however , that number is expected to grow considerably , given plans to add more than 2,000 residences , including townhomes , condos , single-family homes and waterfront rental units . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Call it a master , master plan . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The newest addition to Fairwinds , home to a 350-berth marina and the award-winning Fairwinds Golf Club &amp;mdash ; it hosted the B.C. Junior Boys Golf Championship earlier this month &amp;mdash ; is The Westerly , an upscale 39-unit oceanfront project from Vancouver-based Seacliff Properties . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The six-storey building , with one- and two-bedroom homes ranging up to 1,800 square feet , will be a standout compared to anything constructed in the earlier phases at Fairwinds , says Julie Jaworski , marketing and communications manager at Fairwinds. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Nothing like this exists at Fairwinds , &amp;rdquo ; she says , noting that The Westerly is expected to draw buyers from the Lower Mainland and well beyond . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re looking at affluent 50-year-plus individuals , white-collar professionals likely moving from Calgary , Vancouver and other metropolitan areas to the island permanently or part-time , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; They&amp;rsquo ; re generally lifestyle-oriented people who enjoy the outdoors and are often looking to slow down , but not necessarily retire . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Fairwinds , the outdoor pursuits those residents can enjoy are myriad , with endless opportunities for hiking , fishing , boating , biking , bird-watching and beachcombing. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The master design leaves plenty of wild and green space , ensuring that this community will never feel crowded , just welcoming , &amp;rdquo ; Jaworski says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But the recreational offerings extend far beyond those related to Fairwinds&amp;rsquo ; natural , oceanside setting . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The community&amp;rsquo ; s 20,000-square-foot Fairness Wellness Club includes an indoor pool , hot tub , sauna , tennis court , weight rooms and multi-purpose room for badminton , pickleball and fitness classes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As well , the soon-to-be completed Fairwinds Landing will be &amp;ldquo ; the new heart of Fairwinds , &amp;rdquo ; Jaworski says , &amp;ldquo ; with plenty of amenities and residential and commercial space in a desirable waterside location adjacent to the marina . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Should residents &amp;mdash ; Jaworksi calls them &amp;ldquo ; Fairwinders&amp;rdquo ; &amp;mdash ; wish to relax with their neighbours in the comfort of their homes , they will be able to do so in units with large covered balconies , many with ocean outlooks. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The contemporary residences will also have large windows with roller shade coverings , nine-foot-high ceilings and gas fireplaces . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Floors in the main living areas will be topped with engineered wood , while bedrooms will have plush carpeting underfoot . Most homes will also have walk-in closets . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kitchens will be fitted with soft-close drawers and doors , under-cabinet lighting , quartz countertops and tile backsplashes. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bathrooms will have undermount sinks , quartz counters and tile flooring and shower and tub surrounds . Master ensuites will be fitted with heated floors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Common spaces at The Westerly include a multi-purpose amenity room and landscaped grounds with walking paths . The building will also include underground parking and a storage area for bicycles . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meantime , Jaworski says , next up at Fairwinds will be a 35-unit townhome project called Timber Ridge , situated on some 11 acres overlooking the Strait of Georgia . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;The Westerly&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project location : &lt;/strong&gt; Fairwinds , Nanoose Bay , B.C. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Developer : &lt;/strong&gt; Seacliff Properties &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project size : &lt;/strong&gt; 39 one- and two-bedroom homes &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Residence size : &lt;/strong&gt; Approx. 1,500 &amp;mdash ; 1,800 square feet &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; $450,000 &amp;mdash ; $1,190,000 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales centre : &lt;/strong&gt; 3455 Fairwinds Drive , Nanoose Bay &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Telephone : &lt;/strong&gt; 250-387-4162 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Website : &lt;/strong&gt; fairwinds.ca &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Barbara GunnSat , 27 Jul 2019 12:44:14 **45;17317;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosThe story behind the government 's largest sale of ' surplus ' land : Did taxpayers get a good deal ? http : **35;17364;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;17401;TOOLONG ... title= " Land being readied for development at Cambie between 57th and 59th Avenue in Vancouver . The land was previously part of the Pearson Dogwood lands and the George Pearson Centre . " alt= " Land being readied for development at Cambie between 57th and 59th Avenue in Vancouver . The land was previously part of the Pearson Dogwood lands and the George Pearson Centre . " data-portal-copyright= " Jason Payne " **28;17437;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1060753 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A tree-lined property with two aging health facilities along the busy Cambie corridor is to be transformed into a new community with 21 multi-storey buildings , including homes for 4,500 people &amp;mdash ; the equivalent of nearly every resident of Osoyoos moving onto this 25-acre site . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; These Pearson-Dogwood lands were bought by developer Onni for $302 million . It was , by far , the largest sale under a provincial program started in 2013 to liquidate &amp;ldquo ; surplus&amp;rdquo ; public land to raise money for government . This single transaction represented nearly one-third of the $1 billion in deals the former Liberal government signed while &lt;a href= " https : **33;17467;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;selling more than 160 properties&lt;/a&gt; , including schools , hospitals and industrial lots . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Experts have raised concerns about the structure of the Pearson-Dogwood deal and question whether taxpayers are better off now that they no longer own this prime property , which is near SkyTrain stops , schools and the Oakridge shopping centre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It is not new for governments to sell land , but Postmedia took an in-depth look at the Pearson-Dogwood deal given what it recently&amp;nbsp ; uncovered about the province&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " https : **33;17502;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;2008 sale of Little Mountain&lt;/a&gt; . The developer who bought that property still owes the province $230 million and 229 social housing units more than 11 years after the sale . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The public sector and public institutions or non-profits should never sell their land . If they want to benefit from the value of their land as an asset , it should be leased , &amp;rdquo ; says Herb Auerbach , a veteran real estate consultant and urban planning academic at Simon Fraser University&amp;rsquo ; s City Program . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You have to appreciate that ( with some land ) , it&amp;rsquo ; s not just a simple real estate play . It&amp;rsquo ; s not just a simple transaction , &amp;rdquo ; says Gordon Harris , a professional urban planner and CEO of SFU Community Trust . &amp;ldquo ; Once you sell it , it&amp;rsquo ; s gone . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Provincial government sells prime land&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Pearson-Dogwood development , known as Cambie Gardens , is zoned for 2,160 market condos , 540 social housing units , commercial spaces , a daycare , parks , a community health centre , a long-term care home and 114 supported apartments to replace the two aging health facilities on the site . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The government sold the Dogwood Lodge Complex Care Home in 2015 and the long-term residential care George Pearson Centre and the surrounding land in 2016 . However , Postmedia has learned that Onni has paid just over half the money it agreed to pay : It still owes $137 million because the provincial Liberals gave the developer a low-cost loan and until 2023 to pay off the remainder of the sale price . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the last four years , as Onni subdivided and rezoned the properties , their assessed value jumped to $842 million , nearly three times what it agreed to pay the province for the land . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And that , perhaps , is not surprising given the potential of this property , which spans from 57th to 59th avenues , and from Cambie all the way to Heather Street . In this neighbourhood , homeowners have sold old bungalows for double their market value to developers who have built blocks of four- and six-storey condos. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And the nearby Oakridge Centre mall area is undergoing a massive transformation as Westbank Corp . and QuadReal add more than a dozen residential towers with record-breaking per-square-foot price tags . About 10 blocks in the other direction , PCI Developments built Marine Gateway , another hub of condos , mixed-use and office towers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;17537;TOOLONG ... title= " Cambie Gardens ' Presentation Centre on West 57th Avenue in Vancouver . " alt= " Cambie Gardens ' Presentation Centre on West 57th Avenue in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " Arlen Redekop " **28;17573;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1060755 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Cambie Gardens , Onni plans to build 2.2 million square feet of market housing , and with the average Vancouver condo selling for &lt;a href= " https : **33;17603;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;$1,345 a &lt;/a&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;17638;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;s&lt;/a&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;17673;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;quare foot&lt;/a&gt; , that&amp;rsquo ; s a conservative estimate of $3 billion in real estate sales . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Leasing public land instead of selling it to developers&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The site&amp;rsquo ; s previous owner &amp;mdash ; the provincial government through the Vancouver Coastal Health Authority &amp;mdash ; could have cashed in on some of this profit had it maintained some control over the land , argues Brad Foster , a former project director of Lower Mainland Facilities Management , which oversees real estate for VCH and three other health boards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He and his colleagues pushed for an independent land trust to hold and develop underused health sector properties &amp;mdash ; Pearson-Dogwood being &amp;ldquo ; the diamond&amp;rdquo ; of this group . The idea was modelled after &lt;a href= " https : //www.ubcproperties.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;UBC Properties Trust&lt;/a&gt; , which leases land to developers and negotiates to collect some of the market-housing profits for the university . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The principal reason for forming a trust is to maintain control and ownership of public land assets in perpetuity , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Well-located public land is rare and valuable , so the intent is to retain this value and leverage strategically . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Foster and his colleagues thought the trust could have been overseen by a board of directors with in-depth knowledge of the real estate industry , but the proposal was not approved by the former provincial government . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It all didn&amp;rsquo ; t go the way I hoped or thought it should . The land trust was modelled after the UBC Properties Trust , which we thought was a very intelligent way to treat public land assets . We developed a very strong business case for the Pearson Lands as sort of a way into that for the health sector , &amp;rdquo ; said Foster , who now works for B.C. Housing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The developers are the ones that are going to get the latent value of that land . &amp;hellip ; We ( the public sector ) could have participated in that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;He argues there were some positive elements leading to the Pearson-Dogwood sale : His group worked with the city to get advance approval in 2013 for the site to eventually hold a master-planned community . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was a huge success in many respects . The only thing that niggles at me is we should have formed that trust and managed that development over time , with the development community , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; We would have been able to phase out those land sales over time and make the optimal buck . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Selling public land with low-cost loans&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Liberals justified the sale of public land by saying it needed the money to balance the budget , but in some recent big-ticket sales , it provided low-rate mortgages to the buyers and therefore the province hasn&amp;rsquo ; t yet collected all the money it claimed to have raised . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The province is still owed $137 million for Pearson-Dogwood , $233 million for Little Mountain , and more than $250 million from the 2016 sale of the Oakridge Transit Centre &amp;mdash ; totalling $620 million in outstanding payments . The Finance Ministry could not tell Postmedia the total amount of money currently owed to the government for all land sales deals . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; B.C. Transit , a Crown corporation , sold its 14-acre transit centre across the street from the Oakridge mall to Intergulf-Modern Green Development Corp. , which was offered a 2.1-per-cent , eight-year mortgage by the province . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Similarly , after Onni beat out about 10 other bidders with the highest offer for Pearson-Dogwood , the province offered an eight-year mortgage with 2.61-per-cent financing , and the final payment of $44.5 million is due in 2023. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The deferred payment structure is not that unusual . In this case , the province/Coastal Health is just taking on the role of a banker ( as well as seller ) to provide the financing for the purchase . By doing so , it is able to arrange a preferential interest rate for the buyer , &amp;rdquo ; UBC accounting professor Kin Lo &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;said in an email after Postmedia asked him to explain how the finances of the deal were structured&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span class= " s2 " &gt;. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; What I can tell you about the rate of 2.61 per cent is that it is a very good borrowing rate . It was likely significantly lower than a rate that Onni could obtain commercially to finance the transaction . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Developers and commercial real estate brokers defend the selling of these larger pieces of land with deferred payments and favourable terms such as low interest rates . They say there are few buyers for these expensive properties that carry years of complicated wrangling for approvals and permits before they can be developed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But others say that when it comes to making the most of public land , there are better options such as long-term leases , and they are critical of low-interest loans offered through the public purse . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; These are all examples of the developers using OPM , or other people&amp;rsquo ; s money . Partial payments are usually accepted by sellers as an incentive to the buyer , &amp;rdquo ; says Auerbach , who also wrote a book about the history of real estate called Placemakers : Emperors , Kings , Entrepreneurs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tom Davidoff , a professor of real estate finance at UBC&amp;rsquo ; s Sauder School of Business , said if long-term financing with variable rates is an option for the purchase of public land , then it can be more challenging for government to fairly compare offers from various bidders . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Generally , I think to avoid taxpayers getting ripped off , it is good practice to standardize terms and sell through as close to an auction as possible , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; When ( government ) officials have more discretion in evaluating bids , I think that invites trouble . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Delays at Cambie Gardens&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; City hall gave permission earlier this year for Onni to start excavating and foundation work for two towers on the southeast corner of the site , but has not yet green-lighted full construction for these buildings or any of the others . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The project appears to be behind schedule , as VCH said in 2017 that &lt;a href= " http : **34;17708;TOOLONG ... would start in 2018&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The city says development permits are under review for the remaining buildings in the first phase of the project ; an application by Onni to develop the northeast corner of the property ( Phase 2 ) will be reviewed by the city in the fall and Onni is expected to deliver the social housing units in this phase no later than 2027 ; the development has five phases in total , so a final completion date will be many years down the road . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Onni began selling presale contracts for two of the Phase 1 towers in mid-2018 just as sales and prices started to soften from what had been a super hot market for several years . Last month , these presales &lt;a href= " https : **33;17744;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;hit a seven-year low across Metro Vancouver&lt;/a&gt; , in part , after the introduction of taxes aimed at curbing foreign ownership , speculation and vacancy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Recently , Onni joined other developers trying to spark sluggish presales with various incentives by quietly sending some potential buyers an offer of up to $40,000 off a Cambie Gardens condo. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Onni , &amp;nbsp ; which the city says has offered community amenities valued at $142 million as part of this deal , &amp;nbsp ; did not reply to emails and phone calls from Postmedia. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Developers generally need to sell a certain percentage of their presale contracts in about nine months to get bank financing for construction , and this needs to be in hand before getting a full building permit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s hard to know exactly where things stand in this process , but Dogwood and Pearson residents &amp;mdash ; who will remain in these aging facilities until replacements are built &amp;mdash ; are keen to know what is going on . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;17779;TOOLONG ... title= " Elaine Wass , left , vice chair of the board , at ASK Friendship , with Miranda Haley , director of development . " alt= " Elaine Wass , left , vice chair of the board , at ASK Friendship , with Miranda Haley , director of development . " data-portal-copyright= " Arlen Redekop " **28;17815;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1183199 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; This state of limbo also exists for operators of various Vancouver Coastal Health-backed programs at the Dogwood and Pearson facilities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The ASK Friendship Centre , which runs a daycare program for seniors , vacated the space it had been renting at Pearson in November 2018 and is now paying double the rent at a new location . It wants to return as soon as construction is complete . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We are hoping for a timeline that is not too far away , &amp;rdquo ; said Elaine Wass , chair of the board . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver Coastal Health hopes to move the 113 residents of Dogwood to their new care home by late 2021 . It hopes to relocate 44 of Pearson&amp;rsquo ; s 114 residents into new supportive homes by the spring of 2022 , and the rest will move based on Onni&amp;rsquo ; s construction schedule for future buildings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The health board will fund the construction of the replacement health facilities , using the profit from the land sale , although the estimated cost is not yet available , it says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Onni will pay for the construction of all 361 social housing units , and provide the city with a piece of the site that can hold another 179 affordable homes one day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Lack of transparency on public land sales&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Information on the land sales is not readily available to the public . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 160 surplus land sales &amp;mdash ; which did not include Little Mountain or the Oakridge site &amp;mdash ; are listed on &lt;a href= " http : **34;17845;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;a government website&lt;/a&gt; that , for the vast majority of the transactions , provides no sale prices , no information about the buyer and brief , sometimes vague , descriptions of the lands . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;17881;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;B.C. ' s auditor general raised concerns&lt;/a&gt; about these land sales last year . And a recent &lt;a href= " https : **33;17916;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vancouver Sun investigation&lt;/a&gt; found several curious transactions , including : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Properties that changed hands twice on the same day , benefiting the buyers and not the government . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The province leasing back land after selling it because they still needed the properties . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; The off-loading of &lt;a href= " https : **33;17951;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;50 school sites&lt;/a&gt; , including in areas where new classrooms are now needed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;bull ; Large land parcels being sold to developers who immediately had them subdivided for dramatic increases in their value , including Pearson-Dogwood. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Onni bought the six-acre Dogwood portion of the site in 2015 for $85 million , more than the assessed value at the time , then had the land subdivided in two . Today the combined value of those lots has increased more than fourfold to $380 million . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Onni bought the 19-acre Pearson portion the following year for $217 million , less than the assessed value , had the land subdivided into four , and today those lots have more than doubled in value to $462 million . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The NDP government appears to have slowed the sale of public lands since its election in 2017 , and said it will consider subdividing and rezoning any properties before selling them in the future , on a case-by-case basis , &amp;ldquo ; in order to maximize revenue . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Assessing market potential of health-care sites&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Derek Holloway , who is retired after working for 28 years as an appraiser at B.C. Assessment , said the Crown corporation often &amp;ldquo ; lags well behind&amp;rdquo ; on the potential market value of a property like Pearson-Dogwood . That&amp;rsquo ; s because until the owner gets rezoning approved by the city , the property remains assessed for low-density health facilities , rather than for the future potential of thousands of upscale condos. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He cited two recent sales of health-care facilities by private companies &amp;mdash ; not by the provincial government &amp;mdash ; that showed the buyer paid far above the assessed value , presumably because of the possibility of developing those sites : A seniors facility in Vancouver had an assessed value of $32 million in 2017 and sold for $115 million ; and a West Vancouver care centre had an assessed value of $14 million in 2018 and sold for $32 million . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But Holloway questions why governments are selling land at all , or at least why they don&amp;rsquo ; t wait to sell until after rezoning of the property has been approved by the municipality . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Why would you not subdivide it , rezone it , service it and then sell off the parcels ? And get all that value uplift ? Because it is just insane that the public purse takes $85 million ( for the Dogwood sale ) , and then shares the value uplift with a private developer . There&amp;rsquo ; s no need for that , &amp;rdquo ; said Holloway . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You don&amp;rsquo ; t need to throw away an asset like that . That&amp;rsquo ; s a trophy trophy trophy&amp;rdquo ; property . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **31;17986;TOOLONG " **38;18019;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **28;18059;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;18089;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;18121;TOOLONG " **36;18152;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.comloriculbert " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;18190;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;18223;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Email &lt;a href= " **28;18252;TOOLONG " **35;18282;TOOLONG &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Joanne Lee-Young , Lori CulbertSat , 27 Jul 2019 01:15:24 **45;18319;TOOLONG ... NewsPoliticsReal EstateKitsilano 's Monument project speaks to neighbourhood 's history of Greek communityhttp : **31;18366;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;18399;TOOLONG ... title= " Monument is a project from Epta Development Corp . in Vancouver . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Monument is a project from Epta Development Corp . in Vancouver . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;18435;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1047326 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Greek community&amp;rsquo ; s long history in Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Kitsilano neighbourhood has a special connection for Epta Development Corporation , the developer behind the 22-unit Monument project on Larch Street . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As Epta principal Chris Tsakumis notes , that history provided the developer an opportunity to build a legacy project in the area . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The Greek community is inextricably linked to the fabric of the neighbourhood ; it makes this endeavour as a family business &amp;mdash ; as a Greek family &amp;mdash ; all the more special and poignant , &amp;rdquo ; Tsakumis says , pointing out that the scarcity of land along the West Broadway core limits the opportunities to build infill projects . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; So when a project like this comes along you tend to jump on it . We&amp;rsquo ; re very eager to execute something remarkable with Monument . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The building is intended to be an architectural statement : a contemporary aesthetic with a meaningful nod to West Coast design . Yamamoto Architecture was tasked with designing a modern building , but using exterior elements that relate to the character of the neighbourhood . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The exterior was a culmination of putting together those West Coast elements that you would traditionally see in a lot of Vancouver architecture : so the concrete , metal cladding , longboard and natural stone are all components of the building exterior , and then softening that up with landscape elements , including the communal rooftop area , which is partially a green roof , &amp;rdquo ; Tsakumis says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The building will be a concrete-hybrid &amp;ndash ; the ground-floor level is concrete and levels two to four are constructed using cross-laminated timber , or CLT. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Locally , CLT is being celebrated as a more sustainable alternative to concrete with all the performance benefits that we&amp;rsquo ; ve become accustomed to with concrete , as it relates to noise , seismic performance and energy rating , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The amenities for Monument&amp;rsquo ; s residents are on the common rooftop where green thumbs can garden . There is also a sitting area where residents will be able to enjoy summer days , and a barbecue and dining area . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The building is designed for discerning buyers who are interested in a lock-and-leave home , says Tsakumis , citing its access to local shops and amenities and future rapid transit , all in a premium location . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Kits is Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s most attractive urban village , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; I believe that we&amp;rsquo ; re going to attract a lot of people who are looking to &amp;lsquo ; right size&amp;rsquo ; locally . And again , given the fact there&amp;rsquo ; s such limited supply of product , new product in particular , in Kits , I think there&amp;rsquo ; s also going to be those people who are currently in older apartment buildings in the neighbourhood who are looking to stay in the area but want the benefits of new homes . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The interior design reflects the focus on buyers who don&amp;rsquo ; t want to compromise on features like high-end appliances just because they are moving to a smaller home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18465;TOOLONG ... title= " Monument is a project from Epta Development Corp . in Vancouver . " alt= " Monument is a project from Epta Development Corp . in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;18501;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1053140 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They want the comforts that they&amp;rsquo ; re accustomed to and that means delivering on an interior package that&amp;rsquo ; s going to be consistent with the lifestyle that they currently have , &amp;rdquo ; Tsakumis adds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the display kitchen at the sales centre at 2094 West 41st Avenue , the high-end Gaggenau appliances &amp;ndash ; including a five-burner gas cooktop , wall oven , speed oven , integrated refrigerator with bottom freezer and dishwasher &amp;ndash ; enhance the design with their clean lines . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The white cabinets have Shaker doors with a very fine rail to support the modern look and feel , while also including a gesture to the character homes in the area . The flat-panel doors in the island are a warm taupe-grey that pick up the grey veining in the marble-look sintered stone backsplash. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18531;TOOLONG ... title= " Monument is a project from Epta Development Corp . in Vancouver . " alt= " Monument is a project from Epta Development Corp . in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;18567;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1053139 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The sintered stone backsplash will perform so much better than a real marble , &amp;rdquo ; says Allisa Karvonen , associate at Insight Design Group . &amp;ldquo ; It doesn&amp;rsquo ; t have to be sealed , will never discolour and does not scratch &amp;ndash ; great if you want the gorgeous look of stone without the headaches and maintenance . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Karvonen also had function top of mind when she dedicated a cupboard in the island as a recycling centre to enable residents to fulfil Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s requirements to separate recyclables like compost , paper and glass . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the ensuite bathroom at the sales centre , marble-look 12-by-24-inch porcelain tiles clad the wall and floor , while a recessed kick gives the impression of a floating double vanity , contributing to the spacious feel in the room which is also enhanced by the shower&amp;rsquo ; s frameless glass enclosure . The pulls on the cabinets were custom designed to add a refined touch to the space , which also has a large linen cupboard . Main bathrooms will include a tub . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Most laundry rooms in Monument will include a side-by-side washer and dryer and a countertop for sorting and folding laundry , Karvonen says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the stone-look material choices and cabinetry will be consistent throughout the units , buyers can change the look of their condo by selecting either a light oak hardwood floor , or a darker , warmer oak hardwood floor , Karvonen says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Each home includes a parking stall and buyers of the larger units can purchase a second parking stall . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This is a very vibrant community , and it&amp;rsquo ; s all right there at your doorstep . If you&amp;rsquo ; re lucky enough to live in the Kits neighbourhood , it&amp;rsquo ; s easy to see why people remain ensconced here . It&amp;rsquo ; s hard to give up this lifestyle , &amp;rdquo ; Tsakumis says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; **29;18597;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project address : &lt;/strong&gt; 2485 Larch St. , Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Developer : &lt;/strong&gt; Epta Development Corporation &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Architect : &lt;/strong&gt; Yamamoto Architecture Inc. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Interior design : &lt;/strong&gt; Insight Design Group &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project size : &lt;/strong&gt; 22 one- , two- and three-bedroom homes &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Unit size : &lt;/strong&gt; 895-1,510 square feet &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Price : &lt;/strong&gt; $1,199,900 &amp;mdash ; $2,599,900 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **26;18628;TOOLONG : &lt;/strong&gt; Fall 2019 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales centre : &lt;/strong&gt; 2094 West 41st Avenue , Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales centre hours : &lt;/strong&gt; noon &amp;ndash ; 5 p.m. , Sat &amp;mdash ; Thurs or by appointment ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Phone : &lt;/strong&gt; 604-336-9350 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Website : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //monumentbyepta.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;18656;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; Kathleen FreimondSat , 20 Jul 2019 12:49:39 **45;18690;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosLandlord 101 : No such thing as Easy Streethttp : **34;18737;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;18773;TOOLONG ... title= " Picking the right tenant is crucial , and first impressions absolutely count . " alt= " Picking the right tenant is crucial , and first impressions absolutely count . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;18809;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1041956 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; So you want to become a landlord : do you know what you&amp;rsquo ; re getting yourself into ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a lot of work : showings , &lt;a href= " https : **33;18839;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;screening tenants&lt;/a&gt; , calling references , doing credit checks , coordinating move-ins , booking elevators ( in condo and apartment buildings ) , dealing with complaints or fines from stratas , &amp;rdquo ; explains &lt;a href= " https : //vancouversutton.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;David Hutchinson&lt;/a&gt; , a veteran &lt;a href= " https : **33;18874;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;realtor&lt;/a&gt; , landlord and property manager . &amp;ldquo ; Then there&amp;rsquo ; s keeping up with paperwork and repairs and maintenance &amp;ndash ; there&amp;rsquo ; s no such thing as &amp;lsquo ; set it and forget it&amp;rsquo ; for being a landlord . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As with many endeavours , the difficulty may lie in the details . There are rules that govern what terms can be put in a lease , how often a landlord can inspect a property , and when rent increases , if any , are allowed . Reading the British Columbia &lt;a href= " http : **34;18909;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Residential Tenancy Act&lt;/a&gt; ( RTA ) will give you a good foundation for these details to ensure that you , as a landlord , are following the law and current policies . Check the &lt;a href= " https : **33;18945;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;government 's website&lt;/a&gt; for policy changes . Read up again before signing a lease . Read up again before extending or changing the terms of a lease , issuing a notice of rent increase or issuing a notice of eviction . If you need to apply for &lt;a href= " https : **33;18980;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;dispute resolution&lt;/a&gt; , read everything again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hutchinson has spent 15 years as a realtor and 25 years as a landlord for his own properties . Five years ago , he began managing properties for others . ( He is not currently taking any new property management clients . ) Over the years , he&amp;rsquo ; s figured out what works best to find the right tenants . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19015;TOOLONG ... title= " When it comes time to prepare a lease , it 's useful to use the official forms available through a government website as a starting point . " alt= " When it comes time to prepare a lease , it 's useful to use the official forms available through a government website as a starting point . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;19051;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1041958 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; It begins with doing your research on what amount of rent to charge for your property . &lt;a href= " https : **27;19081;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Craigslist&lt;/a&gt; is a good starting point to get a sense of what others are asking for similar homes , but the figures there do not necessarily reflect what people are actually getting . It may be that you start with one figure , and then drop it slightly if you&amp;rsquo ; re not getting the right level of interest . Hutchinson says it&amp;rsquo ; s actually also a good idea to rent at slightly below market levels ; you tend to get longer-term tenants who take better care of the homes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He does not tend to do furnished rentals &amp;ndash ; that market can run hot or cold &amp;ndash ; and hasn&amp;rsquo ; t done any &lt;a href= " https : **33;19110;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Airbnb rentals&lt;/a&gt; . He also budgets for up to four months of vacancy per year . That means funds are available for repairs as needed ; doing repairs properly and quickly will keep a home in better shape in the long run , and keep a good tenant around . Budgeting for vacancy also makes it possible to wait a little for the best tenant , instead of just signing a lease with the first person ready to put down a deposit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hutchinson says picking the right tenant is crucial , and first impressions absolutely count . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That first point of contact , whether it&amp;rsquo ; s a phone call or an email , that&amp;rsquo ; s an important way to get a sense of what someone is like , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; If they&amp;rsquo ; re being pushy already &amp;ndash ; trying to get you to give them the place right away even before seeing it , that can mean they&amp;rsquo ; ll be difficult to deal with as tenants . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hutchinson likes to treat property rentals the same way as he would the sale of a home ; he runs showings or open houses in a way that lets him chat with each person , and to answer questions about the property . It&amp;rsquo ; s important to know details about the home and &lt;a href= " https : **33;19145;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;19180;TOOLONG amenities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For applications , he&amp;rsquo ; ll ask for proof of capacity to pay rent , a last known address , and three references . Many landlords like to do a &lt;a href= " https : **33;19209;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;credit check&lt;/a&gt; in order to move ahead with the process of renting out a home . However , if they are considering several applicants , it can get expensive . Conversely , if a tenant is applying for several different places , it can affect his or her credit score negatively to have a credit check run repeatedly on the file . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hutchinson has come up with a solution that is more efficient for everyone . He suggests to a tenant that he or she get their own credit check done , and if that person is the successful applicant , he will reimburse the fee involved . The tenant also then has the current credit check available for any other applications he or she may be pursuing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When it comes time to prepare a lease , it&amp;rsquo ; s useful to use the official forms available through a government website as a starting point . You can put in more terms in an addendum &amp;ndash ; Hutchinson includes making a tenant responsible for paying any fines or penalties levied by a strata for poor behaviour &amp;ndash ; but you have to make sure the addendum terms still work within the confines of the RTA . Pets and cannabis can be points of contention , especially since the rules around growing cannabis at home can affect the ability of a landlord to get home insurance . A landlord should also consider what type of home is being rented ( e.g. , a single-family home or a &lt;a href= " https : **33;19244;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;laneway&lt;/a&gt; versus an apartment ) in order to incorporate &lt;a href= " https : **33;19279;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;terms&lt;/a&gt; around repairs and maintenance , and who ( landlord or tenant ) bears responsibility for what . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If all of this sounds too much for you , but you&amp;rsquo ; re still interested in becoming a landlord , consider the possibility of outsourcing . Some companies will find tenants for you for a fee , while others will manage the property for you . A standard property-management contract costs one-half month&amp;rsquo ; s rent and five per cent per month for a year , although terms can vary based on the level of service . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You don&amp;rsquo ; t have the tenant calling you when there&amp;rsquo ; s a burnt-out light bulb , or neighbours calling about noise &amp;ndash ; it gives you a little distance , &amp;rdquo ; says Hutchinson. &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a business expense that will pay off , because a good property manager will get you a better tenant and probably get you a better rent because they know the market . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hiring a property manager does also involve first impressions , including how quickly they respond to inquiries and how much they know about the industry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;19314;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;19356;TOOLONG ... to screen a **27;19391;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;19420;TOOLONG ... long can tenants have guests ? &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;19455;TOOLONG ... extra income through short-term **28;19490;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Claudia KwanFri , 19 Jul 2019 13:00:28 **45;19520;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingBuying &amp;amp ; SellingCondosReal EstateDue diligence is crucial when buying a condohttp : **35;19567;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;19604;TOOLONG ... title= " Make sure you read all the documents : Obtaining documents prior to a condo purchase is only worthwhile if you read them . If there is anything you do n't understand consult your realtor or lawyer . " alt= " Make sure you read all the documents : Obtaining documents prior to a condo purchase is only worthwhile if you read them . If there is anything you do n't understand consult your realtor or lawyer . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;19640;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1040894 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; For many first-time buyers in Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s high-priced property market , a &lt;a href= " https : **33;19670;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;condo&lt;/a&gt; is their only option , while for some empty nesters rattling around a large family home , it&amp;rsquo ; s a preferred choice . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But regardless of the reason for considering a condo , knowing what you are buying into is as important as the unit itself . Condo living is communal living in every sense of the word . A strata council elected by the owners draws up and amends bylaws and rules that govern how the strata complex is managed and what individual owners can and can not do . In that sense , owners have less freedom to do as they please than would be the case in a single-family home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19705;TOOLONG ... title= " When purchasing a condo , knowing what you are buying into is as important as the unit itself . " alt= " When purchasing a condo , knowing what you are buying into is as important as the unit itself . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;19741;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1040895 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Therefore , it&amp;rsquo ; s important to fully understand the bylaws and rules before committing to a purchase . And while most buyers of resale condos are likely to have a satisfactory &lt;a href= " https : **33;19771;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;home inspection&lt;/a&gt; as a subject to their offer ( the inspection would include a report on the complex as a whole , as well as the individual unit ) , it is also advisable to examine the strata council&amp;rsquo ; s minutes going back at least 12 months to identify potential red flags , such as upcoming high-cost maintenance and repairs , which could include a special levy on owners . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tony Gioventu , executive director of &lt;a href= " https : //www.choa.bc.ca/ " &gt;British Columbia 's Condominium Home Owners ' Association&lt;/a&gt; ( COHA ) , a consumer-based non-profit association that promotes the understanding of &lt;a href= " https : **33;19806;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;strata property living&lt;/a&gt; and the interests of strata property owners , says buyers should request , obtain and read all relevant documents before they remove subjects to their offer to buy a condo. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; A Form B Information Certificate is the first place to start , &amp;rdquo ; he adds . &amp;ldquo ; They can only rely on anything they question if they have it confirmed in writing from a party who is authorized to provide that information . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For example , if an owner discloses that they have two allocated parking spaces and a storage locker , Form B will identify them and will give the buyer recourse if the information is incorrect . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The strata corporation&amp;rsquo ; s insurance certificate is also a good indicator of potential problems and should be examined , says Gioventu. &lt;a href= " https : **33;19841;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Excessively high&lt;/a&gt; deductibles or unusual exclusions of coverage could indicate past issues with the building . Engineering documents and environmental reports may provide similar information . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The property&amp;rsquo ; s most recent depreciation report may reveal red flags as well , but it needs to be seen in context , Gioventu says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The depreciation report&amp;rsquo ; s recommendations need to be viewed in conjunction with the strata council&amp;rsquo ; s financial planning . The contingency fund balances and future major repairs need to be compared , and you need to try to figure out what the owners may have to pay for in the next 10 years and how that is going to happen . Have they been saving and planning or will it need special levies , &amp;rdquo ; he adds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Requests for documents are made to the strata corporation , which has an official obligation to provide the information and buyers typically ask their &lt;a href= " https : **33;19876;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;realtor&lt;/a&gt; to take on that task . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; If a seller or strata corporation fails to or refuses to provide documents , it&amp;rsquo ; s an indication of a serious risk associated with the property . It may be operational , governance , legal or administrative , but whatever it is , the buyer should proceed with caution and consult their lawyer , &amp;rdquo ; Gioventu says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Buyers of condos in buildings that have &lt;a href= " https : **33;19911;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;not yet been constructed&lt;/a&gt; have a seven-day cooling off period after signing up . It&amp;rsquo ; s a good idea to immediately consult a lawyer to review what they will be purchasing and any risks or costs they have exposed themselves to , he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The bottom line , says Gioventu , is buyers should follow two rules : &lt;/h3&gt; &lt;ol&gt;&lt;li&gt;Put all communications in writing . If it 's not in writing it may not be true ; &lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;Read all the documents . Obtaining documents prior to a purchase is only worthwhile if you read them . If there is anything you do n't understand consult your realtor or lawyer . &lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ol&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;19946;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;19988;TOOLONG ... home inspection may be the best investment you ever **25;20023;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;20050;TOOLONG ... best 5 tips for choosing a **28;20085;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;20115;TOOLONG ... key for new-home **27;20150;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;20179;TOOLONG ... hidden costs of buying a **25;20214;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Kathleen FreimondMon , 15 Jul 2019 13:00:07 **45;20241;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosSold ( Bought ) : Updated North Delta home offers plenty of living spacehttp : **35;20288;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;20325;TOOLONG ... title= " This home at 8129 Modesto Drive in North Delta sold for $1,055,000 . ( Bought ) : North Van condo has plenty of **30;21703;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Nicola WaySat , 15 Jun 2019 12:41:29 **45;21735;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosConsumers have a good grasp of strata property definitions , says Gioventuhttp : **32;21782;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;21816;TOOLONG ... title= " In a recent query from a strata owner for a charging station in a condo building , Tony Gioventi explains that there may be a valid argument why a station should be installed , particularly if other alterations have been made , such as installing racks for bikes or kayaks. " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;21852;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1023380 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21882;TOOLONG ... title= " VANCOUVER , BC. , April 3 , 2013 -- Tony Gioventu , executive director for the Condominium Home Owners ' Association in action in Vancouver , BC. , April 3 , 2013 . ( Nick Procaylo/PNG ) PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " NICK PROCAYLO Nick Procaylo " **28;21918;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 808730 " /&gt; There may have been some confusion over the differentiation between common property and limited common property with strata organizations in the past , but not any more , says &lt;a href= " https : **33;21948;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Tony Gioventu&lt;/a&gt; , executive director of the Condominum Home Owners Association of B.C. ( &lt;a href= " https : //www.choa.bc.ca " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;CHOA&lt;/a&gt; ) &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This is not a complicated problem for the industry , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; Consumers understand this well . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Common property comprises all of the portions of a &lt;a href= " http : **34;21983;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;strata&lt;/a&gt; corporation that are not designated as part of a strata lot or identified as limited common property , either on the strata plan or through an amendment correctly approved and filed in the Land Title Registry by the strata corporation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Limited common property is common property that has been designated for the exclusive use of one or more strata lots identified on the strata plan or as amended and filed by the strata corporation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Strata corporations have a reasonable expectation to act fairly for all owners . &lt;/span&gt; Where strata corporations and owners can run into issues is if a &lt;a href= " https : **33;22019;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;strata&lt;/a&gt; refuses reasonable requests . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a recent query from a strata owner for a charging station in a condo building , Gioventi explains that there may be a valid argument why a station should be installed , particularly if other alterations have been made , such as installing racks for bikes or kayaks . And if a strata council refuses , such evidence can be presented to challenge a council decision . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My experience &amp;mdash ; over almost 1,000 newspaper &lt;a href= " https : **33;22054;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;columns&lt;/a&gt; and probably close to 25,000 emails generated from the columns &amp;mdash ; is that there are very few misunderstandings , but many circumstances where owners and tenants manipulate information to gain control over common property or inappropriately use limited common property , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gioventu says strata corporations have a reasonable expectation to act fairly for all owners . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think the best message for the public is if you want to understand how property is allocated , look at the registered strata plan and any property amendments that have been filed by the strata corporation , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; If you want to understand who is responsible to maintain and repair &lt;a href= " http : **34;22089;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;common property&lt;/a&gt; and use of property , then look at the &lt;a href= " https : **33;22125;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;bylaws&lt;/a&gt; of the strata corporation . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gioventu says the old theory that your home is your castle doesn&amp;rsquo ; t apply to strata corporations , and not even to bare-land stratas. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There are no simple rules of thumb that can be applied to strata corporations in B.C. as they are all filed with different uses , zoning , geographic conditions , building designs , climate conditions , property designations , easements and inter connected business uses . As a result , each strata has to be analyzed separately and then the &lt;a href= " http : **34;22160;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;act&lt;/a&gt; and bylaws applied to understand property use , maintenance and repair . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gioventu says that when consumers profess not to know the difference between the property designations , he can almost guarantee that they do indeed know . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But they use ignorance to defend the misuse of property , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;22196;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;22238;TOOLONG ... owners before proceeding with plan to sell **26;22273;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;22301;TOOLONG ... members ' duties at the start of each **25;22336;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;22363;TOOLONG ... Pets and rentals are permitted . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;These transactions were compiled by Nicola Way of &lt;a href= " https : **33;23741;TOOLONG ... class= " s2 " **36;23776;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Realtors -- send your recent sales to &lt;a href= " **29;23814;TOOLONG " &gt;&lt;span class= " s3 " **56;23845;TOOLONG ... class= " s4 " &gt;. &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;23903;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;23945;TOOLONG ... ( Bought ) : Vancouver condo showcases captivating **26;23980;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Nicola WaySat , 01 Jun 2019 12:10:49 **45;24008;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosU-Eight designed to meet the need of UBCO studentshttp : **32;24055;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;24089;TOOLONG ... title= " U-Eight is a project from the Mission Group in Kelowna . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;24125;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1009340 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; New and exciting opportunities and activities are all part of the university experience for students . But sometimes , the hunt for suitable accommodation is one of the more difficult challenges . That task is being made a little easier for students attending the University of British Columbia Okanagan campus with the launch of U-Eight , the Mission Group&amp;rsquo ; s 90-unit condo building in Kelowna&amp;rsquo ; s University District . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The three-storey wood-frame building , which will include a selection of studios , one- , two- , and three-bedroom homes , is the last in Mission Group&amp;rsquo ; s series of condo developments in the University District . The developer&amp;rsquo ; s six previous condo buildings comprise 451 sold units in addition to rental units at Ivy Walk , with 63 homes , and Ivy Hall , with 108. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The University District project was based on a strong demand for housing within walking distance to UBCO , &amp;rdquo ; says Randy Shier , Mission Group&amp;rsquo ; s president . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The UBCO campus has expanded enrolment since its first intake of 3,500 students in September 2005 , recording a total of 9,935 students this year . U-Eight is appealing to parents purchasing a home for their children attending UBCO and to investors who intend to rent the units . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Designed by Meiklejohn Architects , U-Eight will have a modern look with clean lines , says Carrie Dawson , Mission Group&amp;rsquo ; s assistant development manager , while a red accent colour selected to mark the entrance to the building will differentiate it from the other condo developments in University District . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bearing in mind that students are expected to be the future residents of the building , the units are designed to be durable and maximize the living space , while each bedroom has its own bathroom . This combination of space to socialize along with the privacy that comes with an individual bathroom has been a successful layout in Mission Group&amp;rsquo ; s previous University District developments , Dawson says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Each home has a full kitchen and in-suite laundry with a stacked washer and dryer . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The in-suite laundry is a favourite feature of the students and includes a stacked washer and dryer with storage space in the laundry closet , &amp;rdquo ; Dawson says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Buyers can choose from two colour boards , either the light or dark scheme . In both options , the upper kitchen cabinets are white while the lower cabinets&amp;rsquo ; wood-grain look varies according to the choice . A tile backsplash and light-coloured laminate countertops ( with only a subtle difference between the countertops in the two palettes ) add to the light and airy ambience favoured by students , Dawson says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Stainless steel appliances include a dishwasher , microwave , range and refrigerator . Vinyl flooring will be used throughout to create a consistent look . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The bathroom vanities , with a single sink and chrome faucet , will have the same wood-grain laminate finish as the kitchen cabinets and all-important storage space . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the interior paint scheme in the completed units will be neutral , the show suite at 725 Academy Way shows how bedrooms can easily be transformed with the addition of a painted accent wall or fun peel-off wall art . Colourful sofas and chairs in the show suite&amp;rsquo ; s living room illustrate how pops of colour enliven a room and in this case , give it a youthful appeal . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A combination of a surface lot and underground parking provides one parking space for each unit . The building is within walking distance of the UBCO campus and the University District is well connected to public transit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For students flying in at the start and end of semesters and for visits from family , U-Eight is also conveniently close to the Kelowna International Airport , the 11th busiest airport in Canada , serving 1.6 million passengers annually . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The nearby Airport Village shopping centre has a range of stores and services , including anchor tenants Nester&amp;rsquo ; s Market , Tim Hortons and Pharmasave. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; U-Eight is the last chance for homebuyers and investors to take advantage of the high demand for University housing in Kelowna . More than 9,000 students are enrolled at UBCO , but there are currently fewer than 1,700 beds on campus , &amp;rdquo ; says Jessica Sorrell , director of sales and customer experience , Mission Group . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; According to the Mission Group , UBCO&amp;rsquo ; s important role in the region includes a $1.5-billion impact on the Okanagan economy . Recent infrastructure upgrades in the region include the $54-million upgrade of Highway 97 to six lanes just south of UBCO and a $12-million connector road linking the Glenmore community and UBCO that opened this September . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Construction of U-Eight is projected to start this spring , with completion scheduled by the beginning of the fall 2020 semester. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **36;24155;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project address : &lt;/strong&gt; 625 Academy Way , Kelowna &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Developer : &lt;/strong&gt; Mission Group &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Architect : &lt;/strong&gt; Meiklejohn Architects &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Interior designer : &lt;/strong&gt; Mission Group &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project size : &lt;/strong&gt; 90 homes &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Bedrooms : &lt;/strong&gt; Studio , 1- , 2- , 3-bedroom ( only 2- and 3-bedrooms still available ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Unit size : &lt;/strong&gt; 312 &amp;mdash ; 962 square feet &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;24193;TOOLONG : Low $200s to mid $400s &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales centre : &lt;/strong&gt; U-Five , 725 Academy Way , Kelowna &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales centre hours : &lt;/strong&gt; Sat &amp;mdash ; Tues , noon &amp;mdash ; &amp;nbsp ; 5 p.m . They can also serve as decor pieces as well , given the number of gorgeous designs on offer . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Designers advise homeowners to paint their walls white or a similar light colour if they want to make their apartment seem bigger , lighter and brighter , which is appealing when you&amp;rsquo ; re living in a small space with a family . &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;It also provides a great backdrop for hanging &lt;a href= " http : **34;24691;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;plants&lt;/a&gt; and artwork , including pieces made by your kids . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you&amp;rsquo ; re living in an apartment , it&amp;rsquo ; s worth spending more money on a single piece of furniture that you love and will cherish , than going to Ikea and filling up a room , &amp;nbsp ; says &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Joanna Leung , vice-president of Vancouver-based outdoor furniture specialists &lt;a href= " http : //www.ratana.com/ " &gt;Ratana&lt;/a ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Choosing the right piece of furniture that truly fits the space will make a big difference to the feel of your home , says Leung , who lived for many years in a &lt;a href= " http : **34;24727;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;tiny apartment&lt;/a&gt; in Hong Kong with young children . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Less is more , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; I buy a lot of ottomans or poofs because you put a tray on an ottoman and it can be a coffee table . If you need seating , you can sit on it . If you need to pull it outside , you can do that . Thinking outside the box is really key . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;24763;TOOLONG ... title= " Ikea furniture modified to provide storage and seating options in family apartment " alt= " Ikea furniture modified to provide storage and seating options in family apartment " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of Ikea " **28;24799;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1008425 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Leung says ottomans and baskets are also great for stashing kids&amp;rsquo ; toys and crafts , and you can put a throw over the top of them to hide what&amp;rsquo ; s inside . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They look nice , are great for storage and from a visual perspective it&amp;rsquo ; s great to have different textures , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There is a movement towards toys made from more natural materials ( wood , and the like ) and neutral colour choices for &lt;a href= " http : **34;24829;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;kids ' bedrooms&lt;/a&gt; , says interior designer &lt;a href= " http : **28;24865;TOOLONG " &gt;A ... Everything from the massing of Kira&amp;rsquo ; s two buildings joined in the middle by a glass atrium to the tall windows and high ceilings in the homes is designed to bring in as much light as possible , company principal James Howard said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We liked the name because it is short and easy to pronounce by any Canadian , new or old , &amp;rdquo ; Howard said . He said that working with Ciccozzi Architecture , the developer was able to demonstrate its design vision for the two buildings to the city of Coquitlam : &amp;ldquo ; The idea was to have more corners &amp;mdash ; and hence more light &amp;mdash ; out of the total building size . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The glass atrium provides a grander entrance than would two separate buildings , he said , and serves as the home for several common amenities . The ground level features the entrance and a classic lounge leading to a garden patio , the second level has the meditation and yoga room , a meeting room and fitness room , while the third level has a rooftop deck for residents to enjoy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; As the morning sun comes up , the light will come straight down Grover Avenue right into the atrium and out the back , &amp;rdquo ; said Howard . &amp;ldquo ; There are lots of opportunity for light . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Woodbridge , founded by Howard&amp;rsquo ; s father 50 years ago , has lots of experience in multi-family home building , with projects in Surrey , Chilliwack , Coquitlam , Port Moody , North Vancouver and Langford on Vancouver Island . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Howard describes Kira as an &amp;ldquo ; A-class site&amp;rdquo ; for its proximity to the Evergreen Line , which opened in December 2016 and has served as a catalyst to fast-paced development along both sides of the SkyTrain line . Kira is located just a five-minute walk from the Burquitlam station , with no need for residents to cross busy streets . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kira&amp;rsquo ; s brick exterior is reminiscent of New York&amp;rsquo ; s brownstones , made modern with the double-height glass atrium . Inside , Woodbridge has ensured the spaces will work for the modern-day family by allowing sufficient storage through pantries in the kitchen , linen closets and counter space in the bathrooms , where the vanities are large enough to provide a bank of four drawers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Portico Design Group has provided two neutral colour palettes in the open-concept floor plans . Laminate flooring is standard in the dining and living areas , while the bedrooms have plush carpeting .
