Now is the time to get rid of anything you&amp;rsquo ; re not using ( donate , sell or throw out ) and for items you wish to keep there are some great &lt;a href= " https : //garageguru.ca/ " &gt;shelving solutions for garages&lt;/a&gt; out there , the key being keep your garage floor empty . &lt;/p&gt; **45;26044;TOOLONG ... &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26091;TOOLONG ... offices&lt;/a&gt; are are often neglected areas of the home , quickly overrun with old bills , papers and stationery odds and ends . Now is the time to tackle this room . Start with surfaces . Completely clear away anything on your desk or book shelves ( apart from your newly organized books ) and then go through all your files , folders and drawers , clearing out anything you don&amp;rsquo ; t need to keep . &lt;/p&gt; **46;26126;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;26174;TOOLONG ... title= " Clean out and wipe out your fridge , labelling anything left behind . While you&amp;rsquo ; re at it , wipe out the inside of your cupboards and drawers . Get rid of anything you don&amp;rsquo ; t love , including that cutlery set you inherited that takes up precious room . Chuck out anything in the fridge you can&amp;rsquo ; t see yourself using within the month ( and the freezer too ) and commit to wiping out the fridge and defrosting the freeze . Take the time to &lt;a href= " https : **33;26275;TOOLONG ... jars and containers . &lt;/a&gt; That will save you plenty of time during the rest of the year . &lt;/p&gt; **46;26310;TOOLONG ... &lt;p&gt; If you&amp;rsquo ; re lucky enough to have a storage shed , no matter how small , now is the time to clear it out . Walk at Lelem to launch new UBC communityhttp : **31;26945;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;26978;TOOLONG ... title= " Cedar Walk is a project from Polygon Cedar Walk Homes Ltd. in Vancouver PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Cedar Walk is a project from Polygon Cedar Walk Homes Ltd. in Vancouver PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " JARUSHA " **28;27014;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 996108 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lelem is a word that means &amp;lsquo ; home&amp;rsquo ; in the language of the Musqueam , but as Doug Avis explains it , it can convey so much more . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Davis is vice-president , real estate , for the Musqueam Capital Corporation , the economic development arm of the Musqueam Indian Band , which is now developing the first phase of its 22-acre Lelem community adjacent to Pacific Spirit Park at UBC. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The whole idea of what Lelem means &amp;mdash ; home &amp;mdash ; is of Musqueam welcoming the rest of the world to this site , to enjoy it and also to experience a bit of Musqueam culture , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To experience that culture , Avis says , is to appreciate the strong bonds of the Musqueam people . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I can&amp;rsquo ; t think of a community that&amp;rsquo ; s closer than the Musqueam are , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; They even call their friends cousins . So we want , in a subtle way , to encourage more social interaction than you would normally see in a development . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lelem , which will comprise some 1,250 homes when complete in some 10 years , will provide opportunities for residents to interact courtesy of a new 15,000-square-foot community centre , day care and adventure playground . Also on site , and being developed as part of the community&amp;rsquo ; s first phase , will be a mixed-use village area with a plaza , grocery store , restaurants , shops and subsidized and rental units . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27044;TOOLONG ... title= " The adventure playground at the Lelem community at UBC . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " The adventure playground at the Lelem community at UBC . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27080;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1001238 " /&gt; But residents of Lelem &amp;mdash ; and of the nearby west-side neighbourhood &amp;mdash ; may also find occasions to strike up conversation when strolling the extensive trail system or enjoying the beauty of what Avis calls &amp;ldquo ; the real jewel&amp;rdquo ; of Lelem : a three-acre park of towering cedars and Douglas fir. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s steps from this park &amp;mdash ; so far unnamed &amp;mdash ; where the first residential component of Lelem will rise : an 18-storey concrete highrise from Polygon called Cedar Walk at Lelem . More than half of its 140 condos and townhomes have now been sold . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As one of what Avis calls the Musqueam&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;ldquo ; preferred developers , &amp;rdquo ; Polygon has erected a one-bedroom display home on the site with floor-to-ceiling windows that take in the stunning views of the enormous trees . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s really the huge thing about Cedar Walk , &amp;rdquo ; says Goldie Alam , Polygon&amp;rsquo ; s senior vice-president of marketing . &amp;ldquo ; You&amp;rsquo ; re basically living in a park , but surrounded by new amenities , close to the west side , and close to UBC. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27110;TOOLONG ... title= " Cedar Walk is a project from Polygon Cedar Walk Homes Ltd. in Vancouver PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Cedar Walk is a project from Polygon Cedar Walk Homes Ltd. in Vancouver PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " JARUSHA " **28;27146;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1001241 " /&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s that proximity to the university &amp;mdash ; it&amp;rsquo ; s but a short stroll away &amp;mdash ; that has resulted in most of the units so far being purchased by those with a UBC connection , Alam notes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Either they&amp;rsquo ; ve gone there in the past and they just love the area , or they&amp;rsquo ; ve got kids who are going there . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Avis notes that Lelem is taking shape on traditional Musqueam territory included in the 2008 reconciliation , settlement and benefits agreement with the province , and takes very seriously the notion that they&amp;rsquo ; re &amp;ldquo ; stewards of the land&amp;rdquo ; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To that end , any trees that have come down have been &amp;mdash ; and will be &amp;mdash ; be harvested and reused , in the construction of the community centre , for instance , or in benches dotting the site . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Cedar Walk homes , which will be in proximity to such UBC amenities as the Chan Centre for the Performing Arts , the UBC Aquatic Centre and the Museum of Anthropology , will have exteriors that reflect their natural setting , both in colours and materials . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Inside , occupants will find engineered wood flooring in the main living areas and wool carpeting in the bedrooms . They will also have the choice of two colour schemes : &amp;lsquo ; Earth&amp;rsquo ; and &amp;lsquo ; Sky&amp;rsquo ; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kitchens will be fitted with natural wood or high-gloss white cabinets , engineered stone countertops and full-height marble tile backsplashes. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ensuites , meantime , will have imported marble tile floors , rain showerheads , vanities with accent lighting and square undermount basins . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Homes will also have stacking washers and dryers and roller shades on all windows . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For Eric Xie , who will be graduating this month with a degree in geological engineering from UBC , a Polygon home at the university represents familiar territory . Since 2014 , when he and his family moved to Vancouver from Regina , there have been five apartment purchases &amp;mdash ; all from Polygon. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We are currently living in one of them as our primary residence , and then my parents will switch to a larger apartment in the spring of 2020 , and then either sell or rent the one that we currently live in , &amp;rdquo ; says Xie , who cites the developer&amp;rsquo ; s reputation and &amp;ldquo ; build quality&amp;rdquo ; as among the reasons they were attracted to Polygon projects . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Most recently , they have purchased as investments two apartments at Cedar Walk , each a one-bedroom unit measuring about 825 square feet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For investors , we really value the location of Cedar Walk . It is closer to the UBC campus than Wesbrook Village and it is surrounded by different stores and the brand-new community centre , which makes it an ideal home for future students . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Polygon will also be a player in the next phase of Lelem , and Alam notes that the developer shares much of its vision with the Musqueam Capital Corporation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Our two organizations have a lot of common beliefs in the way we do business , which is why we work together so well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Respect for our history , for community , for our surroundings and the environment &amp;mdash ; all that was really evident in our planning process with MCC . Scott BrownTue , 30 Apr 2019 15:53:14 **45;28213;TOOLONG ... NewsReal **28;28260;TOOLONG on the rise in Metro Vancouver housing markethttp : **34;28290;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;28326;TOOLONG ... title= " A significant benefit of co-ownership is that each co-owner can have their own mortgage to cover their percentage share of the cost of the home . " alt= " A significant benefit of co-ownership is that each co-owner can have their own mortgage to cover their percentage share of the cost of the home . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;28362;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 991868 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;28392;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Can&amp;rsquo ; t break into the sky-high Lower Mainland &lt;a href= " http : **34;28428;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;housing&lt;/a&gt; market ? Perhaps it&amp;rsquo ; s time to consider co-ownership , a creative option of sharing the financial burden of buying a home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;28464;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;28500;TOOLONG is a way of buying real estate with one or more individuals . They could be family members or friends and the ownership percentage of the property can vary depending on the needs and financial resources of the co-owners. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For example , it could be a 50-50 split between two individuals or a 30-70 split , or a three-way split between three parties . A significant benefit of co-ownership is that each co-owner can have their own mortgage to cover their percentage share of the cost of the home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Samantha Gale , CEO of the Canadian Mortgage Brokers &lt;a href= " http : //www.cmba-achc.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **25;28528;TOOLONG &amp;ndash ; British Columbia , says co-owners become &amp;ldquo ; tenants in common , &amp;rdquo ; which means they have exclusive ownership of their percentage of title . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Generally , the ownership percentage of a tenant in common interest will reflect the respective contributions from co-owners of the deposit and closing costs , and ongoing obligation to pay for the mortgage and maintenance costs , such as 50/50 or 33/67 , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Usually , the co-owners will divide the property into exclusive use areas , which may be entirely separate suites , or just separate bedrooms with the balance of the house being shared , adds Gale . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , decisions about sharing costs and exclusive use areas can be complex , as co-owners typically enter the process with different amounts of down payment and income levels to service the mortgage and maintenance costs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; To complicate matters , houses are rarely set up perfectly for co-ownership , such as with two perfectly equal suites or bedrooms , and co-owners have to make difficult decisions about how to split up uneven exclusive use areas and/or how to pay for renovations to these areas , &amp;rdquo ; says Gale . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;28555;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;28597;TOOLONG ... to own a detached home with a yard in expensive Vancouver ? Try **28;28632;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;28662;TOOLONG ... firm proposes concept for ' missing middle ' **28;28697;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;28727;TOOLONG ... are the best and worst places in Canada to buy a home on one **27;28762;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; She recommends that the parties have a good understanding of how their co-ownership will work and ensure that their agreement is written down in a legally enforceable form . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Co-owners should consider hiring a lawyer to draft a formal agreement for them &amp;ndash ; it will likely not be needed until there is a hiccup in the relationship , but if there is a dispute , the agreement will be vitally important to resolving the dispute , &amp;rdquo ; says Gale . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ryan McKinley , senior mortgage development manager at &lt;a href= " https : //www.vancity.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vancity&lt;/a&gt; agrees , noting that Vancity strongly recommends that individuals enter into a co-ownership agreement that sets out the &lt;a href= " http : **34;28791;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;ownership&lt;/a&gt; rights of both parties and provides for an equitable distribution of the property if the relationship ends , or the property needs to be disposed of for any reason . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He points that people have always been able to co-own and it&amp;rsquo ; s easy enough to put two people on the land title . What&amp;rsquo ; s difficult is to be able to figure out what happens if someone wants to sell their share , or becomes disabled , gets married or passes away . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Basically , this agreement ensures that the investment of all buyers is protected , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancity&amp;rsquo ; s Mixer Mortgage is specifically designed for first-time homebuyers who want to share the cost of buying a home with one or more friends , roommates , co-workers or family members . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We have the ability to split &lt;a href= " http : **34;28827;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;mortgage&lt;/a&gt; terms , &amp;rdquo ; says McKinley. &amp;ldquo ; For example , if two people or two couples are taking on half a million dollars of debt , and $200,000 is earmarked for one person and $300,000 is earmarked for the other , those people could potentially pick different terms , different rates and different amortizations that are more applicable to them . The process is quick when done by professionals , and the final product looks fantastic , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Murray says refacing has been around for quite a while , but the ease of installation makes this the most popular choice right now . &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;29703;TOOLONG ... title= " Before : This condo kitchen had cabinets that stopped almost two feet from the nine-foot ceilings , plus an island in a wood finish on parts of it . Todd : The first journalist to really capture Vancouver 's housing crisishttp : **34;30367;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;30403;TOOLONG ... title= " I ca n't count how many Canadian news stories have appeared since Jim Sutherland 's piece in 2010 that faithfully quoted a host of politicians , developers and academics who denied the trans-national realities he pointed to . " alt= " I ca n't count how many Canadian news stories have appeared since Jim Sutherland 's piece in 2010 that faithfully quoted a host of politicians , developers and academics who denied the trans-national realities he pointed to . " data-portal-copyright= " NICK PROCAYLO " **28;30439;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 996268 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jim Sutherland nailed the key causes of Metro Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s housing crunch almost a decade ago . But few noticed . And many denied it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As a result , the city descended into an ugly affordability crisis , which probably could have been avoided . It&amp;rsquo ; s only in the past couple of years that politicians have mounted a serious response to the tremendous power foreign capital has had in shaping B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s housing market . But Sutherland&amp;rsquo ; s article had explained much of it nine , long , painful years earlier . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The lengthy 2010 essay by the former editor of The Vancouver Sun&amp;rsquo ; s Mix section , who has also served as chief editor to Canadian housing and lifestyle magazines , captured how the world&amp;rsquo ; s wealthy , especially from China , were seeking a safe haven in Metro&amp;rsquo ; s high-end housing , were arriving largely through Canada&amp;rsquo ; s dubious investor-immigrant program and were making most of their riches outside the country . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; If &amp;lsquo ; buyers from China&amp;rsquo ; answers the &amp;lsquo ; who&amp;rsquo ; question about Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s unique real estate market , the followup question &amp;mdash ; &amp;lsquo ; Where is this leading ? &amp;rdquo ; &amp;mdash ; is harder to answer , &amp;rdquo ; Sutherland wrote in a Report on Business Magazine article headlined : &amp;ldquo ; The Price of Paradise : Wealthy immigrants from China are happy to pay stratospherically for homes in Vancouver . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As Sutherland probed a housing affordability ratio that even in 2010 was &amp;ldquo ; the steepest on the continent , &amp;rdquo ; he detailed the &amp;ldquo ; torrid affair between eastern Asia and Vancouver real estate &amp;hellip ; When Chinese immigrants buy property in Vancouver &amp;mdash ; and they utterly dominate the top end of the market &amp;mdash ; they&amp;rsquo ; re actually buying a form of insurance . And what Vancouver gets is an economy that boasts a lot of froth , and not much substance . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Given his experience with housing , Sutherland was astute to track down a prepublication copy of University of B.C. geographer &lt;a href= " http : **34;30469;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;David Ley 's groundbreaking book , Millionaire Migrants&lt;/a&gt; , which showed the most expensive neighbourhoods in Metro were those with high immigrant populations . And he quoted Ley explaining it&amp;rsquo ; s often accurate to describe rich Asian satellite families in B.C. as &amp;ldquo ; migrants&amp;rdquo ; rather than &amp;ldquo ; immigrants , &amp;rdquo ; because &lt;a href= " http : **34;30505;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;many spend most of their time in Asia . &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;30541;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;30583;TOOLONG ... Todd : Immigrants have major impact on Canada 's housing prices : **26;30619;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;30647;TOOLONG ... Todd : Why we need to stop subsidizing foreign ownership of Vancouver **28;30683;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;30713;TOOLONG ... Todd : There 's nothing ' racist ' about Vancouver housing **26;30749;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some of Sutherland&amp;rsquo ; s early insights have become widely accepted in the past couple of years in Metro , though , as Ley points out , not in other North American gateway cities for Asian migrants , such as San Francisco or even Toronto . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But I can&amp;rsquo ; t count how many Canadian news stories have appeared since Sutherland&amp;rsquo ; s piece appeared that faithfully quoted a host of politicians , developers , academics or so-called &lt;a href= " http : **34;30777;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;experts who obfuscated or utterly denied the transnational realities he pointed to&lt;/a&gt; . Many of those denials came laced with accusations of xenophobia. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Asked this week why he wrote about Metro becoming &amp;ldquo ; the Geneva of the Pacific&amp;rdquo ; for dynamic plutocrats , Sutherland said he began in the early 2000s to research how housing prices were rocketing up at the same time immigration was growing from China . And , in his six years editing Western Living magazine , he had &amp;ldquo ; noticed that a large proportion of the houses we featured were built for newcomers from Asia . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sutherland also happens to live in Dunbar , on Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s pricey west side . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was aware many people disputed the Chinese influx and its effect on real estate , &lt;a href= " http : **34;30813;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;decrying racist motives and citing lack of data&lt;/a&gt; . But I could see with my own eyes the houses coming down and the Chinese people moving in to the new mini-mansions. &amp;rdquo ; Friends in real estate , including from Hong Kong , also unanimously assured him &amp;ldquo ; virtually all were from the ( Chinese ) mainland . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30849;TOOLONG ... title= " " I was aware many people disputed the Chinese influx and its effect on real estate , decrying racist motives and citing lack of data , " says Sutherland . " alt= " " I was aware many people disputed the Chinese influx and its effect on real estate , decrying racist motives and citing lack of data , " says Sutherland . " data-portal-copyright= " Paul Joseph " **28;30885;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 995696 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The veteran editor/freelance writer , who is mostly retired , believes his piece of almost a decade ago makes clear he&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;ldquo ; an enthusiastic supporter of immigration&amp;rdquo ; and &amp;ldquo ; doesn&amp;rsquo ; t share the longing that many people seem to have for a less-cosmopolitan Vancouver . &amp;rdquo ; He also wasn&amp;rsquo ; t convinced that the city&amp;rsquo ; s housing prices at the time were ridiculously out of whack ; until , that is , the last &amp;ldquo ; crazy upward spiral&amp;rdquo ; that began in 2014. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sutherland , however , had become discouraged years ago by Canada&amp;rsquo ; s immigrant investor programs and &amp;ldquo ; not just because letting people buy their way in smells bad . &amp;rdquo ; He learned how little benefit Canadians received from the &lt;a href= " http : **34;30915;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;widely abused programs&lt;/a&gt; , which the Conservatives cancelled in 2014 , but which continue today in provincial forms . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It has now been almost a decade since I wrote that article , and I wish I could say everything has turned out well , &amp;rdquo ; Sutherland says . &amp;ldquo ; True , real estate values are edging back to where I think they should be , and the worst excesses of the immigrant investor program have perhaps been dulled. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But , while it&amp;rsquo ; s clear to me that Vancouver is drawing lots of the smart and ambitious immigrants that we need in order to thrive , it&amp;rsquo ; s not clear that another type , the ones who were already well-established upon their arrival , are working very hard to help Vancouver fulfil the entrepot role I envisioned back in 2010. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Regardless of whether he&amp;rsquo ; s technically the first journalist to have twigged to the housing crisis a decade ago or longer , Sutherland is not alone in wishing things had evolved more auspiciously for more people of this city . &lt;/p&gt; Douglas ToddTue , 16 Apr 2019 21:57:29 **45;31252;TOOLONG ... NewsNationalPoliticsReal EstateStaff BlogsA home inspection may be the best investment you ever makehttp : **36;31299;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;31337;TOOLONG ... title= " Home inspection is an important step when considering a potential purchase . " alt= " Home inspection is an important step when considering a potential purchase . " data-portal-copyright= " Getty Images " **28;31373;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 989490 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Home inspections have never gone out of style , but they have lost some of their appeal in the frenzied real estate market of &lt;a href= " http : **34;31403;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Greater Vancouver&lt;/a&gt; in the past few years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since about 2015 , the number of inspections has dropped dramatically &amp;mdash ; the first casualty of high-pressure offers of sale on properties when days , and even hours , used to count in whether a sale was completed . The number of inspections is increasing due to the cooling market , but it&amp;rsquo ; s a shortsighted move not to undertake a professional home inspection , says Helene Barton , executive director of the Home Inspectors Association of BC &lt;a href= " https : //hiabc.ca " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt; ( HIABC ) &lt;/a&gt; . And changing mandates for home inspectors are affecting the industry , which affects you , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We get calls from people when something goes wrong , and there&amp;rsquo ; s nothing we can do , &amp;rdquo ; she says of those who haven&amp;rsquo ; t had professional inspections. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jonathan Sheppard , HIABC vice-president of &lt;a href= " https : //www.jamesdobney.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;James Dobney Inspections&lt;/a&gt; agrees . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I do believe that 98 per cent of the transactions should have an inspection &amp;mdash ; both new and old builds included because what we find would floor you . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But for many homeowners , it&amp;rsquo ; s just as easy not to have an inspection . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; If you throw in the do-it-yourself renovations , along with multiples owners , the homes are filled with unprofessional wiring , plumbing , carpentry , smoke and carbon monoxide detectors missing or out of date , decks that are improperly constructed , missing footings , and poorly constructed hand railings with the decks carrying excess weight loads from non-permitted solariums , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some real gems Sheppard cites include crawlspaces with &lt;a href= " http : **34;31439;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;water ingress&lt;/a&gt; , foundations with cracks due to settlement and Douglas fir trees planted within two feet of a building . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The biggest advantage of a home inspection is that we educate the client about the building that is being purchased , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;nbsp ; &lt;span class= " Apple-converted-space " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But to complicate matters , there is no longer a need for inspectors to belong to a professional organization in B.C. In 2016 &lt;a href= " https : **29;31475;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Consumer Protection BC&lt;/a&gt; introduced a new regulation that lowered the requirements necessary to become a home inspector , and reduced the qualifications of trainers for inspectors . Barton points out that if an inspector isn&amp;rsquo ; t a member of the HIABC , the consumer has no recourse and may , in fact , not be getting proper advice and recommendations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sheppard says HIABC inspectors have the highest standards , have mandatory field training and must pass an exam , plus continue ongoing education . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The non-aligned inspectors have none of this ongoing commitment , &amp;rdquo ; Sheppard says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s an ongoing battle of sorts , Barton says . So why were the requirements lowered ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I have absolutely no idea , &amp;rdquo ; Barton said . &amp;ldquo ; Anything with government is slow , but at least they are working with us. &amp;rdquo ; The HIABC continues to enforce high standards , training and educational requirements as it works with the Minister of Public Safety and the Solicitor-General on this . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On top of this , &lt;a href= " http : **34;31506;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;home inspector renewal licensing fees&lt;/a&gt; increased by about 25 per cent each year for three years , with ensuing increases in 2019 to 2020 , by which the fees will almost double from $525 to $1,025. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What it means is that the additional costs will be passed on to the consumer , and such fees may well dissuade people from entering the profession . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Home inspectors are underpaid , &amp;rdquo ; says Barton , who adds that the regulations , coupled with fewer inspections being performed , hits inspectors hard . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The best advice Barton can give , she says , is to check the HIABC website and go to the &lt;a href= " https : **29;31542;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Find an Inspector&lt;/a&gt; tab to search for qualified inspectors in your area . Interview two or three , she suggests , before choosing one . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And don&amp;rsquo ; t rely on your realtor to provide you with a name , or a recommendation whether to undertake an inspection . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Experienced realtors control the sale . &amp;nbsp ; Often a home inspection rests in their court with a recommendation either way , &amp;rdquo ; says Sheppard . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So what&amp;rsquo ; s the advice for homeowners in this situation ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Realtors should be giving out a minimum of three names of inspectors , &amp;rdquo ; Barton says . &amp;ldquo ; Realtors should be at arm&amp;rsquo ; s length from inspectors . Make sure everybody is doing their job . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;31573;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;31615;TOOLONG ... inspectors ' association says licensing-fee increase a ' money grab ' &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;31650;TOOLONG ... are buying blind ' : Home inspections drop drastically in no-subjects **27;31685;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;31714;TOOLONG ... construction may prove to be a functional solution to the housing **27;31749;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; Lynn MitgesMon , 15 Apr 2019 12:30:53 **45;31778;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingBuying &amp;amp ; SellingCondosLocal BusinessNewer B.C. condo buildings using more energy : studyhttp : **35;31825;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;31862;TOOLONG ... title= " Condo towers under construction on Gilmore in the Brentwood area of Burnaby. " alt= " Condo towers under construction on Gilmore in the Brentwood area of Burnaby. " data-portal-copyright= " Gerry Kahrmann " **28;31898;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 994245 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **33;31928;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; British Columbians living in newer high-rise condos likely have a higher energy footprint than they might think , according to new data from B.C. Hydro . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Crown corporation compared the electricity consumption of condominium buildings that were constructed after 2010 and higher than five storeys to others built in the last four decades , and found the newer condos used almost twice as much electricity per square foot . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This is due at least in part to newer buildings being equipped with all the latest amenities such as hot tubs , media rooms and gyms. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; B.C. Hydro has seen an almost 22-per-cent increase in the number of apartment and condominium accounts since 2011. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With 470,000 accounts , apartments and condos are just over one quarter of B.C. Hydro&amp;rsquo ; s residential customers and account for about 18 per cent of the province&amp;rsquo ; s residential electricity consumption . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Vancouver alone , where more than a third of residents live in condos , the number of hydro accounts for apartments and condos has increased by 14 per cent since 2011 . In Victoria , the increase was 10 per cent , and 15 per cent in Kamloops. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31963;TOOLONG ... title= " Condo towers under construction in the Brentwood area of Burnaby. " alt= " Condo towers under construction in the Brentwood area of Burnaby. " data-portal-copyright= " Gerry Kahrmann " **28;31999;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 919180 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We know with high housing prices in the province , the construction and sale of condominiums has really increased in the last few years as British Columbians are looking to purchase smaller homes , &amp;rdquo ; said B.C. Hydro spokesperson Tanya Fish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s really important for us to understand the changing electricity demand in the province , and really great for B.C. Hydro&amp;rsquo ; s long-term planning . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The largest period of growth in electricity use was between 1999 and 2009 , when high-rise buildings&amp;rsquo ; consumption grew by 65 per cent , from 647,570 kilowatt hours ( kWh ) per year to 1.07 million kWh . The average high-rise condo building ( those with five storeys or more ) built after 2010 uses about 1.05 million kWh of electricity per year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The upward trend has also been seen in condo buildings that are heated by methods other than electricity , such as gas or geothermal. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This is despite individual units becoming more energy efficient because of features such as LED lighting and Energy Star appliances . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Fish said the increase is largely due to the amenities in newer buildings , such as pools , hot tubs , party rooms , fitness centres and movie theatres . Newer apartment buildings tend to use less electricity than condos because they have fewer of these amenities , she added . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Elevators , lighting in the lobby , halls and parking garage , and heating and cooling systems also add to the electricity bill . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In newer condo buildings , about 50 per cent of the overall electricity use is covered by a common-use account that is funded by strata fees . If the cost appeared on a resident&amp;rsquo ; s hydro bill , it could add up to an additional $40 each month . The average apartment or condo&amp;rsquo ; s bill is now $43 per month . The average single-family home&amp;rsquo ; s hydro bill is $103 per month . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To reduce electricity use , Fish said , developers and strata councils could use occupancy sensors to ensure lights are only on when people are present , upgrade to more efficient lighting , and add automated controls for heating , ventilation and air conditioning systems . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s options there just to cut down on the overall electricity use of the entire building , &amp;rdquo ; Fish said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those living in apartments and condos could also shade their windows on sunny days , use a ceiling fan , and install draft proofing around windows and doors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **31;32029;TOOLONG " **38;32062;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/jensaltman " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;32102;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;32135;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;32177;TOOLONG ... group disputes government claim B.C. Hydro wasting $16 billion on unneeded **32;32212;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;32246;TOOLONG ... Hydro 's private power projects wasting billions , says new **27;32281;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;32310;TOOLONG ... government putting alternative energy sector on ice&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; Jennifer SaltmanFri , 12 Apr 2019 20:47:59 **45;32345;TOOLONG ... NewsMichael Geller : In celebration of woodhttp : **36;32392;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;32430;TOOLONG ... title= " Prodigy is an award-winning project from the Adera Development Corp . " alt= " Prodigy is an award-winning project from the Adera Development Corp . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32466;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 979735 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A few years ago , I was having a conversation with an acquaintance regarding The Lagoons , a condominium development at the entrance to Granville Island . He was certain his third-floor apartment was in a concrete building . I told him it was a wood-frame building with a lightweight concrete topping on the floor . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Do you want to bet a bottle of scotch ? &amp;rdquo ; he asked . I told him it would be an unfair bet since I was part of the project&amp;rsquo ; s development team and had watched it being built . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Well it seems like a concrete building , &amp;rdquo ; he responded . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I was reminded of this exchange at the 15th annual Wood Design Awards organized by WoodWorks , a program of the Canadian Wood Council that celebrates innovative structural and architectural achievements using wood . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In early March , more than 400 architects , engineers , designers , builders , owners and government officials gathered in the Vancouver Convention Centre , an appropriate venue considering the walls are covered in B.C. wood . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There were 103 nominations in 14 categories , showcasing wood&amp;rsquo ; s strength , beauty , versatility , environmental and cost benefits . Submissions came from throughout B.C. , as well as the U.S. and Asia , with international projects in China , Korea and Tajikistan . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since 2005 when the program began , there have been some remarkable changes in wood construction in British Columbia . In those days , the maximum permitted height for a wood-frame building was four storeys . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Today , six-storey wood-frame buildings are becoming the norm , and during the awards program , it was suggested that one day 12 storeys might be the norm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I thought this might be wishful thinking , but a week later the B.C. government announced changes to the building code to allow the construction of wood buildings up to 12 storeys . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Much of the credit for this code change must go to Brock Commons , an 18-storey wooden student residence at UBC that won multiple prizes at the 2017 awards . At that time , it was the tallest &amp;ldquo ; mass timber&amp;rdquo ; building in the world using new engineered wood products and construction techniques . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While some may question whether taller wood buildings can be sturdy and safe , it is noteworthy that most of Gastown&amp;rsquo ; s older buildings have structures made of wood . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While their heavy timber construction differs from the lightweight &amp;ldquo ; balloon frame&amp;rdquo ; construction used in houses and smaller apartments , today&amp;rsquo ; s new cross-laminated timber &amp;mdash ; or CLT &amp;mdash ; panels , glue-laminated timber , and parallel strand lumber products are the equivalent of heavy timber . These products , made from gluing layers of smaller pieces of lumber together , can be as strong and fireproof &amp;mdash ; yes , fireproof &amp;mdash ; as steel and concrete . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The award categories included residential wood design , commercial and industrial wood design , interior design , use of red cedar , prefabrication and other innovations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For those of us interested in housing design and construction , there were two categories , single-family and multi-family. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The seven single-family finalists included Michael Green , a Vancouver architect who has become internationally acclaimed for his pioneering wood designs . Other finalists included architects Peter Rose and Farouk Noormohamed . However , the winners were Clinton Cuddington and Piers Cunnington of Measured Architecture for their visually striking Shift House . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32496;TOOLONG ... title= " Shift House , a project from Clinton Cuddington and Piers Cunnington of Measured Architecture , took the single-family award at the 2019 Wood Design Awards . " alt= " Shift House , a project from Clinton Cuddington and Piers Cunnington of Measured Architecture , took the single-family award at the 2019 Wood Design Awards . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32532;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 979737 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The jury commented that the shingles , modernized with seven custom colours , were installed on a near 45-degree bias , with similarly coloured shingles paired to create an illusion of 10-by-10-inch hexagonal shapes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the shingles were stained and refined , the architects also used unstained , tongue-and-groove Western red cedar as a secondary cladding . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finalists in the multi-family category included Allwood Place in Abbotsford , Royce in White Rock , Travino Square in Saanich , West Quay in North Vancouver and Yorkson Creek in Langley , with the winner being Adera Development Corp . for Virtuoso . ( Adera won in the same category last year for its Prodigy project. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Designed by Rositch Hemphill Architects , Virtuoso comprises a six-storey mass timber building and townhomes with a stunning West Coast design , and is Canada&amp;rsquo ; s first private residential multi-family building to be constructed using cross-laminated timber . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Like Brock Commons , Virtuoso uses CLT panels in its flooring systems . They are exposed at each balcony overhang , enhancing the building&amp;rsquo ; s style and highlighting this beautiful wood material . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another benefit of this innovative floor- and wall-assembly system is that it can significantly reduce sound transmission between homes , exceeding standards in both traditional wood-frame and even concrete construction . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was not that long ago that the so-called leaky condo crisis caused significant grief for many B.C. homeowners , architects and developers . While industry experts questioned whether wood construction would ever become popular again , it was evident to everyone attending the awards program that , thanks to new wood products and improved design and construction techniques , wood is back . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This is not just a good thing for the residential construction industry . It is also positive for B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s economy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Michael Geller is a Vancouver architect , real estate consultant and developer . He serves on the adjunct faculty of SFU 's Centre for Sustainable Development and Resource and Environmental Management . His blog can be found at &lt;a href= " http : **32;32562;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;32596;TOOLONG ... and he can be reached at &lt;a href= " mailto:geller@sfu.ca " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **27;32629;TOOLONG &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Michael GellerSat , 30 Mar 2019 12:03:44 **45;32658;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; **27;32705;TOOLONG key to the draw of Duethttp : **36;32734;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;32772;TOOLONG ... title= " NU Duet is a project from Adera in Coquitlam . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " NU Duet is a project from Adera in Coquitlam . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " RAEF Architectural Photographer " **28;32808;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 978843 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Adera has been in operation for 50 years and the company is celebrating the milestone with the launch of Duet , a 72-home project that will be noteworthy for sustainability in multi-family residential living . Eric Andreasen , Adera&amp;rsquo ; s vice-president of marketing and sales , says the quality of the building is set to be a big selling point . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ve always been a leader in sustainability and we&amp;rsquo ; ve only built in wood in 50 years , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re taking that concept to the next level with &amp;lsquo ; SmartWood&amp;rsquo ; , which will allow us to beat code when it comes to sustainability . Financially , it&amp;rsquo ; s cheaper to operate on a monthly basis and it&amp;rsquo ; s also helping the planet . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Duet will also incorporate what we&amp;rsquo ; ve trademarked as QuietHome , &amp;rdquo ; Andreasen added . &amp;ldquo ; This is a propriety system of floor design and dividing walls that means our buildings perform better than the competition when it comes to the acoustics . Sound transmission is significantly reduced . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The sales centre for Duet in Burquitlam Plaza at 552 Clarke Road features a demonstration of Adera&amp;rsquo ; s noise-reducing technology . Potential buyers can compare noise levels in specially constructed rooms built either to standard code or using QuietHome. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Duet also features cross-laminated timber &amp;mdash ; or CLT &amp;mdash ; in the floors and elevator shafts of the building . A carbon-sequestering material that uses wood from sustainably managed forests , CLT performs as well if not better than concrete in numerous ways , according to Andreasen . Adera has installed an example at the Duet sales centre so that visitors will be able to experience it for themselves . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Andreasen said the location of the six-storey residential building less than three blocks from the Burquitlam SkyTrain station on the boundary of Burnaby and West Coquitlam is also likely to prove popular with a wide range of buyers . In its neighbourhood plan for the area , the City of Coquitlam envisioned a walkable , complete and transit-oriented neighbourhood with a mix of housing types and access to shopping , jobs and amenities . Duet is part of that transformation , according to Andreasen. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a very attractive part of the greater Vancouver area right now in terms of the value it offers and the accessibility it provides to downtown , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Duet will give people an affordable place to live , but also allows them to go anywhere with the SkyTrain or West Coast Express very easily &amp;ndash ; rapid transit on your doorstep . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In times when people are getting a bit more discerning about where they spend their money , they want to make sure they&amp;rsquo ; re buying from somebody who&amp;rsquo ; s been around and has the experience they can count on , &amp;rdquo ; Andreason added . &amp;ldquo ; Over 50 years we&amp;rsquo ; ve worked with the very best trades and consultants in the industry and we&amp;rsquo ; re confident in the product that we&amp;rsquo ; re going to build . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As for the homes themselves , kitchens will feature quartz slab countertops with porcelain tile backsplashes and single-handle faucets with pull-down sprays in matte black finishes . The appliance packages are a combination of KitchenAid and Fisher &amp;amp ; Paykel. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Homes at Duet come in two colour schemes , but there&amp;rsquo ; s plenty of opportunity to customize . Adera&amp;rsquo ; s I.D . By Me program allows buyers to personalize kitchens , bathrooms and living areas according to their liking . Options under the program include custom millwork , upgrades to the kitchen appliances , swapping out the tub for a shower with pivoting glass door and adding space-saving custom closet organizers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Duet will include a common kitchen and lounge , secure bike lockers , a storage locker for every home and electric vehicle-charging stalls . The interior designer for the project is Cynthia Florano Designs and the building was designed by Integra Architecture Inc. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32838;TOOLONG ... title= " Duet is a project from Adera in Coquitlam . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Duet is a project from Adera in Coquitlam . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32874;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 980803 " /&gt; &amp;ldquo ; One of our architectural influences has always been West Coast modern design and we&amp;rsquo ; ve also leaned on natural materials like cedar , &amp;rdquo ; Andreasen said . &amp;ldquo ; You&amp;rsquo ; ll see from the renderings that Duet has a feature corner with extensive use of natural materials that makes the building look really quite impressive . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Homes at Duet have one to three bedrooms , range in size between 479 and 1,050 square feet and prices start at $394,900 . Andreasen said he expects buyers from a wide range of demographics. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; A lot of people grew up in Burnaby and West Coquitlam and have maybe since been living in Yaletown or Kitsilano , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; Many we&amp;rsquo ; ve spoken to are actually coming back to the area or still have family there . Our demographic is going to be people who work downtown and need easy access to it but also want to be able to afford a home in this marketplace where it&amp;rsquo ; s very expensive to own real estate . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re also seeing moms and dads who are looking at Duet on behalf of their kids , who may be students at SFU , which is very close by , &amp;rdquo ; Andreasen added . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ve had a significant amount of interest already and there&amp;rsquo ; s only 72 homes to choose from . We expect people will get in pretty quickly . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;32904;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project location : &lt;/strong&gt; 707 Como Lake Avenue , Coquitlam &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project size : &lt;/strong&gt; 72 homes with one to three bedrooms ; 479 &amp;mdash ; 1,050 square feet ; prices starting at $394,900 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Developer : &lt;/strong&gt; Adera &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Architect : &lt;/strong&gt; Integra Architecture Inc. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Interior designer : &lt;/strong&gt; Cynthia Florano Designs &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales centre : &lt;/strong&gt; #106 &amp;ndash ; 552 Clark Road Coquitlam &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales centre hours : &lt;/strong&gt; noon &amp;mdash ; 5 p.m. , daily &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales phone : &lt;/strong&gt; 604-937-9688 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Website : &lt;/strong&gt; Adera.com/Duet &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt; &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Simon BriaultSat , 23 Mar 2019 12:29:36 **45;32939;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosB.C. government launches first-of-its-kind condo pre-sale registryhttp : **32;32986;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;33020;TOOLONG ... title= " Condo pre-sales , such as this one in Langley B.C. , will need to be registered in a new government database . " data-portal-copyright= " Arlen Redekop " **28;33056;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 968922 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;33086;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; VICTORIA &amp;mdash ; B.C. has launched a &lt;a href= " https : **33;33122;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener noreferrer " &gt;first-of-its-kind registry&lt;/a&gt; to track pre-sale condo purchases , a move that may help combat tax evasion but is not expected to drive down condo prices . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The new pre-sale registry will collect data on pre-sale flipping of condo units during development and construction of a building , Finance Minister Carole James said Monday . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The names , ownership , sale and purchase prices will be shared with the Canada Revenue Agency to help make sure people aren&amp;rsquo ; t taking advantage of loopholes for income tax , capital gains tax and property transfer tax , said James . It&amp;rsquo ; s the first such registry in Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It will ensure people taking those assignments when condo assignments are flipped actually pay their fair share of taxes , &amp;rdquo ; said James . &amp;ldquo ; Right now we have no idea . There&amp;rsquo ; s no registry , there&amp;rsquo ; s no tracking of those kind of flips . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The registry will require all developers currently building condos and other strata buildings to register every four months the status of their unit contracts , starting April 30 . After construction is complete and a strata plan filed with government , the developer must report annually for the next six years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Most developers we believe will follow it , &amp;rdquo ; said James . &amp;ldquo ; They are required by law . There are also fines in place if they don&amp;rsquo ; t. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , the registry will not be available for the public and researchers . &amp;ldquo ; This will be private confidential information , &amp;rdquo ; said James . B.C. is creating a public database of beneficial ownership , that will allow people to search the names of those involved in real estate developments , corporations and numbered companies , said James . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Flipping pre-sale contracts &amp;mdash ; known as contract assigning &amp;mdash ; has been a contentious practice in recent years during the hot housing market . &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Some critics have argued pre-sales also allow wealthy foreign investors to get early access to units rather than locals . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The previous Liberal government banned so-called &amp;ldquo ; shadow flipping&amp;rdquo ; of homes in 2016 , which involved multiple quick sales without the original seller&amp;rsquo ; s knowledge and with intermediaries like real estate agents and speculators accused of pocketing the profits . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33157;TOOLONG ... title= " Condo pre-sales , such as this one in Langley , will need to be registered in a new government database . " alt= " Condo pre-sales , such as this one in Langley , will need to be registered in a new government database . " data-portal-copyright= " Arlen Redekop " **28;33193;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 968922 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Real estate experts said gathering the pre-sale assignment data is a welcome move , but cracking down on tax evaders will not actually reduce condo prices . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I know sometimes a developer has been caught selling a unit twice , so I think having more transparency is good , &amp;rdquo ; said Tom Davidoff , the director of the Centre for Urban Economics and Real Estate at the University of B.C. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But there&amp;rsquo ; s a second part of the argument which is when people buy and sell pre-sales that creates upwards pressure on prices , that I&amp;rsquo ; m much more skeptical about . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Davidoff said people who buy a pre-sale condo actually help finance years of development and construction as investors rather than homeowners . Were a bank or pension fund to do the same thing , there&amp;rsquo ; d be no question it was acceptable behaviour , said Davidoff. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They do make pre-sales more expensive when the market is hot that&amp;rsquo ; s true , &amp;rdquo ; said Davidoff. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; But I personally think buying a pre-sale is a fairly lousy way for a first-time buyer to get into the market . It&amp;rsquo ; s like buying a Bruce Springsteen ticket from a scalper &amp;mdash ; that isn&amp;rsquo ; t really a bad way to get a Bruce Springsteen ticket . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Many of the buildings with pre-sold units during the hot housing market in recent years are only now complete . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s not obvious that this behaviour makes Vancouver certainly in the long-run less affordable , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t necessarily buy that . But that said I think people evading capital gains tax and maybe laundering money or who knows &amp;hellip ; I think the transparency part is good . But that this is the path to affordability for the province , I don&amp;rsquo ; t really buy that argument . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33223;TOOLONG ... title= " Tom Davidoff , an associate professor at the Sauder School of Business . " alt= " Tom Davidoff , an associate professor at the Sauder School of Business . " data-portal-copyright= " Stuart McNIsh " **28;33259;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 809556 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; New data from the &lt;a href= " http : **34;33289;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener noreferrer " &gt;B.C . Real Estate Association released Monday&lt;/a&gt; forecast residential sales in B.C. to rise by 2.1 per cent in the 2019 fiscal year , after falling 24.5 per cent in 2018 . Prices are expected to remain mostly flat , provincewide. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Greater Vancouver sales are forecast to rise 6.5 per cent after falling 30.8 per cent last fiscal year , with the average price declining 2.7 per cent . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think if there&amp;rsquo ; s going to be one data gap that needs to be addressed in the housing market it is the pre-sale assignment market because we simply don&amp;rsquo ; t have good data on that , &amp;rdquo ; said B.C. Real Estate Association chief economist Cameron Muir . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But addressing data and tax loopholes won&amp;rsquo ; t impact prices , he added . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t see it as coercing or changing the market itself , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s not going to make housing more affordable . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Quite frankly these issues , the speculation tax , vacancy tax , foreign buyer tax and school tax none of these measures are going to make housing more affordable . They are certainly going to make revenue for government and if government uses that money to help those at the margins of housing affordability that may be a good thing from a policy perspective . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Changes to federal mortgage stress test rules in 2018 are really what have driven the cooling in the market , said Muir . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Developers helped craft the registry with government , said&amp;nbsp ; Anne McMullin , CEO of the Urban Development Institute . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s something we&amp;rsquo ; ve encouraged and supported and are part of , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; McMullin said pre-sale contract assignments are &amp;ldquo ; a very small percentage&amp;rdquo ; of overall sales and in addition being done for profit can be because of changing life circumstances during construction , such as marriage , separation or children . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; As home builders we have supported this . It doesn&amp;rsquo ; t really have anything to do with affordability , or the velocity of pre-sales its just a mechanism to ensure government can collect taxes where taxes are due . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **26;33325;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener noreferrer " **33;33353;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **27;33388;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener noreferrer " **33;33417;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;33452;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;33494;TOOLONG ... registry in B.C. targets under-the-radar condo **29;33529;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;33560;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Email &lt;a href= " **28;33589;TOOLONG " **35;33619;TOOLONG &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Rob ShawTue , 26 Feb 2019 21:43:06 **45;33656;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosLocal NewsPoliticsMaking better use of the balconyhttp : **33;33703;TOOLONG ... &lt;p&gt; As apartments become increasingly smaller , it is important to make the best possible use of every square foot of space . However , anyone looking down from a drone flying above Metro Vancouver will observe a significant portion of many apartments is often completely wasted . I refer , of course , to the balcony . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Derived from the Italian &amp;lsquo ; balcone&amp;rsquo ; , meaning scaffold , the earliest balconies date back to ancient Greece when they addressed purely functional needs such as increasing air circulation or modulating natural light into a building&amp;rsquo ; s interior . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Prior to the 1960s , few Vancouver buildings included balconies since they were generally included in the building area calculation , also known as floor space ratio or FSR . However , to encourage developers to construct balconies , Vancouver City Council in 1964 introduced an FSR exemption for residential open balconies to a maximum of eight per cent of the gross floor area . As a result , new apartment building designs throughout British Columbia began to incorporate balconies . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Unfortunately , many of these balconies were not used . Consequently , in 1985 , Vancouver City Council adopted Balcony Enclosure Guidelines to allow balconies in existing buildings to be enclosed with glass windows and walls . Council subsequently permitted the enclosure of balconies in new construction , provided the space continued to be separate and distinct from the interior of the dwelling . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As written in the 1985 guidelines , &amp;ldquo ; the purpose of an enclosed balcony is to afford an occupant the year-round enjoyment of those uses to which an open balcony normally would be used in fair and warm weather . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To address exterior design , the guidelines stated that &amp;ldquo ; careful attention should be given to preserving the design integrity of facades&amp;hellip ; through a distinctive shape and a predominance of clear glass . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Unfortunately , it did not take long before some developers were enclosing all the balconies to increase saleable area . As a result , buildings became bulkier and less attractive . Consequently , in 1995 , council amended the balcony enclosure policy to restrict enclosed balconies to no more than 50 per cent of the permitted balcony area . North-facing balconies and those along busy streets were often enclosed , while others remained open . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Today , Vancouver allows balconies up to 12 per cent of the building area to be excluded from the FSR , but new enclosed balconies are generally included in the FSR . Other municipalities continue to allow open balconies up to eight per cent of the total building area , without counting in the FSR area calculation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Over the years , throughout Metro , individual condominium owners have often enclosed their balconies . Sometimes this was done with the formal approval of the strata council and the requisite municipal permits . However , in other cases , no approvals were obtained . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I recently became aware of one Vancouver building where 11 owners who enclosed their balconies decades ago without permits are now being asked to remove their enclosures . No doubt many others could soon find themselves in a similar position . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To address complaints about the lack of variety in highrise building design , increasingly architects are using balconies to add articulation and interest to building facades . One of my favourite examples is architect Jeanne Gang&amp;rsquo ; s Aqua Tower , an 82-storey mixed-use building in Chicago . Its curvilinear balconies vary in dept from 2 to 12 feet , resulting in wave-like forms on all sides of the building . The effect is marvellous . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Other equally creative solutions are beginning to appear around Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While these balconies will certainly improve building appearance , often they too will remain unused , especially those in taller buildings where wind buffeting can be so extreme one can hardly leave anything on a balcony without the risk of the wind sucking it off . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While many apartment owners do not use their balconies , they insist on having them because they want to be able to get outside without riding the elevator or because they&amp;rsquo ; re afraid their unit will be harder to sell without a balcony . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One Toronto realtor compared the balcony to the architectural equivalent of the NordicTrack machine . &amp;ldquo ; You buy it because you want to see yourself using it , but seldom do ; though at least an exercise machine is usable in winter . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A few years ago , while travelling through Europe , I saw an elegant , transparent , retractable glass system that made balconies more usable year round without fully enclosing them . It consisted of sliding glass panels that could be completely opened and folded against the wall . When it started to rain , the panels could be quickly closed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since the glass was frameless , the exterior design of the building was not really altered . Furthermore , since there was a small air gap at the top and bottom and between the glass panels , the balcony was not completely sealed off like it would be with an enclosed balcony . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Several European companies are manufacturing these systems . They include Solarlux , ( solarlux.com ) a German company , and Airclos ( airclos.com ) based in Spain . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Perhaps the best known is Lumon ( lumon.com ) , a Finnish company that has set up a manufacturing plant in Canada . It claims its glass-panel system can result in 34-per-cent energy savings and significantly reduce noise levels . It also prevents premature balcony degradation by keeping rain , snow , wind , dust and birds away from the balcony . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Architects and developers in several Metro municipalities are starting to incorporate retractable balcony glass into their upcoming multi-family projects . It is a trend that I expect to become increasingly popular with prospective apartment buyers and renters. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , while the Lumon system can now be installed in new buildings in Abbotsford , Langley City and Langley Township without counting in the building area calculation , to have wider application in B.C. , it will be necessary for all municipalities to update their zoning bylaws. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The reality is , when these bylaws were drafted , balconies could either be open and excluded from FSR calculations , or fully enclosed and included in FSR. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; No one expected that one day a system would be designed that allows balconies to be both completely open or partially closed and evolve into a more sensible and useful space . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But then again , we weren&amp;rsquo ; t expecting Amazon&amp;rsquo ; s Alexa , Uber or autonomous cars either . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " doctext " &gt;&lt;span class= " dochighlight " &gt;Michael Geller&lt;/span&gt; is a Vancouver-based architect , planner , developer and educator . He is an adjunct professor at SFU 's Centre for Sustainable Development and can be reached at geller@sfu.ca. &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Michael GellerTue , 26 Feb 2019 02:26:02 **45;33738;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosSales slump could signal tough times ahead for B.C. realtorshttp : **32;33785;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;33819;TOOLONG ... title= " A real estate sign is pictured in Vancouver on June 12 , 2018. Over the last seven years Soprovich&amp;rsquo ; s real estate company earned more than $13 million . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the court documents Soprovich said &amp;ldquo ; it would be devastating to him if his income for support purposes is based on an average of the realty company&amp;rsquo ; s past three years&amp;rsquo ; net income &amp;hellip ; &amp;nbsp ; He says that the real estate market slowed down from 2016-2017 and is likely to slow further down in 2018. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **28;34307;TOOLONG " **35;34337;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **26;34374;TOOLONG " &gt;twi ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;34402;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;34431;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;34473;TOOLONG ... home sales fall nearly 40 per cent in January , as prices pull **25;34508;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;34535;TOOLONG ... government expects sagging Metro real estate market to cut tax **29;34570;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;34601;TOOLONG ... examples of Metro Vancouver homeowners losing big in a plunging **27;34636;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? Email &lt;a href= " **28;34665;TOOLONG " **35;34695;TOOLONG &lt;/em&gt; &amp;lt ; /p &lt;/p&gt; Glenda LuymesFri , 08 Feb 2019 16:10:26 **45;34732;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingBuying &amp;amp ; SellingCommercial Real EstateCondosLocal BusinessLocal NewsReal EstateB.C. government opens exemption process for speculation taxhttp : **37;34779;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;34818;TOOLONG ... title= " Owners of second properties in Metro Vancouver will soon be able to apply for exemptions from the government 's new speculation tax . " alt= " Owners of second properties in Metro Vancouver will soon be able to apply for exemptions from the government 's new speculation tax . " data-portal-copyright= " DARRYL DYCK " **28;34854;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 809849 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;34884;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; VICTORIA &amp;mdash ; All homeowners in B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s largest urban centres will need to apply for exemptions from the province&amp;rsquo ; s new speculation tax or receive a bill , whether they are speculators or not , the government announced Tuesday . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Ministry of Finance said letters outlining the first speculation tax fees and exemption processes will begin arriving at all homes in Greater Victoria , Nanaimo , Kelowna and Metro Vancouver ( excluding Bowen Island and Lions Bay , but including Abbotsford , Mission and Chilliwack ) starting this week . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It will be up to all homeowners to apply for exemptions , with a deadline of March 31 . Those who don&amp;rsquo ; t apply , or qualify , will be sent tax bills due to be paid by July 2 . The opt-out process will become an annual event for property owners under the new speculation tax rules . People who pay mistakenly can get a rebate within six years , according to the ministry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finance Minister Carole James was not available for an interview , but her ministry said the exemption process &amp;ldquo ; will help identify speculators and empty homes , allowing the government to crack down on speculation and make housing more affordable . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The government&amp;rsquo ; s decision to levy every property owner in major urban centres with a tax notice , and then force them to apply for exemptions , is a backwards way to implement a misguided tax , said Liberal MLA Shirley Bond . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Just when you think a botched tax can&amp;rsquo ; t get any worse , it does , &amp;rdquo ; said Bond . &amp;ldquo ; Now&amp;nbsp ; residents in communities like Nanaimo , Kelowna and Abbotsford , to name a few , have to register to be exempt from this tax or they will get a bill in the mail for a tax they shouldn&amp;rsquo ; t be paying . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It is absolutely astounding to me for an conceived tax that does not target legitimate speculators &amp;mdash ; the majority of people impacted by this tax are hard-working British Columbians . On every front , this is a mess . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The NDP government&amp;rsquo ; s speculation tax was announced in the February 2018 budget and described as a way to encourage owners of empty residences to put them onto the rental market , or sell , in areas where housing is unaffordable and rental vacancy rates are low . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although first billed as a surcharge on out-of-province real estate speculators , it was later revealed that &lt;a href= " https : **33;34920;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;an estimated two-thirds&lt;/a&gt; of those who will pay the tax will be British Columbians. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Public blowback forced Finance Minister Carole James to exempt areas like the Gulf Islands , as well as water down the rate before the tax was passed into law in the fall . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Because the tax is retroactive , the first letters will actually be for taxes incurred in the 2018 calendar year &amp;mdash ; before the tax rules were even finalized. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;PODCAST : Postmedia columnists Mike Smyth and Rob Shaw start 2019 by examining critical issues and predicting possible outcomes for the year ahead . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is former Premier Christy Clark ready for politics after time away ? Will rideshare giants Uber and Lyft drive away from B.C. after encountering a number of roadblocks ? Can federal NDP leader Jagmeet Singh actually win a seat in Burnaby ? Smyth and Shaw explore these topics and more in their expansive discussion about what 's to come in 2019. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; audio src= " http : **35;34955;TOOLONG ... / &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; The tax rate is 0.5 per cent of a home&amp;rsquo ; s assessed value in 2018 , which would be $5,000 a year for a property assessed at $1 million . Starting in 2019 , the rate rises to two per cent for out-of-province owners , foreigners and satellite families ( households where more than 50 per cent of income comes from outside Canada ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Owners are &lt;a href= " https : **33;34992;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;exempt from the tax&lt;/a&gt; if it is their principal residence , they rent it at least six months of the year ( only three months is required in 2018 ) , they are disabled , the property was just inherited , it&amp;rsquo ; s valued under $150,000 , or a person was away and it was vacant due to medical reasons , residential care , work or spousal separation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Condos and apartments in buildings where stratas ban rentals are also exempt , but only for 2018 and 2019 , to give stratas time to change their bylaws , according to the ministry . Exemptions are available for First Nations , local governments , charities , co-ops , some not-for-profit organizations , and &lt;a href= " https : **33;35027;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt; developers&lt;/a&gt; &amp;nbsp ; working on construction or renovation of property . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; British Columbians with second homes who aren&amp;rsquo ; t exempt will still get a credit intended to cover the tax on the assessed value up to $400,000 , with the remaining value of the property then taxed&amp;nbsp ; at the full rate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **26;35062;TOOLONG " **33;35090;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **27;35125;TOOLONG " &gt;t ... &lt;/p&gt; Rob ShawWed , 16 Jan 2019 17:41:39 **45;35154;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; **25;35201;TOOLONG of an ambitious master plan , Onni Group 's Cambie Gardens will revolutionize the neighbourhoodhttp : **27;35228;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;35257;TOOLONG ... title= " An artist 's rendering anticipates the future of Cambie Gardens . In the kitchens these schemes have dual-tone cabinets&amp;mdash ; white satin-painted flat-panel cabinets with wood accents in a choice of light oak or dark walnut cabinets with corresponding brushed oak floors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both these design options feature marble countertops and backsplashes and the same stone is on the islands , with their elegant waterfall edges . The islands accommodate a stainless steel undermount sink and a Dornbracht faucet with a pullout hand spray , and also have an overhang that creates room for a few bar-height stools for casual dining . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Heather , the third palette , has satin-painted soft grey flat-panelled cabinets .
