Construction of Cambie Gardens is expected to start in early 2019. &lt;/p&gt; Kathleen FreimondSat , 24 Nov 2018 14:10:13 **45;35455;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosWestcoast Homes and Design50 Electronic Avenue speaks to its Port Moody locationhttp : **32;35502;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;35536;TOOLONG ... title= " 50 Electronic Avenue is a project from the Panatch Group in Port Moody . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " 50 Electronic Avenue is a project from the Panatch Group in Port Moody . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " Vincent Klassen " **28;35572;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 881772 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Twenty years ago , when Kush Panatch bought a 3.5-acre parcel of industrial land opposite Port Moody 's downtown , he knew it might take a long time for it to appreciate in value . What he had n't anticipated was the new SkyTrain Evergreen extension or the nearby Westcoast Express station would make it excellent space for a 358-unit condominium development . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " I remember coming here . I went down and walked around the park . It was simply a big industrial area with a sawmill , " said Panatch , a first-generation Canadian whose family fled Africa for a better life in Canada . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Called 50 Electronic Avenue , the two six-storey buildings are part of a transformation the former industrial area has been undergoing over the last few years , with some of Metro Vancouver 's best-known developers investing heavily in new projects there . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Panatch 's family business , the Panatch Group , is fuelling that urban renewal with an offer to help young families get started in real estate by offering a rent-to-own scheme for some 30 homes in the complex . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;The plan , developed after some Port Moody council members expressed concern about the lack of affordable housing in the neighbourhood , provides below-market rentals for qualifying families . Panatch said those families are given the option two years later to buy the home by applying the money they have saved on the discounted rent and receiving credit for all rent payments they have made . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;That offer is just one of several ways the development demonstrates its community spirit . The homes share a 9,000-square-foot amenity building with everything from a doggy wash to separate areas for adults , teenagers and children 's activities . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;The project , designed by Rob Ciccozzi and his firm Ciccozzi Architecture Inc. , works hard to engage with the community and to " activate " street life on both Murray Avenue and Klahanie Drive which the two buildings border , Ciccozzi said . The east building is entirely residential , while the west building has commercial and retail space on the ground floor . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " I can see a coffee place and a little deli , the kind of commercial spaces that would activate the street , " said Ciccozzi . " We want to encourage the public to walk up and down the streets . You ca n't do that without having the critical mass ( of residents. ) " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;To encourage that interaction , Ciccozzi broke down the massing of the two buildings with a walkway between the two , complete with a water feature . There are exterior details , such as glass railings , balconies up to the fourth floor and a parapet for top levels that makes the building heights a little less imposing . The materials used -- red brick , steel and rough timbers -- also make the buildings attractive , while paying homage to the industrial history of the rapidly evolving neighbourhood , where a brewer 's row and accompanying restaurants have popped up over the last few years . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;The building interiors were also designed to combine traditional elements with modern innovations to make family living more convenient and practical , say Cheryl Broadhead and Nicole Duval of BYU Interiors . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " We had a lot of conversations at the beginning with the developer about how to build community into the project , making sure that neighbours knew neighbours , and that they had comfortable homes that had character that they were proud of and that really worked for them , " said Broadhead. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; " People have space within their units that can really house their belongings or display them if they want , " she said . " Family values really seem to come through . It 's a joy to work with a developer that is like that . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;A tour of the two show homes -- a two-bedroom unit with a rooftop patio and a one-bedroom unit -- illustrate how the firm maximized the use of space . The homes make liberal use of pantry cupboards extending into the space adjacent to the kitchens . The two-bedroom home features his-and-hers closets and drawers in a corridor leading to a spacious ensuite bathroom , leaving the bedroom free of a space-hogging chest of drawers or other furniture . Medicine cabinets are incorporated into all bathrooms , as are the accompanying niches for storage and design appeal around the mirrors . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35602;TOOLONG ... title= " 50 Electronic Avenue is a project from the Panatch Group in Port Moody . In the kitchen , the team made use of subway tiles at counter level , a throwback to another era , as a complement to the clean lines and background colours of the cabinetry. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;The two-bedroom model also shows off the convenient staircase that leads to a spacious , tiled private roof garden on top of the buildings . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;BYU was also responsible for planning the use of Club 50 , the three-storey shared amenity building , which allocates a separate area for children attached to an outside playground , a video arcade for teens and a lounge area for adults . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;The amenity also has a fitness facility , a yoga studio , a dog-wash room , a bicycle repair room , a media room and co-working space with two boardrooms . Outside , there is a one-acre elevated private backyard that includes a dog park and children 's playground , green lawns and quiet courtyards . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35668;TOOLONG ... title= " 50 Electronic Avenue is a project from the Panatch Group in Port Moody . &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt; &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Michael BernardSat , 20 Oct 2018 13:09:04 **45;35863;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosDouglas Todd : Condos changed Metro Vancouver forever , for better and worsehttp : **35;35910;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;35947;TOOLONG ... title= " Condofication has caused a revolution in urban history -- and no more so than in Metro Vancouver . " alt= " Condofication has caused a revolution in urban history -- and no more so than in Metro Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " DARRYL DYCK " **28;35983;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 882742 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;36013;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was the year the Beatles released Hey Jude , Pierre Trudeau was elected prime minister , and &lt;a href= " http : **34;36049;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Rev . Martin Luther King&lt;/a&gt; was assassinated . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was 1968 &amp;mdash ; the year that Metro Vancouver got its first condominium complex . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A great deal has changed in the ensuing 50 years . Now roughly one out of three residents of the region live in a condominium . Metro Vancouver has about 600,000 condo units , with the city of Vancouver having 130,000 of those . The province in total has about 900,000 units , according to the Condominium Homeowners Assoc. of B.C. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;36085;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Metro Vancouver&lt;/a&gt; could be the poster city illustrating the results of the legal changes half a century ago that made condominiums possible . A strong case has been made that the &amp;ldquo ; condofication&amp;rdquo ; of Metro Vancouver has done more than anything else to define this West Coast city . It&amp;rsquo ; s come with some benefits , including for many owners , but the deeper structural effects have not all been pretty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The condo metamorphosis has led to increased density , higher land prices , more vacant dwellings , more vertical neighbourhoods , fewer purpose-build rental units , more investor owners ( domestic and offshore ) and discord between different kinds of condo owners and users . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The condo boom has arguably provided many people who could not afford a detached property the chance to own a less-costly dwelling through a strata title , without directly owning a private chunk of land . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; https : //www.youtube.com/watch ? v=4QnpN3laWjc &a; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And it&amp;rsquo ; s clear the property laws that made condominiums possible increased the density and shape of the city and its neighbourhoods &amp;mdash ; bringing far more tower clusters and making land in general more expensive . The vast majority of Metro&amp;rsquo ; s&amp;nbsp ; &lt;a href= " http : **34;36120;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;more than 1,300 highrise residential towers&lt;/a&gt; are condominium buildings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But the expansion of condominiums has also , many say , discouraged the construction of purpose-built rental buildings . Developers generally prefer constructing condo complexes , since they can sell the units right away , or in advance , and realize their profits quickly . They don&amp;rsquo ; t have to patiently wait years to collect their profits in rents . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the most intriguing pieces ever written about the condofication of Metro Vancouver , and other parts of North America , is by &lt;a href= " http : **34;36156;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Douglas Harris&lt;/a&gt; , a specialist in legal history and property law at the University of B.C. His article is titled Condominium and the City : The Rise of Property in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the many points Harris makes is that the laws that made condominiums possible in Metro Vancouver contributed to the high-end gentrification of the city , and to some extent the squeezing out of low-income residents . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36192;TOOLONG ... title= " The condo phenomenon ignited in the late 1980s after the Social Credit government shocked many by selling the Expo 86 lands to one of Asia 's most wealthy real-estate developers . ( Photo : Cabinet minister Grace McCarthy , who spearheaded the sale. ) " alt= " The condo phenomenon ignited in the late 1980s after the Social Credit government shocked many by selling the Expo 86 lands to one of Asia 's most wealthy real-estate developers . ( Photo : Cabinet minister Grace McCarthy , who spearheaded the sale. ) " data-portal-copyright= " Colin Price " **28;36228;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 611486 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Much of the explanation for the higher prices in the last two decades lies in the influx of overseas capital and migrants , &amp;rdquo ; Harris writes . &amp;ldquo ; Enormous economic expansion in East Asia coupled with moments of political uncertainty and instability brought people and capital to North America and its west coast in particular . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The condo phenomenon especially ignited in the late 1980s , Harris says , after the Social Credit government shocked many British Columbians by &lt;a href= " http : **34;36258;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;selling Vancouver 's former Expo 86&lt;/a&gt; lands to one of Asia&amp;rsquo ; s most wealthy businessmen , real-estate developer Li Ka-shing of Hong Kong . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; People from Hong Kong , Taiwan and China became particularly active in the skyrocketing condominium market in Metro Vancouver . &amp;ldquo ; Since the 1990s , Vancouver has had the highest residential housing prices in the country , &amp;rdquo ; with condominium law providing the mechanism that &amp;ldquo ; expanded the market for land , embedded the city in international flows of people and capital , and , by doing so , contributed to the rising prices . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the mushrooming of condominium units has made it possible for many residents to buy into housing options other than single-family dwellings , it&amp;rsquo ; s also led to conflicts between those who own them , live in them , rent them and leave them empty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s hard to obtain hard province-wide figures on the number of investor-owned apartments . But one reliable report to Vancouver City council , based on B.C. Assessment data , found 35 per cent of condo units in the city were owned by investors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;a name= " pda10140631 " &gt;&lt;/a&gt; &lt;div class= " PDSPoll " id= " PDIcontainer10140631 " style= " display:inline-block ; " &gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id= " PDsuperContainer " &gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;script type= " text/javascript " charset= " UTF-8 " src= " https : **33;36294;TOOLONG ... &lt;noscript&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **29;36329;TOOLONG " &gt; ... Our **31;36360;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tony Gioventu , executive director of the Condominium Homeowners Association of B.C. , said it&amp;rsquo ; s reasonable to estimate 35 to 40 per cent of the province&amp;rsquo ; s strata units are investor owned . &lt;span style= " color:black ; font-family:Calibri , Helvetica , sans-serif ; font-size:medium ; " &gt;&lt;span dir= " ltr " &gt;&lt;span style= " color:black ; " &gt;&lt;span lang= " en-CA " **34;36393;TOOLONG ... would mean more than 300,000 apartments are not lived in by those who bought them ( whether using money made in a Canada or transferred from foreign lands ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The majority of investor-owned units are rented out , said Gioventu , especially since 2010 , when the B.C. Liberals changed the law to allow developers to designate most new units as rentable for 100 years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another result of investment and speculation is that many units remain empty , even in the midst of Metro&amp;rsquo ; s housing and rental affordability crisis . There are hundreds of condo towers in Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s downtown core , and Gioventu estimated 25 to 35 per cent of their units are vacant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That is just one of many factors that have led to condominium stresses : Between people who live in their apartments , those who rent them , speculators and recreational-property owners who leave them empty and investors who turn them into businesses through Airbnb. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;36429;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " autoplay ; encrypted-media " **34;36465;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; One of the most notorious conflicts involving investor-owners and owner-occupiers was to go this week before B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s Human Rights Tribunal , but was settled out of court Monday with a non-disclosure agreement . It centred on condominium owner Andreas Kargut&amp;rsquo ; s protest against Mandarin-language-only strata council meetings at his Richmond complex . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But it&amp;rsquo ; s not hard to find other forms of discord between investors and owner-occupiers . The condominium rental study for Vancouver City council discovered complaints absentee owners were &amp;ldquo ; less willing to spend on additional or unforeseen maintenance&amp;rdquo ; and were negligent at screening renters , some of whom crowd into small units . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While generally defending the many renters in condominiums ( including a large portion of the &lt;a href= " http : **34;36501;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;more than 100,000 international students in Metro Vancouver&lt;/a&gt; ) , Gioventu said serious problems , if not horror shows , are arising because some investors are hawking their units as short-term Airbnb rentals . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Too many condo owners don&amp;rsquo ; t realize they&amp;rsquo ; re liable for all damages , complaints and illegal activities related to renters , including those who sub-rent through Airbnb , Gioventu said . Not only do some Airbnb &amp;ldquo ; guests&amp;rdquo ; cause noise problems , flood bathtubs and use their units for illicit purposes , a portion take advantage of their condo security access to break into storage lockers and the units of others . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s an understatement to say condominiums have been a game changer in North America . Condofication has caused a revolution in urban history &amp;mdash ; and no more so than in Metro Vancouver . The story of condos is only beginning , and their consequences will continue to unfold in far-reaching ways . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **26;36537;TOOLONG " **54;36565;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **25;36621;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;36648;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;a name= " pda10140632 " &gt;&lt;/a&gt; &lt;div class= " PDSPoll " id= " PDIcontainer10140632 " style= " display:inline-block ; " &gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id= " PDsuperContainer " &gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;script type= " text/javascript " charset= " UTF-8 " src= " https : **33;36676;TOOLONG ... &lt;noscript&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **29;36711;TOOLONG " &gt; ... Our **31;36742;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;36775;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;36817;TOOLONG ... Todd : The ups and downs of highrise **42;36853;TOOLONG href= " http : **34;36897;TOOLONG ... housing market squeezed by foreign students and other temporary **45;36933;TOOLONG href= " http : **34;36980;TOOLONG ... politicians for Metro 's housing crisis : UBC **42;37016;TOOLONG &lt;hr&gt; &lt;h2&gt;You might also be interested in ... &lt;/h2&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;37060;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " autoplay ; encrypted-media " **34;37095;TOOLONG Douglas ToddThu , 18 Oct 2018 18:06:43 **45;37131;TOOLONG ... NewsPoliticsStaff BlogsDouglas Todd : The ups and downs of highrise livinghttp : **34;37178;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;37214;TOOLONG ... title= " " You never talk to your neighbours . I only see them once a week or less , " says Nelson Lee , who lives with his two adult children in an 800 square-foot apartment on the 25th floor of building in Vancouver 's Coal Harbour neighbourhood . " alt= " " You never talk to your neighbours . I only see them once a week or less , " says Nelson Lee , who lives with his two adult children in an 800 square-foot apartment on the 25th floor of building in Vancouver 's Coal Harbour neighbourhood . " data-portal-copyright= " Jason Payne " **28;37250;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 876277 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rob McDowell thought a condo on the 29th floor of a Yaletown highrise would be the route to contentment. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It had a beautiful view of Burrard Inlet . It had everything I wanted . But I soon realized I felt like I was in a gilded cage . I saw people in the elevator , but that was about it . I realized I wanted to be around people more . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So two years after he bought into the tower at 501 Pacific Boulevard , McDowell moved into a unit that opened onto that building&amp;rsquo ; s leafy courtyard . The &amp;ldquo ; accidental meetings&amp;rdquo ; among families that occur daily in the courtyard have led to lasting friendships. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; McDowell&amp;rsquo ; s story offers one perspective on the psychological risks and benefits of residential highrise living . But there are hundreds of thousands of stories from people in Metro Vancouver who live in towers , and Nelson Lee offers another one of them &amp;mdash ; with decided cons and pros . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In general , I love being in downtown Vancouver . I&amp;rsquo ; ve lived in towers in Taiwan and Hong Kong and I love the freedom . But you never talk to your neighbours . I only see them once a week or less . And it&amp;rsquo ; s usually just a polite hello and goodbye , &amp;rdquo ; said Lee , who lives with his two adult children on the 25th floor of a Coal Harbour tower . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lee appreciates towers for the way they house a lot of people on a small footprint of land . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ve got three people living in 800 square feet and we never turn on the heat , so that helps a little in the battle against climate change , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Defenders of highrise living say the units cost less to buy and rent than ground-level dwellings , create density that increases the range of nearby shops and transit options and , of course , often offer panoramic vistas. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , people skeptical of the rapid expansion of residential highrises , especially in Metro Vancouver and Toronto , maintain they tend to breed loneliness . They&amp;rsquo ; re not great for most children , according to research . Critics also report some Metro Vancouver towers have many empty units &amp;mdash ; and that some of the monoliths create hard , unfriendly streetscapes. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both sides of the highrise debate can cite evidence to make their cases . So it&amp;rsquo ; s hard to come to a simple conclusion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2&gt;Metro has 1,300-plus highrises&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;p&gt; Residential towers have become impossible to ignore in Metro Vancouver , where some new ones soar a dizzying 60 storeys and higher . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A city official said Vancouver proper has more than 1,064 residential highrises ( typically defined as buildings of more than five storeys ) . Burnaby has 193 . The City of North Vancouver has 78 . Surrey has 28 . Richmond doesn&amp;rsquo ; t have a count on its many towers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile it&amp;rsquo ; s virtually impossible to find a politician running in the civic elections who is promising to build more of them . Candidates are instead campaigning for the need to add &amp;ldquo ; gentle density ; &amp;rdquo ; buildings for those who can&amp;rsquo ; t afford a detached dwelling and don&amp;rsquo ; t want to raise kids at lofty heights . That&amp;rsquo ; s why we&amp;rsquo ; re mostly hearing about the need for row houses , duplexes and apartment buildings five storeys and under . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It turns out Metro Vancouver , where politicians are devising a &amp;ldquo ; livable region strategic plan&amp;rdquo ; plan for 2040 , does not even keep a tally on the number of highrises . Data gaps like that make it hard to figure out what one thinks of 2016 census results showing &lt;a href= " http : **34;37280;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Metro Vancouver is home to four of the 10 most densely populated municipalities in Canada&lt;/a&gt; : They are New Westminster , White Rock and North Vancouver City , with Vancouver proper topping the country . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The rooftop cranes dotting the skyline signify more highrises are coming . Michael Ferreira , principal of Urban Analytics , said 237 more highrises are slated for Metro Vancouver , bringing 93,000 new dwelling units . They&amp;rsquo ; re going up in Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s downtown , Burnaby&amp;rsquo ; s Metrotown and Brentwood neighbourhoods , North Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Lonsdale , Richmond Centre , Surrey Metro Centre , Coquitlam&amp;rsquo ; s core and elsewhere . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While construction soars , people argue not only over what towers are doing to neighbourhoods and traffic , but about their design . Some complain too many are massive and hard-edged , while others appreciate some seem to be more livable , and may be contributing to community life . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2&gt;Psychology of highrise living&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;p&gt; What do people who live in towers think about the high life ? Feelings are all over the urban map . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Despite enjoying the shops , restaurants and boardwalks of Coal Harbour , Nelson Lee said the downside is there are many empty units in his tower and many Airbnb guests , whose suitcases frequently fill the lobby . In addition , a 47-storey tower is being built directly outside Lee&amp;rsquo ; s window and he laments how , since he works out of his home , he&amp;rsquo ; s going to have to listen to construction for three years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s former chief city planner , Brent Toderian , chooses to live in the &amp;ldquo ; mid-rise &amp;ldquo ; level of his downtown tower , which overlooks both the courtyard and the street . &amp;ldquo ; I often refer to myself as a ground person , &amp;rdquo ; Toderian says . But he&amp;rsquo ; s all for choice , including for residents who want to hover way above terra firma. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37316;TOOLONG ... title= " Simon Lin lives in a Brentwood highrise in Burnaby , which has views of Metrotown in the background . " alt= " Simon Lin lives in a Brentwood highrise in Burnaby , which has views of Metrotown in the background . " data-portal-copyright= " Jason Payne " **28;37352;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 876278 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Simon Lin , who arrived a year ago from Taiwan , says it&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;ldquo ; so far so good&amp;rdquo ; in the 12th floor two-bedroom unit he bought for his wife and college-student daughter in one of the many new towers popping up at Brentwood in Burnaby. &amp;nbsp ; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a good view . And it&amp;rsquo ; s not as crowded here as in Taipei , &amp;rdquo ; said the 54-year-old retired businessman as he ate a curry salad on a patio outside Whole Foods Market . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; William Wu , a 20-year-old student from China , said he found his unit in a 40-storey tower at the east end of Burnaby&amp;rsquo ; s Metrotown through a &amp;ldquo ; Chinese people&amp;rsquo ; s real estate website. &amp;rdquo ; He&amp;rsquo ; s not lonely . Smoking a cigarette near the entrance , Wu said he pays his Mainland Chinese landlord $1,800 a month while he hangs out , including in the indoor gym , with other international-student friends who live in the building . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The opinions of people who live or work near Metro Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s expanding new highrise clusters are also mixed , but lean more to the negative . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m not a fan . I think it&amp;rsquo ; s better to have a bit of space and greenery , &amp;rdquo ; said Vancouverite Sandra Freeman , who once lived in a highrise because it was less expensive . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jeanne Bridge , who works next to new towers around Brentwood mall , said the ceaseless construction has brought a new supermarket to the neighbourhood but also led to the death of some favourite coffee shops . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;37382;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Charles Montgomery , author of Happy City : &lt;/a&gt; Transforming Our Lives Through Urban Design , says he&amp;rsquo ; s not against highrises . But he advocates smaller-scale housing ; especially &amp;ldquo ; co-housing&amp;rdquo ; clusters of 12 family units facing a shared courtyard . Psychological studies of highrise towers , Montgomery said , repeatedly show many residents end up experiencing an emotional paradox : &amp;ldquo ; They feel lonely and crowded at the same time . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Karn Bains , 31 , has his own insights on such alienation . As he enjoyed a coffee break at his regular spot on a concrete bench at the quiet base of several luxury condo towers at Nelson and Jubilee in Burnaby , Bains said , &amp;ldquo ; I never see people walking here , or kids playing . All I ever see is cars coming out of the underground parking lots , which is kind of weird . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2&gt;Psychological studies of high rises&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;p&gt; The University of Victoria&amp;rsquo ; s Robert Gifford is one of the world&amp;rsquo ; s leading experts on living in high rises . The specialist in psychology and environmental design published a groundbreaking analysis in 2007 of more than 100 studies on whether highrises improve or diminish well-being . It&amp;rsquo ; s gone viral . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Many studies showed people are more depressed in highrises , that mental illness increased at higher floor levels , that more crowding reduced the amount of neighbour support , that tower residents worried more about fire and contagious disease , that one&amp;rsquo ; s sense of community attachment is reduced and that , overall , people were less satisfied . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gifford is the first to acknowledge many older studies were skewed because they focused on low-income highrises in the U.S. and Britain . Since then many new skyscrapers have been marketed to wealthy people . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nevertheless , Gifford&amp;rsquo ; s survey helped him come to some broad conclusions , including that the upper floors of towers are generally not good for children . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Children are better off in low-rise housing . Highrises either restrict their outdoor activity or leave them relatively unsupervised outdoors , which may be why children who live in highrises have , on average , more behaviour problems , &amp;rdquo ; Gifford writes in &lt;a href= " http : **33;37418;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The Consequences of Living in Highrise Buildings . &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Research is also unanimous in finding that rates of helping others are lower in highrise buildings . The nature of most highrises supports anonymity and depersonalization . Residents of highrises encounter many more other residents &amp;hellip ; but have fewer friendships in the building per capita than residents of low rises . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gifford emphasizes , however , that residential towers can work for some people . &amp;ldquo ; Evidence suggests residents in a high rise will be happier if they are not parents of small children , do &lt;em&gt;not&lt;/em&gt; &amp;nbsp ; plan to stay long and are socially competent . &amp;hellip ; Highrises are more satisfactory for residents when they are more expensive , located in better neighbourhoods and residents chose to live in them . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;37453;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;37495;TOOLONG ... Todd : Why are Toronto and Vancouver so unhappy ? **36;37531;TOOLONG href= " http : **34;37569;TOOLONG ... Todd : Metro Vancouver 's ranking for ' hygge ' dropping **40;37605;TOOLONG href= " http : **34;37647;TOOLONG ... Todd : Vancouver 's rental and housing markets squeezed by temporary **41;37683;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Like other urban specialists such as Montgomery and Toderian , Gifford says the success of highrises depends a great deal on how they&amp;rsquo ; re designed . Metro has examples of towers that encourage connection , say urbanists . But the region also has more than its share of ugly , Stalinesque highrises that kill residents&amp;rsquo ; satisfaction and enjoyment of the neighbourhood by passersby. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Melbourne , Australia , provides a parallel to Metro . Ranked among the most livable cities in the world , Melbourne is also a gateway city that has been hosting a wave of transnational newcomers and investors , which politicians and developers have responded to with an explosion of highrises. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of Australia&amp;rsquo ; s most acclaimed architects , Kerry Clare , warns sky-high living is harming Australia&amp;rsquo ; s urban fabric , creating &amp;ldquo ; physical and psychological silos&amp;rdquo ; and causing more people to be &amp;ldquo ; detached from street life . &amp;rdquo ; Towers , she said , are largely &amp;ldquo ; built during economic bubbles&amp;rdquo ; and many are empty , investor-owned properties . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her complaint jibes with analysis of the tower booms in Vancouver and Toronto . SFU planner Andy Yan and Stephen Punwasi , of Better Dwelling , have created maps that show Metro &lt;a href= " https : **33;37726;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;neighbourhoods with the most unoccupied dwelling units&lt;/a&gt; frequently align with those with the most towers . Highrise clusters at Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Coal Harbour and Joyce SkyTrain Station and Burnaby&amp;rsquo ; s Metrotown are more than 20-per-cent unoccupied . The highrises of Yaletown and Richmond Centre are 15 to 20 per cent empty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s not conducive to community connection . As Montgomery , the author of Happy City , walked among the scores of soaring towers of Yaletown , he talked about a &lt;a href= " http : **34;37761;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vancouver Foundation survey&lt;/a&gt; finding that residents of towers were &amp;ldquo ; half as likely to have done a favour for a neighbour&amp;rdquo ; and more likely to report having trouble making friends . &amp;ldquo ; People living in towers consistently reported feeling more lonely and less connected than people living in detached homes . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The problem of low trust correlates with how &amp;ldquo ; vertical neighbourhoods tend to be home to the city&amp;rsquo ; s most transient residents : foreign students , young people and renters who do not stay long enough to forge deep ties , &amp;rdquo ; he writes in Happy City . There are &lt;a href= " http : **34;37797;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;more than 100,000 international students in Metro&lt;/a&gt; , one of the highest rates per capita in the world . &amp;ldquo ; Crowding , &amp;rdquo ; Montgomery said , &amp;ldquo ; while pushing us together physically , actually pushes us apart socially . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2&gt;High rises and their options&lt;/h2&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since high rises remain attractive to developers , investors and some residents , urban planners urge designing them more thoughtfully . Too many buildings , critics say , are ruthlessly bare-bone , with few design elements that foster human connection . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The possibilities to make them more human-friendly are endless , but often ignored . Many architects say highrise towers need more lush rooftop gardens , gyms and playrooms , scattered among the floors . &amp;ldquo ; After all , kids can&amp;rsquo ; t play in the hallways , &amp;rdquo ; said McDowell . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As Montgomery strolled the streets of Yaletown , he pointed favourably to highrises where he believes the architects made an effort to help residents have vibrant daily experiences . Their ground levels were not walls of grim concrete , but a &amp;ldquo ; soft&amp;rdquo ; streetscape featuring small shops , restaurants , gardens , greenery , supermarkets and household doorsteps. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Montgomery and Toderian are among the urbanists who praise some of the City of Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s signature &amp;ldquo ; podium and point tower&amp;rdquo ; high rises , which feature a low-rise&amp;nbsp ; apartment building abutting the sidewalk to psychologically soften the massiveness of the high tower behind it . It&amp;rsquo ; s called &amp;ldquo ; the Vancouver model . &amp;rdquo ; However , Montgomery has had depressing experiences among many highrise clusters in Metro , where he often notices blank walls at the base of residential towers . They create a &amp;ldquo ; hard&amp;rdquo ; street atmosphere , and alienation. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even though Metro Vancouver is known for its dramatic shift to highrises , public discussion remains muted . Despite being in the midst of civic elections , Yan notes , &amp;ldquo ; Nobody in the campaign has said , &amp;lsquo ; Let&amp;rsquo ; s put up more towers . ' &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While towers are &amp;ldquo ; highly efficient investment vehicles , &amp;rdquo ; Yan said &amp;ldquo ; they&amp;rsquo ; re not a panacea&amp;rdquo ; for a region in which one of the biggest challenge of families and businesses is &amp;ldquo ; finding a way to keep people in their mid 30s. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Many alternatives to towers , especially for young families , are not often being tried in Vancouver or North America . Melbourne planner Michael Buxton is one tower critic who values how his city is finally starting to build more six-storey apartments . &amp;ldquo ; Substantial increases in urban density don&amp;rsquo ; t require highrise buildings . Many of the world&amp;rsquo ; s densest cities are located in Europe and the Middle East with uniform building heights between three to seven storeys , &amp;rdquo ; Buxton said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Likewise , Yan tends to support those who are calling for more lowrise housing for the &amp;ldquo ; missing middle . &amp;rdquo ; Let&amp;rsquo ; s not forget , the planner said , that most people in New York City don&amp;rsquo ; t live in highrises in Manhattan , but in low-rises in the Bronx and Queens . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The debate over the pros and cons of highrise living , to Yan , is part of a crucial larger one for the city . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Who are we building for ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **26;37833;TOOLONG " **33;37861;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **25;37896;TOOLONG " **26;37923;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Douglas ToddSat , 06 Oct 2018 16:38:38 **45;37951;TOOLONG ... NewsRelationshipsStaff BlogsWestcoast Homes and DesignConcord Pacific 's amenity-rich Park George will be big on smart designhttp : **34;37998;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;38034;TOOLONG ... title= " Park George is a project from Concord Pacific in Surrey . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Park George is a project from Concord Pacific in Surrey . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38070;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 871496 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are not many places where a new condo comes with access to two private swimming pools , an outdoor hot tub theatre , a tennis court , a work lounge , a sports social lounge , a virtual spin studio and a video conferencing room . Park George , the latest offering from Concord Pacific in Surrey , is one such place . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Part of the reason for the extraordinary range of amenities on offer at Park George is because residents will be able to make use of the facilities that are already in place at Park Avenue , two of Concord Pacific&amp;rsquo ; s previous residential towers nearby . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We call our developments master-planned communities because we try to share the amenities across a number of towers &amp;ndash ; in this case four &amp;ndash ; so that it keeps people&amp;rsquo ; s strata fees down , &amp;rdquo ; explained Grant Murray , senior vice-president of sales at Concord Pacific . &amp;ldquo ; In total , there will be about 110,000 square feet of amenity space , 30,000 square feet of it indoors . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You simply won&amp;rsquo ; t find the same range of amenities in a single residential tower , or even two towers , &amp;rdquo ; Murray added . &amp;ldquo ; And you certainly won&amp;rsquo ; t find them in a location that is so well connected in terms of transit . It&amp;rsquo ; s really a big selling point for us. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Park George&amp;rsquo ; s two towers of 36 and 39 storeys will include 339 homes in total . The neighbourhood around the development has been a decade in the making , when the City of Surrey came up with a long-term plan to transform the city centre into a new urban core . This involved adding multiple highrise residential buildings , retail offerings and amenities , a new location for Surrey City Hall , the expansion of Surrey Memorial Hospital , and a new RCMP headquarters . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Then you&amp;rsquo ; ve also got the extra rapid transit that will be coming to Surrey in the near future , extending the transit options south down to Newton , &amp;rdquo ; Murray said . &amp;ldquo ; We recognized 10 years ago that Surrey was really going to explode in popularity as somewhere people want to live and work . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Park George is going to be dead centre in all of this new development and it&amp;rsquo ; s also right next to two parks &amp;ndash ; Holland Park and Quibble Creek , &amp;rdquo ; Murray added . &amp;ldquo ; That means you&amp;rsquo ; ll never have anything in front of you and there are excellent view corridors . This is because Quibble Creek is a riparian park that is designated as protected green space . It gives you a lovely suburban feel in an urban setting . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Murray explained that the City of Surrey is aiming to transform , not just its residential environment , but its economic one as well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Everything&amp;rsquo ; s been gearing up to make this a new urban area and they&amp;rsquo ; re hoping to attract high-tech companies to the city in particular , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The City of Surrey&amp;rsquo ; s website talks extensively about its aspirations to become a &amp;ldquo ; Smart City&amp;rdquo ; , which aims to create &amp;ldquo ; sustainable economic development and a high quality of life for&amp;hellip ; residents by considering innovation and technological advancements as a key ingredient in&amp;hellip ; decision making , strategy and investment . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Concord Pacific and Park George , in particular , are well set up to contribute to this vision , according to Murray . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; These are the first towers in Surrey that will have 100-per-cent electric vehicle parking , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; They also have a keyless entry and the WiFi connection will be fantastic so you&amp;rsquo ; ll be able to walk around anywhere in the building and be connected , including in the elevators . We&amp;rsquo ; re really big on technology and that&amp;rsquo ; s why we&amp;rsquo ; re installing smart thermostats in every suite so you&amp;rsquo ; ll be able to set the temperature in your home while you&amp;rsquo ; re away . With all of these smart ideas , we&amp;rsquo ; re really catering to that generation that does everything with their phone . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kitchens at Park George include Panasonic microwaves and Bosch appliance packages with wall ovens , four-burner gas cooktops , stainless steel hood fans and built-in fridge-freezers . Bathrooms have Grohe fixtures , Kohler sinks and built-in shower niches . There are two colour palettes to choose from &amp;ndash ; Sunrise Chiffon and Evening Silk &amp;ndash ; and suites also come with Blomberg washers and dryers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Murray expects the amenities at Park George to be a key selling point when sales open this month . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Because the density in the area is so high , you&amp;rsquo ; re generating enough money to be able to put in a huge amount of amenity space , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; The amenities across the four towers really complement each other so there&amp;rsquo ; s always something on offer no matter what you&amp;rsquo ; re into or what the weather is doing at any given time of the year . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Homes at Park George will range in size between 573 and 2,056 square feet . Prices for one-bedroom homes start at $379,900 , at $540,000 for two-bedroom homes and $899,900 for three-bedroom homes . Completion is expected for mid 2022. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s just an ideal location for a master-planned community , &amp;rdquo ; Murray said . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s been a fantastic ride over the last 10 years and these are the last two towers in the area so we&amp;rsquo ; re expecting lots of interest . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Park George&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project location : &lt;/strong&gt; 13768 &amp;mdash ; 100 Avenue , Surrey &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project size : &lt;/strong&gt; 339 homes ranging in size between 573 and 2,056 square feet . Prices for one-bedroom homes start at $379,900 , at $540,000 for two-bedroom homes and $899,900 for three-bedroom homes &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Developer : &lt;/strong&gt; Concord Pacific &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Architects can sometimes run a gauntlet when they are designing residential buildings , trying to create an interesting looking structure from the outside without running afoul of design guidelines or compromising the interior space . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That is the challenge that Andrew Emmerson and GBL Architects faced when they were asked to design Winona on Cambie , a new 38-unit building along the Cambie Corridor . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We wanted to create some energy and movement and cadence to the exterior expression of the building , &amp;rdquo ; Emmerson said . &amp;rdquo ; So we worked with the idea of a flux expression along the fa&amp;ccedil ; ade , &amp;rdquo ; which reflects the flow of traffic on a busy Cambie Street . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The result is a kind of interweaving of balconies and floor orientation vertically and horizontally with the building , which will rise over the next two years at West 61 **25;38246;TOOLONG &amp;nbsp ; Avenue and Cambie , adjacent to Winona Park . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; A lot of credit goes to the client , &amp;rdquo ; the architect noted . &amp;ldquo ; When he came to us , he wanted to do something different and challenged us with the design task of breaking out of the mould . He was fairly hands off during the design process and gave us a free rein . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Along the way , the building&amp;rsquo ; s form was designed to optimize sunlight inside the homes while providing shade from the summer sun , he said . As well , stretching out the balconies along the building&amp;rsquo ; s west side facing Cambie served to dampen the sound of traffic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Emmerson said the client also wanted to have a mix of homeowners who reflected the area , whether they are first-time buyers or people moving out of larger single-family homes in the area . As a result , the building&amp;rsquo ; s suites range from relatively modest studios at 418 square feet to three-bedroom penthouses of 2,109 square feet and large private rooftop patios . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mukhan Rai , president of Raichu Development Group , said he is pleased with the final result . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We worked closely with GBL to create Winona&amp;rsquo ; s striking exterior fa&amp;ccedil ; ade , and I am excited that this building will be unlike anything on the Cambie Corridor , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; The stunning geometric architecture of the building is engineered with a unique angled fa&amp;ccedil ; ade that maximizes opportunities for view exposure , privacy and natural light . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rai said many of the homes are indeed spacious . &amp;ldquo ; Someone who is looking to downsize from a single-family home to single-level living will not have to compromise on space or quality of finishes . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He noted all homes include a Sub-Zero and Wolf appliance package in the kitchens , polished Cambria quartz countertops with a waterfall edge and a unique built-in wood-frame detailing over the bathroom vanity . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Our one- and two-bedroom homes are very spacious as well . From 754 to 874 square feet , these homes are designed for those seeking a lifestyle balance with their home being nearby to urban conveniences such as transit and shops , as well as recreation and parks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Each home offers outdoor living space on a private balcony that includes electricity , gas and water connections . Portico Design Group chose wide-plank engineered hardwood floors through all the living spaces and bedrooms . Windows come with roller shades and four-inch recessed lighting in nine-foot ceilings . All homes have a Blomberg front-load washer and dryer . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Appliance packages vary with the suite sizes , with 30-inch integrated refrigerators with two freezer drawers in the two- and three-bedroom homes , while one-bedroom and studio models have 24-inch wide versions . The same applies with 30-inch and 24-inch Wolf built-in convection wall ovens . All homes come with Panasonic microwaves and Asko dishwashers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the bathrooms , 24-by-24-inch tile extends from the floor to walls , and a tiled shower with glass enclosure has a niche and linear shower drain . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In main bathrooms , the bathtub has a tile front face that matches the floor . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rooftop patios with views of surrounding parks on Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s west side feature an outdoor kitchen with a 36-inch Napolean built-in stainless steel barbecue and a 28-inch flush-mount cabinet with double stainless steel doors for a propane tank and 17-inch Napolean drawers . Topping it off is a 24-inch Marvel undercounter outdoor stainless steel refrigerator . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All homes are covered by a 2-5-10 New Home Warranty and are LEED Gold certified on completion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project : &lt;/strong&gt; Winona on Cambie &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project location : &lt;/strong&gt; 7638 Cambie St. , Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project Scope : &lt;/strong&gt; 38&amp;nbsp ; studio , one- , two- and three-bedroom homes , ranging from 418 to 2,109 square feet in a six-storey concrete building . Close to Winona Park , and Oakridge shopping centre , Langara Golf Course and the Canada Line &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; From $900,000s &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Developer : &lt;/strong&gt; Raichu Development Group &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Architect : &lt;/strong&gt; GBL Architects &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Interior Designer : &lt;/strong&gt; Portico Design Group &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **26;38273;TOOLONG Centre : &lt;/strong&gt; 5710 Cambie St. ( Cambie and West 41 **25;38301;TOOLONG Ave. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Centre 's Hours : &lt;/strong&gt; TBA &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales Phone : &lt;/strong&gt; 604-364-2688 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Email : &lt;/strong&gt; info@winonaoncambie.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Website : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;u&gt; **28;38328;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Completion : &lt;/strong&gt; Early 2021 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Michael BernardSat , 29 Sep 2018 13:01:18 **45;38358;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondosLedingham McAllister Properties ' Cedar Creek development in South Burnaby appeals to wide range of buyershttp : **34;38405;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;38441;TOOLONG ... title= " Cedar Creek is a project from Ledingham McAllister in Burnaby . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Cedar Creek is a project from Ledingham McAllister in Burnaby . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38477;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 861554 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38507;TOOLONG ... title= " Cedar Creek is a project from Ledingham McAllister in Burnaby PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Cedar Creek is a project from Ledingham McAllister in Burnaby PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38543;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 867118 " /&gt; Cedar Creek , Ledingham McAllister Properties&amp;rsquo ; new residential development in South Burnaby , is part of the revitalization of the city&amp;rsquo ; s Edmonds neighbourhood and will be located adjacent to the planned nearly 60-acre Southgate City community . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Construction of the first building in the three-phase Cedar Creek development at 7133 14th Avenue , Burnaby , is scheduled to start in early 2019 . The 128-unit , six-storey wood-frame building comprises a range of homes from one-bedroom and den through to three-bedroom suites . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This neighbourhood has a loyal and established long-term community and is attractive to first time buyers , people who are moving up to larger homes or those who are looking to stay in the community and downsize &amp;ndash ; it appeals to a very wide range of people , &amp;rdquo ; says Manuela Mirecki , Ledingham McAllister&amp;rsquo ; s senior vice president of marketing and design . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mirecki says there is a lot of interest in the development from people who have lived in the community for a long time and those who prefer the scale of the six-storey buildings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The architecture is very West Coast with big overhangs and wood grain soffits . There is lots of articulation in the building , so it looks more like a collection of smaller buildings &amp;mdash ; it modulates in depth and height , creating a more vibrant and organic flow , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the sales centre at 7166 14th Avenue , Burnaby , potential buyers can choose from two colour palettes , Stone and Sand . The Stone scheme can be seen in the one-bedroom and den example while the choices in the Sand palette are on view in the two-bedroom unit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Whenever we can have two display suites we always aim to show two vastly different interpretations in the d&amp;eacute ; cor , &amp;rdquo ; says Mirecki , noting that the two-bedroom suite has a sophisticated and urbane sensibility with pops of colour and a glamourous ambience , while the one-bedroom unit is aimed at a younger buyer . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The one-bedroom d&amp;eacute ; cor was inspired by a sweater-wrapped chair I saw at a fashion show in New York , &amp;rdquo ; she adds , explaining that her inspiration , in the form of two sweater-wrapped chairs , is part of the show suite&amp;rsquo ; s d&amp;eacute ; cor. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the kitchens , different colours of the same cabinet style are used in both schemes . Mirecki is upbeat about the cabinetry. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We spend a lot of time meeting with our suppliers , and on a factory tour I saw a small sample of the cabinetry we are using for Cedar Creek . I immediately liked it , it&amp;rsquo ; s almost a twist on shaker style . It has the cross-grain at the top and bottom and it can be interpreted as contemporary or traditional , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The kitchen cabinets include soft-close mechanisms while matte black pulls on lower cabinets add a contrasting detail to the doors and drawers . The light countertops are complemented by the full-height backsplash of stacked white 12- by 4-inch tiles &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The major kitchen appliances , all by KitchenAid , include a five-element ceramic-glass cooktop and oven ; refrigerator with freezer and dishwasher . The modern hood fan is by Venmar and the microwave is by Panasonic . The in-suite laundry includes a front-loading washer and dryer by Whirlpool . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The developer has used Kohler products for all its faucets , sinks and plumbing fixtures in Cedar Creek says Mirecki , referencing Ledingham McAllister&amp;rsquo ; s long-term relationship with the brand . The deep-bowl stainless steel kitchen sink and Purist faucet with pull-down spray nozzle is a nod to contemporary style in this functional area . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the ensuite bathrooms , marble-like 12- by 24-inch porcelain wall tiles and limestone-look floor tiles give the spaces a relaxing ambience . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In smaller spaces the less aggressive the pattern on the large surfaces the more open and bright the space will feel , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38573;TOOLONG ... title= " Cedar Creek is a project from Ledingham McAllister in Burnaby . PNG Merlin Archive " alt= " Cedar Creek is a project from Ledingham McAllister in Burnaby . PNG Merlin Archive " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38609;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 867117 " /&gt; In the main bathrooms , with their Kohler Hytec soaker tubs , the large format white wall tiles , enhance the fresh and clean design esthetic. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Laminate flooring connects the entry , kitchen , dining- and living-rooms while bedrooms are carpeted . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We have found people like carpet in bedrooms , they want to step out of bed onto something soft , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the one-bedroom show suite , the den is shown as an office , but Mirecki says in other developments she has seen this space repurposed for a variety of uses from a sewing or craft room to accommodate a day bed for a comfortable reading nook . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All units will have one parking stall and a bike storage locker in the underground parkade . Charging stations for electric vehicles are also included ( a total of five in the three phases ) . The developer is also working with the car-sharing organization Modo Car Cooperative , and will provide on-site parking and access to three Modo cars . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Shared amenities for the whole Cedar Creek development include play areas for children ( one of these play areas will be completed to coincide with Phase 1 ) , a fitness room , guest suite , meeting room and community garden plots . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Burnaby&amp;rsquo ; s official flower , the rhododendron , is prominent in the landscaping plan that includes planting more than 170 of the flowering shrubs . More than 225 trees will also be planted including nearly 50 dogwoods ( B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s floral emblem ) and 26 maples in addition to many other shrubs and bushes including California Lilacs and evergreen huckleberries. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project : &lt;/strong&gt; Cedar Creek &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project address : &lt;/strong&gt; 7133 14th Avenue &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project City : &lt;/strong&gt; Burnaby &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Developer : &lt;/strong&gt; Ledingham McAllister &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Architect : &lt;/strong&gt; Integra &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Interior designer : &lt;/strong&gt; The Mill &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Project size : &lt;/strong&gt; 3 , 6-storey buildings ( first phase : 128 units ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Bedrooms : &lt;/strong&gt; one-bedroom and den ; two-bedroom ; two-bedroom and flex space ; three-bedroom &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Unit size : &lt;/strong&gt; 639 to 995 square feet &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Price : &lt;/strong&gt; From low $500,000s &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **26;38639;TOOLONG : &lt;/strong&gt; Starts early 2019 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales centre : &lt;/strong&gt; 7166 14th Avenue , Burnaby , BC &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sales centre hours : &lt;/strong&gt; Saturday &amp;ndash ; Thursdays from 12 noon to 5 p.m. ( closed Fridays ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Phone : &lt;/strong&gt; 604-529-8868 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Website : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //liveatcedarcreek.com " &gt;liveatc ... &lt;/p&gt; Kathleen FreimondSat , 22 Sep 2018 13:10:29 **45;38667;TOOLONG ... &amp;amp ; SellingCondos
